\subsection{1. Plan, audit and decisions that open stage 2 (2026-09-05)}\label{1-plan-audit-and-decisions-that-open-stage-2-2026-09-05}

State of the tree at the start: HEAD \texttt{35d9dd5} (clean apart from \texttt{docs/}),
binary rebuilt from it with gfortran 16.2 (md5 \texttt{16cde610347c10c0a48db5c2a7fa6cbb}).
All twelve regression goldens differ from the case outputs (they are the
pre-section-170 snapshots); by decision they are not refreshed until the end
of Phase 1 (below).

Documents written today, all under \texttt{docs/}:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{physics\_numerics\_audit\_20260905.md} (external audit of \texttt{35d9dd5}), its
  diagnostics in \texttt{audit\_20260905/}.
\item
  \texttt{development\_plan\_20260905.md}, \texttt{\_rev1.md}, \texttt{\_rev2.md}, \texttt{\_rev3.md} and the
  three reviews \texttt{\_review.md}, \texttt{\_review2.md}, \texttt{\_review3.md}: the plan and its
  revisions after each review. Rev 3 is the plan of record; its section 10
  holds the independent five-group source audit (three new P0, twelve new P1,
  about fifty P2), with the reports and the verification probe in
  \texttt{audit\_20260905/source\_audit/}.
\item
  \texttt{development\_plan\_20260905\_execution.md}: Steps 0-7, who acts, gates.
\end{itemize}

Decisions taken by the user today (recorded in rev 3 sections 8 and 10.5
and in the execution document, Step 0):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Goldens are refreshed once, at the end of Phase 1; until then a scratch
  baseline (\texttt{backup/regression/baseline\_post170\_\textless{}date\textgreater{}/}, read through a
  \texttt{REGRESSION\_GOLDEN\_DIR} variable to be added to \texttt{run\_check.sh}) is the
  control for byte-identity. \texttt{golden/} is not touched before that.
\item
  ROE interface: \texttt{speed\_estimate\_ROE} returns a status and, in the vacuum
  case, the two gas-edge speeds; \texttt{Num\_flux} gains an HLLE vacuum branch.
  Signature shown before coding.
\item
  One spectrum type builds every band. Power-law input: power law on the
  whole grid, XUV and below 13.6 eV alike (the present construction is the
  stated meaning of the key). A new \texttt{Spectrum\ type:\ Planck} gives the
  Planck photosphere from \texttt{Stellar\ Teff} and \texttt{Stellar\ radius} on the whole
  grid. \texttt{Load}: the table on the whole grid. The Balmer continuum follows
  the run\textquotesingle s type. The setup report states the sub-13.6 eV source and the
  integrated grid flux.
\item
  \texttt{m\_He/m\_H\ =\ 3.9715} in the species table; \texttt{RJ\ =\ 7.1492e9} cm (IAU
  nominal equatorial) in the code and the Wind-AE bridge alike (R0 of every
  run +2.26\%); MJ and MSUN to the IAU nominal values.
\item
  The \texttt{du} stop uses the signed spread of rho v r\^{}2 over the mean, as
  \texttt{flux\_spread} does.
\item
  Every planet folder, benchmark and planet example runs on the SED its
  reference literature used or assumed, one file per planet under
  \texttt{inputdata/sed/} with provenance; the input switch is a configuration
  change, the reruns stay instruction-gated.
\item
  A loaded SED that ends above the band an active absorber needs (He 2\^{}3S
  at 2583 A, a low-IP metal at its threshold) stops the run; the message
  offers the two remedies (turn the absorber off in the input, or supply a
  covering SED). No continue key.
\end{enumerate}

Open: decision 4 (A3 retry design: carrier substep retry or outer step
controller); 2, 5, 6 inside D0; 7 before B2; 10 in Phase 5.

renamed to \texttt{Update\_EXHALE\_stage1.*}, \texttt{Update\_EXHALE\_early\_phase.*} to

\texttt{e779298d0b482bfbc40ae2f9aa80d60a} because four of the rewritten references
are inside printed message strings (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} 2602,

statement changed. The pointers in \texttt{README.md}, \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} and the

Scratch baseline created: \texttt{backup/regression/baseline\_post170\_20260905/}, the

\subsection{2. Phase 0: the tests exist and are red on the known defects (2026-09-05)}\label{2-phase-0-the-tests-exist-and-are-red-on-the-known-defects-2026-09-05}

Plan reference: \texttt{development\_plan\_20260905\_execution.md} Step 1. Four
workers, disjoint files; every result below was re-run by the advisor after
the worker\textquotesingle s report (times are the advisor\textquotesingle s runs).

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{2.1 \texttt{src/tests/physics\_probe/} (W1): 41 assertions, 15 PASS, 26 FAIL, 3.9 s}{2.1 src/tests/physics\_probe/ (W1): 41 assertions, 15 PASS, 26 FAIL, 3.9 s}}\label{21-srctestsphysics_probe-w1-41-assertions-15-pass-26-fail-39-s}

Derived from the frozen diagnostics in \texttt{docs/audit\_20260905/} (which stay);
links the production modules through a computed \texttt{use} closure
(\texttt{source\_closure.py}), so no module can be left out of the link by hand.
Drivers named for the physics: \texttt{riemann\_wave\_speeds.f90} (LLF signal speed
1 against the required 3, mass flux -3.5 against -4.5, mirror symmetry; ROE
normalization invariance broken by 0.661 and 3.162 in the two branches; NaN
star speeds on the equal-pressure expansion; the production ROE flux leaves
thermal energy -2.93 after one Euler update on the vacuum-producing
expansion), \texttt{h2\_channel\_detector\_ratio.f90} ((S+D)/M 0.2545 against the
measured 0.2827, D/(S+D) 1/9 against 0.20; channel sum and stoichiometry
GREEN), \texttt{h3p\_cooling\_limits.f90} (cooling independent of n(H2) at 0, 1,
100 cm\^{}-3; joins +0.430, -0.00113, -0.0235 at 300, 800, 1800 K; factor
jump 1.5e-3 at 1e14), \texttt{h2\_rovibrational\_identity.f90} (chemistry
T\^{}2 dlnZ/dT against the EOS u\_rv: -0.63\% to -8.29\% over 300-8000 K),
\texttt{photoevent\_energy\_ledger.f90} (H event at 20 eV closes exactly through the
production \texttt{photoelectron\_share}; the H2 double event at 80 eV accounts for
60.28 of 80 eV, 19.72 eV unassigned), \texttt{radiation\_exchange\_ledger.py} (two-cell
ownership rule, GREEN by construction).

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{2.2 \texttt{src/tests/grid\_and\_gates/} (W2): 6 programs, all RED, 13.4 s}{2.2 src/tests/grid\_and\_gates/ (W2): 6 programs, all RED, 13.4 s}}\label{22-srctestsgrid_and_gates-w2-6-programs-all-red-134-s}

\texttt{grid\_width\_identity.f90} (production \texttt{define\_grid}): \texttt{dr\_j(j)} against
\texttt{r\_edg(j)\ -\ r\_edg(j-1)} off by 1.0145 on the Mixed grid at r\_max = 10, 1.0100
at r\_max = 2, 1.0046 on Stretched, exact on Uniform (where every cell has the
same width, which is why the defect was invisible).
\texttt{photon\_grid\_quadrature.f90} (production \texttt{set\_energy\_vectors}, \texttt{J\_inc},
cross sections; \texttt{wasp\_full} spectrum): code/exact P\_HI 1.0951 (floor 4.8
eV), 1.0890 (5.139 eV), 1.0003 (13.6 eV); P\_HeI 1.0157 and P\_HeII 1.0299 in
every configuration; heating 0.99984. New from this test: P\_HeI carries the
same threshold-bin defect (24.6 eV is a grid point whatever the floor), and
the X-ray block ends at 1184.19 eV, not 1240 (share of P\_HI 1.9e-10).
\texttt{mass\_flux\_spread\_functional.py}: the coded \texttt{du} and the signed
\texttt{flux\_spread} on the baseline states (lower\_profile 823.4 vs 3.61 with two
sign changes in the window; mol\_base\_handoff 1.29 vs 0.696 with none;
wasp\_full 9.85e-4 vs 9.85e-4), and \texttt{du} = 0 on a synthetic +F/-F window.
\texttt{output\_state\_consistency.sh} (200-step \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}): header
\texttt{sec\_ion=T}, heat\_total/heat median 0.2752, largest difference 0.822.
\texttt{base\_level\_single\_statement.sh} (\texttt{lower\_profile}, 2 steps): setup 2.0035e-5
bar against the profile\textquotesingle s 1.00e-6 bar, factor 20.035.
\texttt{restart\_grid\_guard.sh} (\texttt{roundtrip} on Mixed, restarted on Uniform with the
same N): exit 0, the restart\textquotesingle s \texttt{r} equals the file\textquotesingle s Mixed radii exactly and
sits 0.60 relative from the run\textquotesingle s own grid.

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{2.3 \texttt{inputdata/sed/} (W4): the SED inventory of decision 16}{2.3 inputdata/sed/ (W4): the SED inventory of decision 16}}\label{23-inputdatased-w4-the-sed-inventory-of-decision-16}

Four files built from data already in the workspace, each with a
provenance header, a build script under \texttt{inputdata/sed/build/}, and the
coverage test \texttt{sed\_coverage\_and\_order\_check.py} (31 assertions, all PASS):
WASP-52 b (eps Eri, MUSCLES v22, reproduces the existing planet-folder file
to round-off), hot Uranus (the benchmark\textquotesingle s Ribas band table continued to
2995 A from the Gueymard composite it came from), HD 209458 b (WHI 2008
solar minimum at 0.04707 au, the p-winds file reproduced exactly), LHS 1140 b
(GJ 1132 proxy). Not built, data missing: WASP-121 b (Huang 2023\textquotesingle s LLmodels
F5V spectrum above 1700 A) and HD 189733 b (Salz 2016\textquotesingle s composite; its solar
table is not here). \texttt{inputdata/scaled\_solar\_hd189.sed} judged an orphan
(no provenance, hardness inconsistent with its own normalization, ends at
1899.5 A). Verbatim paper quotes and the open items are in
\texttt{inputdata/sed/README.md}; rev 3 decision 16 records the status. Also found:
\texttt{WASP-52b/input.inp} points at a directory that no longer exists (\texttt{EXHALE/}),
the top-level Koskinen benchmark runs at 0.048 au against the paper\textquotesingle s 0.05,
\texttt{HD209458b/input.inp} at 0.048 au against the literature 0.047.

\subsubsection{2.4 Regression harness and new cases (W3)}\label{24-regression-harness-and-new-cases-w3}

\texttt{run\_check.sh}: \texttt{REGRESSION\_GOLDEN\_DIR} (default \texttt{golden/}; \texttt{golden} mode
still writes only to \texttt{golden/}); the rebuild is fatal with its log kept in
\texttt{backup/regression/.build.log}; the summary counts only the cases of the run;
the binary copy is status-tested; \texttt{FILES} gains the two \texttt{\_adv.txt} profiles
(the post-processing pass was outside the regression); a file without a
reference is \texttt{MISS}, not \texttt{FAIL}, and a run that compared nothing prints
\texttt{NO\ VERDICT}; a restart case keeps its IC pair in \texttt{\textless{}case\textgreater{}/IC/} and the script
restores it after clearing \texttt{output/}; \texttt{phase0-cases} prints the new case
list. \texttt{Makefile}: \texttt{make\ check\ CASES=...}; \texttt{make\ test} runs
\texttt{element\_census\_tests}, \texttt{diffusion\_tests}, \texttt{residual\_determinism} and every
executable \texttt{src/tests/*/run.sh} found at run time, every suite even after a
failure, nonzero exit if any failed.

New cases, not in \texttt{DEFAULT\_CASES} (\texttt{PHASE0\_CASES}): \texttt{hydrostatic\_column}
(hot-Uranus gravity, irradiation 18 orders below normal, no key exists to
switch cooling off so the gas is held where it is negligible; 300 steps,
isothermal to 2\%, but draining through the outflow boundary at 0.8 Mach,
so it is a configuration for the Phase 4 D2 measurement, not a hydrostatic
state), \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} (\texttt{examples/18\_oxygen\_chemistry} verbatim, 1000
steps, 3 min 52 s single-threaded under load), \texttt{hp\_front}, \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed},
\texttt{hp\_trace\_seed} (restarts of the \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} state with \texttt{Ionization\ transport:\ True}; the H II column set to 0 and to 1e-12 of the H nuclei by
moving the protons into H I, since \texttt{load\_IC} refuses a changed He/H; the
three 100-step states are mutually distinct, so the seed reaches the
solver). No dump hook asserts the seed at the entry of \texttt{solve\_carriers};
the README names the two lines where one belongs.

Measured (advisor re-run of two cases: 6.2 s, byte-identical on all four
files): \texttt{check\ wasp\_full\ mol\_base\_handoff} against the scratch baseline is
byte-identical on all four files per case (W3, 13 min); the five new cases
reproduce their own baseline copies; \texttt{make\ check\ CASES=mol\_base\_handoff}
against the stale \texttt{golden/} reports the known 8.3e-4 and 3.6e-4 movements
and \texttt{MISS} on the two \texttt{\_adv} files; \texttt{make\ test} (31 s) runs five suites:
\texttt{diffusion\_tests} and \texttt{residual\_determinism} PASS, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} and
\texttt{physics\_probe} RED on the known defects as intended, and
\textbf{\texttt{element\_census\_tests} does not compile at HEAD} (pre-existing:
\texttt{carrier\_state} gained \texttt{nrho} and \texttt{wfac} in section 158 and the test\textquotesingle s two
calls still pass ten arguments; \texttt{carrier\_write\_back} likewise takes \texttt{nrho}
where the test passes \texttt{ntot}). Fixed in section 3 below.

Also found by W3: with the flux made negligible, the He recombination
coupling warning (\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90} 947, 952) fires in every
cell with a ratio of 1e80 because both sides sit at their guards; diagnostic
only.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{3. \texttt{element\_census\_tests} compiles again (2026-09-05)}{3. element\_census\_tests compiles again (2026-09-05)}}\label{3-element_census_tests-compiles-again-2026-09-05}

\texttt{src/tests/element\_census\_tests.f90}: the two \texttt{carrier\_state} calls pass the
\texttt{nrho} and \texttt{wfac} outputs the routine has had since stage 1 section 158, and
the two \texttt{carrier\_write\_back} calls pass \texttt{nrho} (the density the carrier
fraction is a fraction of) in place of \texttt{ntot}. Test only; no production
source. Result: builds, 20 assertions PASS (E1 stoichiometry and charge, E2
elemental closure through the write-back including the CO collapse, E3 the
H2 photoevent nuclei ledger, E4 the base H2 fraction). Until now the suite
had been unbuildable, so its assertions had not been checked since section
158.

\subsection{4. Phase 1 batch 2a: bounded corrections with no golden movement (2026-09-05)}\label{4-phase-1-batch-2a-bounded-corrections-with-no-golden-movement-2026-09-05}

Seven items, seven workers on disjoint files in one tree (private
\texttt{OBJDIR}/\texttt{EXE} builds, no shared regression runs); one rebuild and one gate
by the advisor afterwards. Plan references: rev 3 sections 4.1 (A2), 10.2
items 7, 8, 10, 12, 10.3, 10.5 decisions 3, 13, 17.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{A2, ROE estimator and caller} (\texttt{speed\_estimate\_ROE.f90} rewritten;
  \texttt{Num\_Fluxes.f90} ROE branch; \texttt{input\_read.f90}; \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}):
  \texttt{docs/a2\_roe\_interface.md} implemented as approved: \texttt{roe\_star\_state},
  status OK/VACUUM/INADMISSIBLE, the vacuum test first, PVRS kept only when
  admissible, two-rarefaction exponent \texttt{1/z}, two-shock densities times the
  upstream densities, HLLE flux on faces without an admissible star state
  with a printed counter, refusal of \texttt{Numerical\ flux:\ ROE} with molecular
  chemistry and the ladder EOS. Four deviations accepted: a positivity guard
  on the input states at the top of the estimator; the refusal placed in the
  consistency block after the optional keys (where \texttt{thereis\_mol} is known);
  the old \texttt{p\_min} floor removed (the guard supersedes it); the counter printed
  for ROE runs only. Test: 30 ROE assertions GREEN (scale invariance,
  equal-pressure expansion \texttt{c\_*} 0.9576611, stationary contact, asymmetric
  shock and rarefaction against exact Riemann solutions at 5\%, vacuum edges
  -6.127017/+6.127017, positive thermal energy after one Euler update).
\item
  \textbf{2a-SED, decision 17} (\texttt{sed\_read.f90}): a table that stops above the
  band an active absorber needs stops the run with the file, the missing band
  in eV and A, the absorbers (He 2\^{}3S; each active neutral metal below the
  file minimum, K I at 4.341 eV included) and the two remedies; no continue
  key. Test \texttt{sed\_coverage\_stop.sh} (six assertions) GREEN; no configuration
  of record trips it (the Koskinen runs have the metastable off).
\item
  \textbf{2a-OPAC} (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} \texttt{PH\_heat\_H}/\texttt{PH\_heat\_HHe},
  \texttt{opacity\_models.f90}): model P\textquotesingle s factor multiplies the shared flux weight
  \texttt{int\_f} of every absorption integrand (rates, heating, secondary
  ionization, \texttt{acc\_q}), so the beam\textquotesingle s loss equals the local absorption;
  test \texttt{opacity\_model\_p\_ledger.f90}: a cell with f = 10 closes energy and
  H I ionizations to 3e-8 (was a factor 10 off).
\item
  \textbf{2a-LYA} (\texttt{lya\_rt.f90}, \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} \texttt{fuv\_lw\_photon\_field},
  \texttt{water\_photolysis.f90} header, plus the two call sites in
  \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90} and \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90} that the
  new \texttt{nHI} argument required): band B2 is attenuated by
  \texttt{lya\_stellar\_beam\_transmission(T,\ tau)} = \texttt{erfc(x1/(sqrt(2)\ X\_s))}, the
  expression \texttt{jlya\_escape\_prob} carried inline and now calls (bitwise equal
  at 4941 test points). Test \texttt{lya\_band\_transmission.f90} (ten assertions)
  GREEN. Movement, \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} 1000-step snapshot: du 903 -\textgreater{} 596,
  log10 Mdot 9.62 -\textgreater{} 9.61, B2 transmission 1e-291 at the base, 1e-3 at
  1.07 R\_p, 0.98 at 1.5 R\_p; H2O moves up to 6.2x and OH 7.3x in the
  layer. Scratch baseline of that case re-snapshotted (previous copy kept).
\item
  \textbf{2a-WAE} (\texttt{wae\_exhale\_input.f90}): mass fractions from the same
  \texttt{m\_He/m\_H} the solver is handed; test: reconstructed He/H equals the input
  to 1e-12 (was 1.0071696x). RJ/MJ/MSUN untouched (decision 14 is 2c).
\item
  \textbf{2a-HPFIX} (\texttt{constrained\_chemical\_equilibrium.f90}): \texttt{hp\_is\_fixed}
  pins \texttt{species\_fixed(is\_HII)} like H2 and the oxygen carriers; new suite
  \texttt{src/tests/constrained\_network\_layout/} (eight assertions) GREEN. The three
  \texttt{hp\_*} cases never enter the continuation (every cell is a class-1 root),
  so no run exercises the fix yet.
\item
  \textbf{2a-PLANCK} (\texttt{J\_inc.f90}, \texttt{set\_energy\_vectors.f90}, \texttt{input\_read.f90},
  \texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90}, \texttt{excited\_hydrogen.f90}, \texttt{docs/input\_schema.md}):
  \texttt{Spectrum\ type:\ Planck} (requires \texttt{Stellar\ Teff} and \texttt{Stellar\ radius})
  builds the whole grid from \texttt{pi\ B\_nu(T\_eff)\ (R\_star/a)\^{}2}; the Balmer
  routines call the same shared function (unchanged result); the setup
  report states the spectrum type, the source of the band below 13.6 eV and
  the integrated grid flux against the nominal J\_XUV. New suite
  \texttt{src/tests/spectrum\_type/} (12 assertions) GREEN. Measured on the
  \texttt{wasp\_full} star: Planck/power-law 1642 at 4.8 eV, 358 at 6.2 eV, 2.9 at
  10 eV, 0.014 at 13.6 eV; He 2\^{}3S rate 45.2 s\^{}-1 (Planck) vs 0.100
  (power law); integrated grid flux / nominal 1.42 (power law, floor 4.8 eV),
  0.995 (floor 13.6), 112.8 (Planck).
\item
  \textbf{2a-GUARDS} (\texttt{define\_grid.f90}, \texttt{set\_IC.f90}, \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90},
  \texttt{viscous\_conduction.f90}, \texttt{steady\_newton.f90}, \texttt{input\_read.f90},
  \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}, \texttt{water\_photolysis.f90}, \texttt{oxygen\_rates.f90},
  \texttt{lyman\_werner.f90}, \texttt{parameters.f90}, \texttt{molecular\_infrared\_data.f90}):
  \texttt{cell\_nearest\_radius} (the bare \texttt{minloc} read \texttt{r(503)} = 0 out of bounds
  on the default grid), \texttt{j\_min\ \textgreater{}=\ 1}, dead \texttt{report\_unmet\_steady\_gates}
  removed, Mdot guarded for small N and non-outflow, conduction floor
  counted and reported, \texttt{okres} read in the ray test, correct sweep count,
  \texttt{get\_word} initialized, dead \texttt{close(unit=1)} removed, \texttt{adv\_corr} zeroed on
  allocation, dead \texttt{Tcode} argument removed from four carrier routines and
  their callers, \texttt{carrier\_mass\_amu} through the named species indices,
  \texttt{wp\_ready} used as the guard it names, dead constants and stale headers
  fixed, \texttt{molecular\_infrared\_data}\textquotesingle s \texttt{i} private. Test \texttt{grid\_window\_indices}
  (seven assertions) GREEN.
\end{itemize}

Gate, advisor\textquotesingle s single rebuild (md5 \texttt{b52c79ebb15ee9014858425bfea4cb1c}):

0.84); \texttt{inputdata/sed/README.md} describes the old warning in six places;
\texttt{docs/input\_schema.md} row 11a should state the coverage requirement;
\texttt{docs/a2\_oxygen\_option\_design.md} 402 describes the pre-2a B2 field; the
\texttt{physics\_probe/README.md} summary counts are stale; \texttt{J\_XUV} (used by the
Wind-AE bridge), the FUV band fluxes and the monochromatic flux do not yet
follow the spectrum type (decision 13 is enforced for the photon grid only);
\texttt{backup/regression/parse\_golden/} was already stale at 35d9dd5; the three
audit probe scripts under \texttt{docs/audit\_20260905/} no longer compile against
the tree and are marked as frozen records.

\subsection{5. Phase 1 batch 2b: bounded corrections that move some outputs (2026-09-05)}\label{5-phase-1-batch-2b-bounded-corrections-that-move-some-outputs-2026-09-05}

Six workers (five code items and one documentation pass), disjoint files,
private builds; one rebuild (md5 \texttt{d2173f0210abcdc84f41cb6fb5076e18}) and one
gate by the advisor. Plan references: rev 3 sections 4.1 (A1, A3 increment
(a), A8) and 10.3 (\texttt{k\_lw} cell mean, He I singlet, He II recombination
cooling).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{A1, LLF signal speed} (\texttt{Num\_Fluxes.f90}, \texttt{RK\_rhs.f90}, \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}):
  \texttt{a1\ =\ max(abs(vL)+aL,\ abs(vR)+aR)}, the spectral radius of the Euler flux
  Jacobian, replacing \texttt{max(abs(vL+aL),\ abs(vR+aR))} (1 instead of 3 on the
  inflow test pair); the header\textquotesingle s positivity claim now describes the
  coefficient the code carries. Reporting: calls of the positivity repair,
  repaired interfaces, repaired interfaces in accepted steps, and LLF faces,
  all printed even when zero. Test: the three LLF assertions GREEN. Movement:
  none (no case selects LLF and the repair path is never entered in the
  matrix: \texttt{flux\ correction:\ 0\ positivity\ repair\ call(s)} in every run).
\item
  \textbf{A3 increment (a), carrier reference scales and status}
  (\texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}, \texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90},
  \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}): one \texttt{carrier\_element\_reference\_density(ic,\ nH,\ nO,\ nC)}
  (H2 and H+ hydrogen, OH and H2O oxygen, CO min(O, C)) read by both the
  Jacobian step (\texttt{carrier\_source\_derivative\_step}) and the residual floor;
  the reported residual is that of the state after \texttt{limit\_to\_element\_budget};
  \texttt{solve\_carriers} returns converged / iteration cap / line search failed and
  the caller stops on a failed status in a carrier the limiter did not move
  (rejection and retry remain increment (b), decision 4 open); a cumulative
  CO-ceiling record (cells, applications, molecules removed) in the run
  summary and \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out}; \texttt{carrier\_mass\_amu} stops on a species
  missing from the table. New suite \texttt{src/tests/carrier\_reference\_scales/}
  (14 assertions; before the change the proton row\textquotesingle s diagonal derivative was
  exactly zero at H+ = 0 and 1e-12 n\_H because the step had collapsed to
  1e-300). Movement of this item alone: \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed} up to 7.8e-4 at the
  ionization front, \texttt{hp\_trace\_seed} 2.8e-5, \texttt{hp\_front} round-off,
  \texttt{mol\_carrier} and \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} byte-identical. Cost of the extra
  residual assembly +1.5\% wall time on \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed}.
\item
  \textbf{A8, Chung detector inversion} (\texttt{h2\_photo\_channels.f90}): Table II is
  the detector event ratio \texttt{(N\_S+N\_D)/N\_M} (Chung et al. 1993 p. 886, one
  pulse per event, quoted at the code site), \texttt{q\_D\ =\ N\_D/(N\_S+N\_D)} = 0.20 at
  80 eV; \texttt{sigma\_M\ =\ (1-f\_di)\ sigma\_i}, \texttt{sigma\_S\ =\ (1-q\_D)\ f\_di\ sigma\_i},
  \texttt{sigma\_D\ =\ q\_D\ f\_di\ sigma\_i}; \texttt{yan\_rho} converted with \texttt{q\_D\ =\ rho\_D/(1+rho\_D)/f\_di}; an inadmissible \texttt{q\_D} stops the run instead of being
  clipped; \texttt{frac\_H2\_double\_of\_protons} renamed
  \texttt{frac\_H2\_double\_of\_proton\_events}. At 80 eV: D 0.02254 -\textgreater{} 0.04408, M
  0.7972 -\textgreater{} 0.7796, S 0.1803 -\textgreater{} 0.1763. Test: 18 assertions GREEN including
  the stored-channel and yield integration checks; E1 test 4 prints the same
  6.951e-3. Movement of this item alone: \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} n\_e up to 1.8e-2
  at r = 1.018, x(H+) 1.9e-2 at the base, T 4.6e-4; \texttt{mol\_sec\_ion} at the
  1e-3 to 5e-3 level, with a 0.078 cm/s velocity shift moving the v = 0
  crossing by one cell at r = 1.024-1.026 and flipping the advection-
  correction branch of the \texttt{\_adv} files there. Mdot unchanged.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{k\_lw} cell mean} (\texttt{lyman\_werner.f90},
  \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} \texttt{fuv\_lw\_photon\_field}): new
  \texttt{lyman\_werner\_dissociation\_rate\_cell\_mean(F,\ N\_out,\ N\_in,\ T,\ n\_H,\ tau\_out,\ tau\_in)}, a composite three-point Gauss rule on segments of at most 0.05
  dex of column and 0.5 of continuum depth (the level-resolved cross section
  is a piecewise power law with slope up to 3.3, so the water bands\textquotesingle{}
  exponential mean does not apply; measured accuracy 2.5e-4 worst over 1500
  random cells); the star-ward face carries \texttt{NH2col(j+1)}. Test: seven
  assertions GREEN (the old inner-face rate was 3.18x too small on a cell
  spanning 1.12 dex). Movement: \texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner} k\_LW +1.4\% to +28.1\%
  (median +2.7\%), n(H2) -20\% and n(H3+) -20\% at the front r = 1.071, T up to
  8e-3, n\_e 1.7e-2; \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} and \texttt{wasp\_full} identical (no LW
  flux). Cost under 0.5\%.
\item
  \textbf{He I singlet and He II recombination cooling} (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}
  \texttt{eval\_cool}/\texttt{eval\_cool\_cells}, \texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90},
  \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90} comment): the 24.6 eV collisional ionization
  and the Cen (1992) excitation of He I are charged to the ground singlet
  \texttt{he\_ground\_singlet\_density(nhei,\ nheiTR)} (the metastable had paid both
  levels); \texttt{lambda\_rec\_HeII\ =\ kT\ alpha\_rec\_HeII\_total(T)} with
  \texttt{alpha\_rec\_HeII\_total\ =\ alpha\_11S\ +\ alpha\_23S} when the metastable is
  tracked (the coefficient the balance removes He+ at) and \texttt{alpha\_B}
  otherwise; the coefficient is built inside \texttt{eval\_cool} from T and the run\textquotesingle s
  flags so that \texttt{energy\_semi\_implicit}\textquotesingle s finite-difference \texttt{dC/dT} sees one
  function (a coefficient-passing form would have left an offset the
  derivative multiplies by 1e5). At 1e4 K the He II coefficient rises
  2.807e-13 -\textgreater{} 3.640e-13 (+30\%); the He recombination coupling\textquotesingle s own weight
  (\texttt{y\_net\ alpha\_1}) is still not charged (about 10\% of this channel, stated at
  the code site). Test: 15 assertions GREEN. Movement of this item alone:
  \texttt{wasp\_full} median 2e-5 / max 1.7e-4 in rho, T max 5.9e-4, Ca I and Na I
  up to 4.7e-3; \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} cool column median 1\% / max 2.6\% (the
  recombination channel is 64\% of the cooling there), state 1e-4.
\item
  \textbf{Documentation pass}: \texttt{inputdata/sed/README.md} (seven places, the
  stop), \texttt{docs/input\_schema.md} (row 11a coverage rule; rows 11, 11b, 11d,
  K3, K4 line references corrected), \texttt{docs/a2\_oxygen\_option\_design.md}
  (B2 row), \texttt{src/tests/physics\_probe/README.md} (two summaries: 41/15/26 at
  35d9dd5; 83 assertions, 69 PASS, 14 FAIL after 2a with the LLF fix already
  in the tree) and \texttt{grid\_and\_gates/README.md}, rev 3 section 10.2 and 10.5
  "landed" notes, the execution document Steps 1 and 2a marked done,
  \texttt{README.md} and \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} (\texttt{Spectrum\ type:\ Planck}, the coverage
  rule, \texttt{make\ test}, the regression section\textquotesingle s case list corrected from
  eight to eleven).
\end{itemize}

Gate (advisor): \texttt{make\ test} runs eight suites; every 2b assertion GREEN,
RED only on the 2c and Phase 3 targets. Regression, sixteen cases against
the post-2a scratch baseline, single-threaded: \texttt{wasp\_he23off} and
\texttt{hydrostatic\_column} byte-identical (metastable off, no carriers); the other
fourteen moved, Mdot unchanged to two decimals in all sixteen. Largest
relative differences over all cells and columns (single cells; the large
values are v near a zero crossing or trace species in one cell, see the
items):

The scratch baseline was re-snapshotted from these outputs; the post-2a
state of every case is kept in \texttt{backup/regression/baseline\_post2a\_20260905/}.

Follow-ups recorded: \texttt{T\_equation.f90} (the temperature root of the
advection post-process) carries no He 2\^{}3S cooling channel and charges the
He I ground-state coefficients to the summed neutral column, so the \texttt{\_adv}
temperature and the hydro temperature are not solved with one cooling
function (Phase 3 A5/A6 or the \texttt{\_adv} consistency item of Phase 6);
\texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90} 392 calls \texttt{eval\_cool} without \texttt{nheiTR};
\texttt{cross\_sec.f90} 519-528, \texttt{parameters.f90} 475-501 and \texttt{input\_read.f90}
856-858 still describe the H+ column as a proton count and quote a "factor
1.6" between the two double-ionization models that is now reversed and much
smaller; \texttt{write\_output.f90} 200-203 says the \texttt{f\_shield} column is the factor
the rate carries (the rate is now the cell mean); \texttt{pct\_limited} counts only
overshoots above 1e-10 while the clamp acts at any size (same class as the
CO counter, now recorded cumulatively); the give-up path of \texttt{retry\_step}
leaves the loop through the same exit as an accepted step (decision 4);
\texttt{set\_oxygen\_coeffs} is called with strided sections that create array
temporaries in the carrier Jacobian; \texttt{docs/EXHALE\_user\_manual.tex} is stale
on the spectrum types and the SED stop.

\subsection{6. Phase 1 batch 2c: corrections that move every output (2026-09-05)}\label{6-phase-1-batch-2c-corrections-that-move-every-output-2026-09-05}

Fourteen worker items on disjoint files plus advisor edits, every one
measured alone against a frozen snapshot of the tree as it stood when the
item started (private builds; the items therefore chain, and the "before" of
one item is the "after" of those that landed earlier). One rebuild by the
advisor with the conda gfortran 16.2 and its OpenBLAS (md5
\texttt{5845df587aa7948153f1bed3becb28ac}), \texttt{make\ test}, and one regression run
against the post-2b scratch baseline. Plan references: rev 3 sections 10.1
(items 1-3), 10.2 (items 1-6, 9, 11), 10.5 (decisions 13-15, 17, 2c-CONST).
Movement is quoted as the largest relative change over the 500 physical
cells of \texttt{wasp\_full} (WASP-121b, He 2\^{}3S + metals, \texttt{du} stop, no Newton
finish, so its Mdot carries the path-dependent spread of that stop) and
\texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} (hot Uranus, 12000-step relaxation snapshot, not steady,
so its outer-tail and base-cell numbers compare two points of one transient).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{2c-DRJ, the cell width is the cell\textquotesingle s own width} (\texttt{define\_grid.f90},
  \texttt{set\_IC.f90}): \texttt{dr\_j(j)\ =\ r\_edg(j)\ -\ r\_edg(j-1)}, an exact identity of the
  finite-volume grid; the stored width had been the width of cell j+1, 0.7 to
  1.25 percent too large over the stretched region. Every consumer confirmed
  to use \texttt{dr\_j(j)} for cell j; none changed. \texttt{grid\_width\_identity} GREEN.
  \texttt{wasp\_full}: T 2.0e-3, v 5.4e-3 (base cell), n\_e 6.3e-3, x(H+) 1.1e-2,
  mass flux +0.65 percent, printed log10 Mdot 13.24 both. \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}:
  bulk profiles 1e-2 class with the transient tail at 1e-1, mass flux -0.96
  percent, printed Mdot 10.52 -\textgreater{} 10.51.
\item
  \textbf{2c-QUAD, thresholds on bin edges} (\texttt{set\_energy\_vectors.f90}): the photon
  grid is built from bin edges, \texttt{e\_v} the geometric center of each bin and
  \texttt{de\_v} its exact width; H I, He I, He II, the metastable edge and \texttt{e\_mid},
  \texttt{e\_top} are edges, and the X-ray block reaches \texttt{e\_top}.
  \texttt{photon\_grid\_quadrature} GREEN except P\_HeI at 1.2e-3 (closed by 2c-THR).
  \texttt{wasp\_full}: T 7.5e-2 at 1.12 Rp, n\_e 7.8e-2, x(H+) 0.23, base v 0.47,
  mass flux +0.82 percent, log10 Mdot 13.24 -\textgreater{} 13.25. \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}: T
  4.7e-2 (tail), n\_e 2.5e-2, mass flux +0.08 percent, Mdot 10.51 both. This
  is the single largest movement of the batch after the Jupiter radius, and
  it is the removal of the 9.5 percent overcount of P\_HI found by the audit.
\item
  \textbf{2c-BALMER, the Balmer continuum follows the run\textquotesingle s spectrum type}
  (\texttt{excited\_hydrogen.f90}, \texttt{J\_inc.f90}; decision 13): the n = 2
  photoionization rate and heating integrate the field the photon grid uses,
  the power law for a power-law run, the Planck field for \texttt{Planck}, the
  loaded table for \texttt{Load} (\texttt{stellar\_flux\_eV}, \texttt{spectrum\_covers\_eV}); a table
  that does not reach 3647 A stops the run with the decision-17 message
  naming H(n=2). \texttt{balmer\_continuum\_field} GREEN (11 assertions).
  \texttt{wasp\_full} (n = 2 coupling armed): gamma\_2 falls by a factor 2939, n\_e at
  1.02 Rp -12.5 percent, x(H+) at the base -27 percent, T 9.6e-3, wind above
  1.2 Rp and Mdot unchanged (13.25). \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}: byte-identical (no
  stellar lines).
\item
  \textbf{2c-KICK, the momentum kick removed} (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}): the
  \texttt{u(2,:)\ +\ 1e-16} diagnostic aid is gone; the relative step change skips
  cells whose old momentum is exactly zero. \texttt{momentum\_row\_from\_fluxes\_only}
  GREEN. Movement round-off on \texttt{wasp\_full} (1e-9), 4e-6 on
  \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, 4.4e-3 in v on \texttt{lower\_profile} (cold-start snapshot
  with a sign-changing base flux amplifies it); Mdot unchanged everywhere.
\item
  \textbf{2c-STATE, one state per output file} (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}): when the
  secondary-ionization coupling never switched on during the march, the
  final write no longer forces it on; the header says \texttt{sec\_ion=F}, and the
  \texttt{\_adv} files and the heating breakdown are built from the relaxed state.
  \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt} / \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} byte-identical in every case; the
  \texttt{\_adv} files and \texttt{Heating\_breakdown.txt} of the fixed-step snapshots whose
  flip never fired (\texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, \texttt{lower\_profile}, and the other
  molecular gates) change by up to 0.97 in \texttt{n(H\ II)\_adv} at the base.
  \texttt{output\_state\_consistency} stays RED at 1.2e-5: the heat column is
  assembled from the pre-sweep rates and the breakdown re-evaluates on the
  post-sweep composition (Phase 3, together with the \texttt{excited\_H\_update}
  call between the loop and the final write).
\item
  \textbf{2c-DU, the signed mass-flux spread} (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}; decision 15):
  one functional \texttt{mass\_flux\_spread}, \texttt{(max\ -\ min)/max(\textbar{}mean\textbar{},\ 1e-30)} of the
  signed \texttt{rho\ v\ r\^{}2} over \texttt{j\_min..N}, read by the stop, the Newton arming
  and the stage flip, and written to \texttt{run.log} at the final step
  (\texttt{mass\_flux\_spread\_functional.py} GREEN). \texttt{wasp\_full} byte-identical
  (every threshold crossing on the same step); \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}
  byte-identical (fixed steps; the reported \texttt{du} falls from 1.29 to 0.70);
  \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} flips and hands off one step earlier, solution moves
  by 7e-8.
\item
  \textbf{2c-RESTART, a restart keeps the run\textquotesingle s grid} (\texttt{load\_IC.f90}): the file\textquotesingle s
  cell centers are compared with the run\textquotesingle s over the physical cells and a
  relative difference above 1e-10 refuses the restart, naming both grids.
  \texttt{restart\_grid\_guard.sh} GREEN; \texttt{roundtrip} and the three \texttt{hp\_*} cases
  byte-identical on the same grid. Consequence: after the grid-moving items
  of this batch (RJ +2.26 percent of the domain in R\_p units, \texttt{dr\_j}) the
  shipped \texttt{hp\_*} IC pairs are refused, and are regenerated with
  \texttt{backup/regression/make\_hp\_initial\_conditions.py} from a fresh
  \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} output (below).
\item
  \textbf{2c-BASE, one base level with a lower-atmosphere profile}
  (\texttt{input\_read.f90}, \texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90}): with a profile, \texttt{n0} follows
  from \texttt{p\_match\_bar} exactly as from \texttt{p\_base} of \texttt{base.inp}; a density key
  beside a profile is refused unless it agrees within 1 percent
  (\texttt{base\_level\_single\_statement.sh} GREEN). \texttt{lower\_profile} starts at the
  profile\textquotesingle s level: base density 1.0e14 -\textgreater{} 5.0e12 cm\^{}-3, its 12000-step
  snapshot changes entirely (printed log10 Mdot 8.44 -\textgreater{} 7.92, ionization
  front inward, base n\_e x8). The case README and
  \texttt{examples/17\_lower\_profile/input.inp} drop the density key. Nothing else
  moves.
\item
  \textbf{2c-CI, the C I Voronov row} (\texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}): \texttt{P\ =\ 0,\ X\ =\ 0.193,\ K\ =\ 0.25,\ A\ =\ 6.85e-8,\ dE\ =\ 11.26} (Voronov 1997 Table 1; the transcription
  had X and K in the wrong slots, 2.56x at 2000 K, 1.69x at 1e4 K).
  \texttt{voronov\_carbon\_ionization} GREEN. \texttt{wasp\_full} 1e-5 class, x(C I) 6e-4,
  Mdot -1e-5 dex; \texttt{mol\_metals} bitwise identical (collisional ionization of
  C I is negligible at its temperatures).
\item
  \textbf{2c-MASS and 2c-MASS2, helium mass and Jupiter radius} (decision 14;
  \texttt{species\_table.f90}, \texttt{parameters.f90}, \texttt{wae\_exhale\_input.f90},
  \texttt{wae\_ic\_writer.f90}, \texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90},
  \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}, \texttt{lower\_column.f90}, \texttt{utilities.f90},
  \texttt{examples/exhale\_io.py} and the Python tools): He I / He II / He III /
  He 2\^{}3S carry 3.9715 m\_H (HeH+ 4.9715), \texttt{RJ\ =\ 7.1492e9} cm, \texttt{MJ\ =\ 1.8982e30} g, \texttt{Msun\ =\ 1.98842e33} g (IAU 2015 nominal), one definition
  each, the Python tools importing \texttt{RJ\_CM}, \texttt{MJ\_G}, \texttt{MSUN\_G} from
  \texttt{exhale\_io}. Tests \texttt{atomic\_mass\_and\_radius\_constants},
  \texttt{jupiter\_radius\_python\_tools.py}, suite \texttt{species\_masses} GREEN. Measured
  as two sub-steps: the helium mass alone moves \texttt{wasp\_full} by 2.3e-3 in T,
  1.1e-2 in x(H+), Mdot +0.095 percent, and \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} by 1.2e-2
  (tail), Mdot -0.004 percent; the Jupiter radius then rescales every
  physical length (R0, the domain top \texttt{r\_max} in R\_p units, the gravity)
  and moves \texttt{wasp\_full} by 7.3e-2 in T, 0.16 in n\_e, 0.34 in x(H+), raw
  Mdot +11.0 percent (printed 13.24 -\textgreater{} 13.29), and \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} raw
  Mdot +15.8 percent (printed 10.52 -\textgreater{} 10.58). MASS2 alone (the remaining
  copies): \texttt{mol\_diffusion} 2.8e-5, \texttt{mol\_carrier} 6.5e-8, \texttt{wasp\_full}
  byte-identical.
\item
  \textbf{2c-THR, one definition of each threshold} (\texttt{parameters.f90},
  \texttt{cross\_sec.f90} and the files that restated a threshold): \texttt{e\_th\_HI\ =\ 13.598434599}, \texttt{e\_th\_H2\ =\ 15.425927}, \texttt{e\_th\_HeI\ =\ 24.587389}, \texttt{e\_th\_HeII\ =\ 54.417765}, \texttt{e\_th\_HeTR\ =\ 4.767775} eV (NIST ASD), every cross section
  turning on exactly there; \texttt{sigma(E,\ Z)} takes the ion\textquotesingle s measured potential
  as an optional argument so He II is not restated as \texttt{13.6\ Z\^{}2}.
  \texttt{ionization\_threshold\_turn\_on} and \texttt{threshold\_literal\_uniqueness.py}
  GREEN; the P\_HeI residual of QUAD closes (0.99878 -\textgreater{} 0.99995).
  \texttt{wasp\_full} 7e-4 at most, mass flux +0.044 percent; \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}
  1.3e-3 (tail); printed Mdot unchanged.
\item
  \textbf{2c-N2FLOOR, H(n=2) is an absorber of the grid-floor rule} (\texttt{sed\_read.f90}
  \texttt{photon\_grid\_floor\_eV}, \texttt{J\_inc.f90}, \texttt{set\_energy\_vectors.f90},
  \texttt{excited\_hydrogen.f90}): with the excited-hydrogen coupling armed the grid
  floor is \texttt{e\_th\_HI/4} = 3647 A, a loaded SED is read down to it and the

  table to 3700 A now runs). Tests \texttt{sed\_coverage\_stop.sh} and
  \texttt{photon\_grid\_n2\_floor} GREEN: the 20 bins added below the lowest bulk-gas
  absorber change no rate the grid computes, exactly. \texttt{wasp\_full} 2e-4 at
  most (the edge written as \texttt{e\_th\_HI/4} instead of 3.40 eV), Mdot 0.0000
  dex; wall clock +2.3 percent for the 20 bins.
\item
  \textbf{2c-CONST, the particle masses and the astronomical constants in one
  place} (\texttt{parameters.f90}, \texttt{species\_table.f90}, \texttt{lower\_column.f90},
  \texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}, \texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90},
  \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}, \texttt{lyman\_werner.f90}, \texttt{oxygen\_rates.f90}):
  \texttt{m\_He\_atom\ =\ 6.6464790722e-24} g (4.002603254 u x CODATA 2018 u; the brief\textquotesingle s
  6.6464731e-24 was 9.2e-7 low and is not the helium-4 atom), \texttt{amu}, \texttt{m\_e},
  \texttt{m\_p} in \texttt{parameters.f90}, \texttt{m\_He\_over\_m\_H} and \texttt{amu\_over\_m\_H} derived,
  \texttt{bsp\_mass} built from them, \texttt{melem\_A\ =\ melem\_A\_u\ *\ amu/mu}. This closed a
  UNIT ERROR: the standard atomic weights in u had been added to mass sums
  carried in hydrogen atoms, every metal nucleus 0.78 percent too heavy in
  \texttt{rho}, the mean molecular weight, the base composition, the census closure
  and the metal element diffusion. The advisor closed the remaining local
  copies (\texttt{Cool\_coeff} line-center opacity now takes the atomic weight in u
  and uses the global \texttt{amu}; \texttt{amu\_C/N/O\ =\ melem\_A\_u(iel\_*)}; the local
  \texttt{m\_amu\_g} and \texttt{kb\_erg}/\texttt{m\_h2} copies of the diffusion, Lyman-Werner and
  oxygen modules replaced by the globals). \texttt{wasp\_full} 1.0e-3 at most
  (base v), mass flux +3.2e-5; \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} 2.8e-5; printed Mdot
  unchanged.
\item
  \textbf{2c-METALEDGE, every active threshold a bin edge}
  (\texttt{set\_energy\_vectors.f90}): each of the six bands keeps its bin budget and
  is cut at every ionization threshold strictly inside it (H2 when the
  molecular chemistry is on, every photo-ionizable stage of an element with
  nonzero abundance), the budget shared over the sub-intervals in proportion
  to their logarithmic width with at least one bin each; \texttt{Nl} is derived
  from the partition and equals the previous count in every configuration
  (240 \texttt{wasp\_full}, 220 \texttt{mol\_metals}, 220 \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}). A band with
  no interior threshold keeps exactly the partition it had.
  \texttt{photon\_grid\_threshold\_edges} GREEN: seventeen thresholds at relative
  distance 0 from an edge (eighteen with H2), P\_m of C I 1.045 -\textgreater{} 1.000,
  Ca I 0.933 -\textgreater{} 0.997, Na I 1.030 -\textgreater{} 0.998, Fe I 0.965 -\textgreater{} 0.994 of a
  400001-point reference, total H2 photoabsorption 0.985 -\textgreater{} 1.000.
  \texttt{wasp\_full}: T 9.6e-3, n\_e 5.2e-3, x(C I) 0.14 at the base, mass flux
  -0.17 percent, log10 Mdot 13.2860 -\textgreater{} 13.2852. \texttt{mol\_metals}: bulk 2e-3,
  n\_e 4.5e-2 in the neutral layer at 1.1 Rp (ionization degree 1e-6), Mdot
  unchanged. \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}: 1e-4 class, by the H2 edge alone. Open
  (decision needed, below): Mg I, Ca I, Na I, Fe I still miss 1e-3 by 0.25
  to 0.58 percent, the rectangle-rule error of the 20-bin sub-Lyman band.
\item
  \textbf{Advisor edits after the worker:} the H2 channel thresholds
  \texttt{e\_th\_H2\_di} (18.076 eV) and \texttt{e\_th\_H2\_dd} (51.4 eV) join the edge set when
  the molecular chemistry is on, because the channel shares of one cross
  section switch on there (the 32-41.5 eV neutral-dissociation window is
  continuous and is not cut; the comment at the code site says so); the setup
  report prints the grid floor as the lower edge of the first bin
  (\texttt{photon\_grid\_floor\_eV}, or the table floor for a loaded SED) instead of
  the first bin center, which the new partition had moved to 4.95 eV
  against a floor of 4.768 eV. The documents that restated the changed
  quantities were brought in line: \texttt{docs/input\_schema.md} (profile level,
  R\_J), \texttt{docs/a2\_oxygen\_option\_design.md} (molecular masses in m\_H units),
  rev 3 and the execution document (landed marks).
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection{Decision handed to the user: the sub-Lyman band budget}\label{decision-handed-to-the-user-the-sub-lyman-band-budget}

The band \texttt{{[}4.768,\ 13.598{]}} eV has 20 bins (edge ratio 1.054), 4.4 times
coarser in \texttt{d\ ln\ E} than the pure-H I band (50 bins, 1.012), and it is where
every low-IP metal is photoionized; Fe I\textquotesingle s cross section falls by a factor
2.5 across the single bin above its threshold. Measured ladder (code/exact):

\textbf{Decided by the user 2026-09-05: \texttt{num\_TR\ =\ 89}}, the budget that gives the
band the bin ratio of the pure-H I band (0.0118 in ln E). Measured with the
same driver: Mg I 0.999853, Ca I 0.999870, Na I 0.999902, Fe I 0.999615, so
the four rates are asserted at the 1e-3 of every other ion. \texttt{Nl} becomes
309 for \texttt{wasp\_full} (was 240) and 289 for the hot-Uranus cases (was 220);
the H(n=2) band keeps its 20 bins. Every He 2\^{}3S and metals-on case moves
again by this, and the gate below was re-run on the 89-bin binary (md5
\texttt{b0a4ada9f84ee8410c84fda6780ba097}) before the baseline re-snapshot. The
user also decided the \texttt{LHS1140b/examples/photochem\_profile\_base/input.inp}
density key: deleted (the profile fixes the level).

\subsubsection{Gate (advisor)}\label{gate-advisor}

\texttt{make\ test} on the single rebuild: every assertion of this batch GREEN
(\texttt{threshold\_edges}, \texttt{n2\_floor}, \texttt{balmer\_continuum\_field},
\texttt{atomic\_mass\_and\_radius\_constants}, \texttt{species\_masses},
\texttt{ionization\_threshold\_turn\_on}, \texttt{threshold\_literal\_uniqueness},
\texttt{voronov\_carbon\_ionization}, \texttt{grid\_width\_identity}, \texttt{photon\_grid\_quadrature},
\texttt{restart\_grid\_guard}, \texttt{base\_level\_single\_statement},
\texttt{mass\_flux\_spread\_functional}, \texttt{momentum\_row\_from\_fluxes\_only},
\texttt{sed\_coverage\_stop}); \texttt{diffusion\_tests} had 13 of 35 assertions fail because
its own references carried the helium mass as 4 m\_H (7.2e-4 = 1 - 3.9715/4),
and now derives every helium mass from \texttt{m\_He\_over\_m\_H} (35 of 35, no
tolerance changed, the T9 ambipolar targets written as functions of the
mass ratio). RED, as intended, only on the Phase 3 targets:
\texttt{output\_state\_consistency} (1.2e-5), \texttt{h3p\_cooling\_limits},
\texttt{h2\_rovibrational\_identity}, \texttt{h2\_double\_photoevent\_energy\_sum},
\texttt{helium\_collisional\_ionization\_by\_level}.

Regression, sixteen cases against the post-2b scratch baseline,
single-threaded: the thirteen cases that ran all moved beyond the 1e-3
tolerance, as every item of this batch predicts; the three \texttt{hp\_*} restart
cases were refused by the new grid guard (their IC pairs sat on the old
grid: domain top 4.7253 -\textgreater{} 4.6215 R\_p from R\_J, base spacing from \texttt{dr\_j})
and were regenerated with \texttt{make\_hp\_initial\_conditions.py} from the fresh
\texttt{mol\_base\_handoff/output} (nuclei conserved row by row; H II as written /
0 / 1e-12 of the nuclei) and re-run. Movement of the thirteen (the
cell-wise maxima compare the same cell index, whose radius moved by up to
2.2 percent with the domain, so they overstate the change at fixed radius;
the Mdot column is the run\textquotesingle s printed value):

\subsubsection{Golden refresh: prepared, awaiting the user\textquotesingle s command}\label{golden-refresh-prepared-awaiting-the-users-command}

\texttt{golden/} was archived to \texttt{backup/regression/golden\_pre170\_20260905/} with a
README (provenance from the headers, unknowns marked), \texttt{roundtrip} was re-run
on the post-2c binary, and \texttt{DEFAULT\_CASES} of \texttt{run\_check.sh} now lists the
five Phase 0 cases as well. The refresh itself, \texttt{run\_check.sh\ golden} over
the seventeen cases followed by \texttt{make\ check} and one \texttt{run\_fcheck.sh}, is the
user\textquotesingle s command to give; until it is given the reference of record is the
pre-2c state and \texttt{make\ check} reports every case as moved.

\subsubsection{Gate on the 89-bin band (2026-09-06)}\label{gate-on-the-89-bin-band-2026-09-06}

Fourteen cases re-run against the post-2c (20-bin) baseline with md5
\texttt{b0a4ada9f84ee8410c84fda6780ba097}: \texttt{hydrostatic\_column} byte-identical (no
sub-Lyman absorber), every other case within 1.3e-3 in any cell (\texttt{mol\_metals}
rho 1.3e-3, \texttt{wasp\_full} v 1.3e-3 at the base) and printed Mdot unchanged, the
size the 20 -\textgreater{} 89 ladder predicts; the \texttt{hp\_*} IC pairs were regenerated on
the 89-bin \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} output and the three cases run (Mdot 10.56).

\textbf{\texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} stopped on the 89-bin grid, diagnosed.} At step 13,
cell 7, the carrier Newton reached its iteration cap (30) with a relative
OH row residual of 0.63 and the status stop of item 2b-A3a ended the run.
Diagnosis (worker, measured on frozen snapshots): the Newton is correct (a
root exists; the same solver with a larger budget reaches it in 141
iterations, the last three 5.4e-2, 3.1e-9, 3.9e-12) and its residual scale
and the element limiter are not involved. The stiffness comes from upstream:
at step 12 the ionization sweep accepted a non-root at cell 7 under the
relaxation amnesty (\texttt{info\ 4}, normalized residual 5.2e4), which put n\_e =
3.5e10 and n(H+) = 2.6e8 into the frozen background where the previous step
had 1.1e3 and 0, so the H+ + H2 channel asked for two thirds of the cell\textquotesingle s
H2 in one 2.85 s step. On the 20-bin grid cell 7 never took that path. The
"7.8e-2 carrier row" of the 20-bin summary is the STEADY residual of a
relaxation snapshot (dt -\textgreater{} infinity), not the Newton\textquotesingle s; the backward-Euler
Newton converged to below 6e-12 on all 999 steps there. Two things landed:
the iteration budget is 200 (the comment at the code site states the
measurement), and the attribution test of the status stop, which asked
"did an element constraint act" by carrier over the whole grid, now asks it
by cell (the CO ceiling, acting in 160 cells of this case, had made any CO
failure anywhere excusable, and had left OH rows it moved by 49 percent
through the oxygen closure reported as the solver\textquotesingle s); new suite
\texttt{src/tests/carrier\_constraint\_attribution/}, 7 assertions, RED before.
Open for the user: decision 4 (reject and retry the step on a shorter dt,
the structure that also covers a step without a root) and the ionization
sweep\textquotesingle s relaxation amnesty, which handed the carrier system a non-root
(acceptance policy, \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90} class 4).

\subsubsection{Golden refresh executed (2026-09-06, on the user\textquotesingle s instruction)}\label{golden-refresh-executed-2026-09-06-on-the-users-instruction}

\texttt{run\_check.sh\ golden} over sixteen cases: the eleven of the previous
\texttt{DEFAULT\_CASES}, \texttt{hydrostatic\_column}, the three \texttt{hp\_*} restart cases and
\texttt{roundtrip}, all from the 89-bin binary \texttt{b0a4ada9f84ee8410c84fda6780ba097}
(conda gfortran 16.2, OpenBLAS); the previous golden is
\texttt{golden\_pre170\_20260905/}. \texttt{golden/} now carries the \texttt{*\_adv.txt} files as
well. \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} has no golden entry yet (it stops, above); it stays
in \texttt{DEFAULT\_CASES}, so \texttt{make\ check} reports it as the one failing case until
the carrier solver question is settled. \texttt{make\ check} and one
\texttt{run\_fcheck.sh} follow in this section.

\subsubsection{After the refresh, same day (2026-09-06): the loaded-SED grid, the carrier budget, hygiene, the SED files}\label{after-the-refresh-same-day-2026-09-06-the-loaded-sed-grid-the-carrier-budget-hygiene-the-sed-files}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{2c-SEDGRID, the photon grid of a loaded SED} (\texttt{sed\_read.f90}, \texttt{J\_inc.f90},
  \texttt{parameters.f90}, \texttt{set\_energy\_vectors.f90}; item C5 of the D0 draft): the
  Load path kept the table rows as bin points with central-difference
  widths, so no threshold was a bin edge there, on the path every planet
  calculation of record uses (decision 16). Now the bins are built from
  edges (geometric means of neighboring rows, the table span as the two
  end edges), every active ionization threshold inside the span is inserted
  as an edge with the split bin\textquotesingle s two halves carrying the row\textquotesingle s flux (exact
  for the integrated flux), \texttt{e\_v} the geometric center, \texttt{de\_v} the exact
  width; \texttt{read\_sed} receives the threshold list from
  \texttt{ionization\_thresholds\_active}, one definition. The table rows themselves
  are kept in \texttt{e\_sed\_node}/\texttt{F\_sed\_node} (undiluted), which \texttt{stellar\_flux\_eV},
  \texttt{loaded\_table\_floor\_eV} and \texttt{spectrum\_covers\_eV} now read, so the loaded
  field no longer depends on the binning. Test \texttt{sed\_edges}
  (\texttt{src/tests/spectrum\_type/}, 15 assertions) on the eps Eri table of
  WASP-52 b: thresholds at distance 0, flux identity to 2e-16, rate
  deviations from the exact table integral P\_HI 1.8e-3 -\textgreater{} 4.6e-7, P\_HeI

\subsubsection{Phase 1 closing gate (2026-09-06)}\label{phase-1-closing-gate-2026-09-06}

\texttt{make\ test} on the final binary \texttt{b2c15e53c888d09fa90ffdad80443c3a}: ten
suites, eight PASS; RED only on the Phase 3 targets (\texttt{output\_state\_consistency}
1.19e-5, \texttt{h3p\_cooling\_limits}, \texttt{h2\_rovibrational\_identity},
\texttt{h2\_double\_photoevent\_energy\_sum}, \texttt{helium\_collisional\_ionization\_by\_level}).
\texttt{make\ check}: sixteen \texttt{DEFAULT\_CASES} against the refreshed \texttt{golden/}, all
four files byte-identical in every case (\texttt{REGRESSION\ PASS}); the sixteen
goldens taken from the earlier 89-bin binary reproduce on the final one,
which is the measured statement that the loaded-SED grid, the carrier budget,
the \texttt{alpha} removal and the report edits leave the analytic-spectrum matrix
untouched. \texttt{run\_fcheck.sh} result recorded below.
\texttt{run\_fcheck.sh}: the \texttt{-fcheck=bounds,do,mem} build ran 1500 bounded HD 209458 b
steps with no runtime trap (\texttt{fcheck\ regression:\ CLEAN}, log10 Mdot 9.59), and
the production build was restored to \texttt{b2c15e53c888d09fa90ffdad80443c3a}.
\textbf{Phase 1 is closed.} Open for the user before Phase 3: decision 4 (carrier
step rejection/retry), the relaxation amnesty of the ionization sweep that
handed the carrier system a non-root, the D0 draft
(\texttt{docs/d0\_governing\_system\_20260906.md}, 33 consistency items, 15 questions)
for acceptance, the switch of the planet inputs to their loaded SEDs (2d), the
WASP-121 b SED choice (solar shape, WASP-17 shape, or the two joined at 1700 A),
and the sub-Lyman band budget of the \texttt{Load} grid (the table\textquotesingle s own resolution
is kept; Mg II stays at 5.5e-4).

\subsection{7. PLAN\_20260906\_rev2, Step A: acceptance contracts and run modes (2026-09-06)}\label{7-plan_20260906_rev2-step-a-acceptance-contracts-and-run-modes-2026-09-06}

and their two reviews are kept beside it; the execution document of

  \textbf{Advisor correction to the B4 draft:} the formation-energy flux
  \texttt{F\_form\ =\ sum\_s\ eps\_s\ F\_s} is the RESERVOIR\textquotesingle s transport and enters no
  thermal equation (photoheating already carries \texttt{h\ nu\ -\ I}; recombination
  emits \texttt{I}); the draft had written it as a thermal source, which would
  heat a cell by the ionization energy leaving it. B4-2 becomes the
  diagnostic reservoir balance that the ledger identity T1.5 tests, plus the
  face-composition enthalpy (T-B4.5). The B4-2 pre-measurement
  (\texttt{src/utils/formation\_energy\_flux\_diagnostic.py}, section 7 of the design)
  stands as the size of that reservoir transport: on \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} 28
  percent of the absorbed heating leaves the column as advected ionization
  energy (median 14 percent of the local heating, 52 percent at the
  ionization front at 1.23 Rp); the present energy face flux is \texttt{v\ (E\ +\ p)}
  with the caloric H2 internal energy already inside \texttt{E}, addressed by the
  owning cell (T-B4.5). Findings: \texttt{lower\_profile}\textquotesingle s golden mass flux is not
  flat (1.1e-2), so continuity-based diagnostics on it discriminate nothing
  until it is Newton-finished; \texttt{benchmarks/hd209/output} integrates to
  cooling 24 percent above heating on its saved state.

  \texttt{\textbar{}res\textbar{}/phys\ =\ 3.2e-8}, \texttt{\textbar{}res\textbar{}/full\ =\ 2.2e-12}, four orders above the
  round-off bound): the element limiter\textquotesingle s imbalance one cell beyond a

  the run summary and the certification carrier entry are B3a\textquotesingle s (owner of
  both files).

  identical; \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} T 1.3e-5, n(H2) 3.1e-4; \texttt{mol\_diffusion} T

  \textbf{A3b} (\texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}; \texttt{carrier\_retry} 89 assertions):

  (count, first/last step, worst row and both scales), the carrier history
  is marked not certifiable (\texttt{carrier\_history\_certifiable()}), and the march
  continues from the entry carriers. The accepted CO record is indexed by
  A0-impl\textquotesingle s \texttt{ledger\_family}. Impact: \texttt{mol\_carrier}, \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}
  bitwise identical; \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} (init, its default) now runs its
  1000 steps, exit 0, with 998 exhausted intervals (first at step 2, worst
  CO at cell 313), log10 Mdot 9.61 against the 20-bin 9.60, and a final
  certification refusing on four carrier rows at 1.0: its carriers are
  frozen at the step-2 values for the whole run, which is exactly what the
  mark and the ledger say. The refused row is the real imbalance beside a
  clamped cell (\texttt{\textbar{}res\textbar{}/full} 4e-12 against a 1.4e-14 round-off bound), open
  physics of the excluded CO model, not a controller defect. Its golden is
  re-taken at the end of this series with the mark noted in the case

\item
  \textbf{B5pre} (\texttt{steady\_newton.f90}; suite \texttt{steady\_completion\_flag}): the JFNK
  and PTC completion flag is decided AFTER the certification of the returned
  state, from the same record the certification prints; a solve that met
  the gate at the loop top and not at the returned state ends \texttt{info\ =\ 2}
  naming the row. Measured on \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}: loop-top \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 5.5e-6},
  returned state 4.2e-4; the existing own-composition consistency pass
  opens the gap (0, 1, 2, 3, 8 sweeps: 5.5e-6, 1.9e-4, 3.2e-4, 3.9e-4,
  4.2e-4), so the hydro-only Newton converges to a state that is not a root
  of the coupled hydro + composition system: the loop-top gate measures a
  lagged residual. Consequence today: \texttt{EXHALE\_main} treats \texttt{info\ =\ 2} as a
  solver failure and falls back to marching, which ends on a du plateau
  with a state that certifies worse than the JFNK state; assigned to B3a
  (\texttt{info\ =\ 2} = a failed stationarity claim, written uncertified, exit 2, no
  fallback) and to B5a (\texttt{steady\_newton.f90}: the residual evaluated on the
  self-consistent composition of the current iterate, so gate, merit and
  returned state measure one thing). Also found: the JFNK stop message
  prints an unset \texttt{resid\_th} (B3a); \texttt{certification\_row\_measure} and
  \texttt{relnorm\_over\_cells} are one expression written twice.
\item
  \textbf{B3b-PE} (\texttt{h2\_photo\_channels.f90}; \texttt{photoevent\_energy\_ledger} 105
  assertions): the 19.725 eV missing from the H2 double photoionization
  event is the kinetic energy release of the two protons: 51.4 eV is a
  vertical threshold (Yan et al. 1998 section 4), the asymptotic product
  energy is \texttt{D0(H2)\ +\ 2\ I(H)\ =\ 31.675} eV, and energy conservation at the
  threshold fixes the fragment kinetic energy with no free parameter
  (Franck-Condon check \texttt{e\^{}2/R\_e\ =\ 19.42} eV, 1.5 percent). Second defect
  found: the 33 to 41 eV neutral window dissociates into \texttt{H(2p)\ +\ H(2s)}
  (Chung et al. 1993 p. 887), so 20.398 eV per event is internal excitation
  leaving as prompt Lyman-alpha and two-photon continuum, which the
  integrand charged to heat. \texttt{h2\_channel\_energy\_recipients} (reservoir,
  electron, fragment kinetic, radiated; sums to \texttt{h\ nu}) with the per-channel
  table and sources. Channels M and S already closed; every atomic absorber
  and all 17 metal ions correct (\texttt{wasp\_full} byte-identical). Impact:
  \texttt{mol\_sec\_ion} T 4.2e-4, \texttt{heat\_H2} 4.5e-2; \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} T 2.1e-5;
  Mdot unchanged. The four-line wiring of the two new terms into the
  heating integrand of \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} is handed to B4-6 (owner of that
  file). Open: suprathermal H2 dissociation credits heat but destroys no
  H2; the double channel has no absorber row of its own; channel S has no
  source for a fragment kinetic split.
\item
  \textbf{B3b-H3} (\texttt{h3p\_cooling.f90}; \texttt{h3p\_cooling\_limits} 17 assertions): the
  Miller et al. 2013 fits as the paper defines them: Table 5 is the LTE
  emission of one molecule per steradian (\texttt{Lambda\ =\ n(H3+)\ 4\ pi\ E(T)\ s}),
  Table 6\textquotesingle s \texttt{s(T,\ n\_H2)} is scaled LTE with H2 the only collider (Oka and
  Epp coefficient; the paper\textquotesingle s upper-limit caveat at the code site); below
  the table\textquotesingle s \texttt{1e6\ cm\^{}-3} the non-LTE factor follows the exact collisional
  limit (linear in \texttt{n\_H2}, zero at zero), replacing the clamp at the lowest
  column; above \texttt{1e14} the table edge is held; the four Table 5 segments do
  not join (measured with the paper\textquotesingle s coefficients: +43 percent at 300 K,
  -0.11 percent at 800 K, -2.4 percent at 1800 K) and the code joins them by
  a stated linear blend in \texttt{log\_e\ E} over the 5 percent below each join
  (Table 6\textquotesingle s 300 K value supports the upper polynomial); B1 T6.3 reworded to
  say so. Published values reproduced per segment to the paper\textquotesingle s precision.
  Impact: the H3+ cooling peak unmoved; T moves 1e-5 to 2.7e-4 in the three
  molecular cases; the clamp removal changes cells beyond 1.22 Rp where
  \texttt{n\_H2\ \textless{}\ 1e6} by more than 50 percent at 1e-10 of the peak (emission from
  gas with no collision partners, gone); \texttt{wasp\_full} identical. Four domain
  counters exposed (B6 wiring is the certification owner\textquotesingle s).
\item

  EXHALE\textquotesingle s 912 to 1110 A beam, unshielded 2.6e-10 s\^{}-1 in the Draine field,

  on the \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} run\textquotesingle s own base columns (log N\_CO 18.96, log N\_H2
  21.80) it gives \texttt{tau\_dest\ =\ 2.8e8} s, twelve decades faster than the
  barrier channels and still two to six decades short of \texttt{tau\_dest\ \textless{}\textless{}\ tau\_res} at the base; zero in \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, whose spectrum carries no
  flux longward of 911.8 A. RATE12\textquotesingle s paper prints no entries (the data file
  is still needed for the ion channels); Venot 2012 is valid 300 to 2500 K
  and 0.01 to 100 bar (EXHALE\textquotesingle s base at 1e-6 bar is four decades below);
  none of the six sources covers the 3000 to 4000 K band where the ceiling
  fires. The CO exclusion (B1 decision 7) stands, now quantitatively. If a
  CO photodissociation term is ever added it is a fourth absorber on the
  existing Lyman-Werner beam with a second column (\texttt{N\_CO}) and the cell-mean
  form of \texttt{lyman\_werner\_dissociation\_rate}.

    Photon conservation on the written states: cell mean 1.000000000 of the

\item
  \textbf{A2tol} (measurement; \texttt{a2\_certification\_contract\_20260906.md} section 9):
  the \texttt{du} stop and the hydrodynamic row measures are not measures of one
  thing (over a decade of \texttt{du} on \texttt{wasp\_full} the mass row falls at order
  0.2, the momentum row rises, the energy row is not monotone; \texttt{mol\_carrier}\textquotesingle s
  carrier row falls at order 0.08 over 12000 to 48000 steps); the JFNK
  returns an energy row 41 to 47 times its target at every rung of a
  two-decade \texttt{Resid\ tol} ladder (structural; B5a); the elemental transport
  rows cannot be measured on the marching route (the operator advances 1e-6
  of the way per CFL step) and the diffusive cases never reach the steady
  route (du stalls at 5 to 6e-2 against the 1e-2 hand-off). Round-off floors
  on the converged JFNK state: mass 1.0e-13, momentum 5.1e-12, energy
  1.6e-11 (residual bitwise reproducible). Recommended and adopted
  (applied by B3a in \texttt{certification.f90}, provisional until B5a):
  \texttt{cert\_tol\_mass\ 3e-12}, \texttt{cert\_tol\_momentum\ 1e-8}, \texttt{cert\_tol\_energy\ 5e-5},
  \texttt{cert\_tol\_element\ 1e-8} (with the homopause regime caveat), carrier,
  closure and level tolerances kept. At these values \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}
  certifies with \texttt{Resid\ tol:\ 1.0e-7} (marginally at 1e-6) and nothing as
  shipped. Noted for the user: the shipped default \texttt{Resid\ tol} 1e-5 is one
  to two decades looser than the solve reaches at no cost.
\item
  \textbf{B5a} (\texttt{steady\_newton.f90}; suite \texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual}):
  \texttt{eval\_residual} eliminates the composition AT the state it evaluates
  (the equilibrium sweep iterated at fixed conserved variables until the
  composition stops moving), so the loop-top gate, the line-search merit,
  the returned state and the certification measure one object; the gate
  equals the certification to 1e-12 by construction. Finding: the
  eliminated residual\textquotesingle s Jacobian carries the summed Picard response
  \texttt{(I\ -\ M)\^{}-1}, and a finite-difference probe with a convergence test stops
  after one pass (the seed already is the state\textquotesingle s composition), so the
  Newton direction ascended; the probes now run a FIXED sweep count taken
  from an elimination seeded at a different state (about 15 terms), and the
  "frozen-composition Jacobian" the brief offered as a fallback is exactly
  the arrangement that fails. \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}: \texttt{info\ =\ 0} at
  \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 4.4e-7} in 9 iterations and CERTIFIED (was \texttt{info\ =\ 2} at
  4.8e-5 after 26), state moved 1.0e-4 at most, log10 Mdot 13.27, cost
  1.49x. \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} identical. Open: \texttt{Resid\ tol} values were
  calibrated on the lagged measure; the other \texttt{Solver:\ Newton} cases and
  the PTC route are measured at the series gate; \texttt{n\_selfconsistent\_max}
  comment stale (B3a); the duplicated row-measure expression (B3a).

  rule of \texttt{calc\_column\_dens} on the cell\textquotesingle s own opacity, with the cell\textquotesingle s own

  where the photon is created). Impact: \texttt{hydrostatic\_column},

  \textbf{A2exit} (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} exit block; \texttt{run\_mode} suite 30 assertions,
  \texttt{certification} shell gate): the certification exit status follows the
  CLAIM, not the mode: a run that declared a stationary state (du stop,
  residual gate, JFNK finish) and was refused exits 2 in every mode with
  its outputs written; a capped or plateaued run exits 0. \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}
  now exits 2 with \texttt{info\ =\ 2}; \texttt{run\_check.sh} never reads the status. Advisor:
  \texttt{run\_newton.sh} and \texttt{run\_baseline.sh} (set -e loops) now report a status
  2 instead of aborting. Open: the \texttt{EXHALE\_PTC=1} route stops before the
  certification and so claims a stationary state with no verdict (B5).
\item
  \textbf{B4-4a} (\texttt{Apply\_BC.f90}; \texttt{hydrostatic\_residual} +1 and new
  \texttt{free\_outflow\_boundary} 18 assertions): the outer free-outflow ghost is the
  isothermal hydrostatic continuation of the last cell (\texttt{rho\_g\ =\ rho\_N\ exp{[}-(phi\_g\ -\ phi\_N)/(p\_N/rho\_N){]}}, \texttt{p\_g} by the same factor, \texttt{v\_g\ =\ v\_N}),
  one rule for PLM and WENO3, replacing the zero-gradient copy (PLM) and the
  linear extrapolation (WENO3). The outer cell\textquotesingle s momentum residual on the
  analytic column falls from 0.750 of its weight at every N to 4.4e-3 to
  1.0e-4 over N = 250 to 2000 (order 1.8 PLM, 1.6 WENO3); interior and base
  unchanged; four ghost rules measured, this one 30 to 40x better. Velocity:
  the copy (a continuity-exact rule stopped fastest but divides by a ghost
  density the stratification makes small and was the one rule under which
  \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} segfaulted at -O3). Two premises of the brief
  corrected: \texttt{wasp\_full}\textquotesingle s edge is subsonic (Mach 0.18 to 0.96;
  \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} is the supersonic case), and a supersonic edge blocks
  the ghost only through the Riemann solution, not the stencil, so byte
  identity is not available. Impact: \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} one cell above 1e-4;
  \texttt{hydrostatic\_column} 3.5e-3 at cell N; \texttt{wasp\_full} du stop 16208 -\textgreater{} 17135
  steps with the base-breathing phase moved (11 percent in rho at 1.27 Rp,
  mean mass flux above \texttt{r\_flux} 0.078 percent); \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} Mdot
  13.27 both. Removing the outer residual does not by itself stop the
  marched hydrostatic column\textquotesingle s drain (still 0.79 c\_s at 300 steps). Reported:
  a reproducible -O3-only segfault reachable from the rejected velocity rule
  (latent; O3SEGV to diagnose); \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} 2538-2540 ghost-counter
  comment stale (B3c); B4 design section 8.3 (ii) historical.
\item
  \textbf{O0FPE} (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}; shell test \texttt{fpe\_traps} in \texttt{grid\_and\_gates};
  \texttt{run\_fcheck.sh} header): the \texttt{-O0} failure was two defects. (1) An integer
  division by zero in \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} about 1234: \texttt{mod(count,\ n\_err\_every)} with \texttt{n\_err\_every\ =\ 0} in \texttt{init} mode, reached because \texttt{.and.}
  does not short-circuit (\texttt{-O1} and above happen to skip it); fix handed to
  B3c (owner of the file). (2) A real floating-point overflow in
  \texttt{he\_rec\_coupling} (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90:2813}): \texttt{1100\ exp(1.2/T4)\ sqrt(T4)} at
  17 K is 3.9e309, an \texttt{Inf} consumed as \texttt{ne/Inf\ =\ 0} into the correct
  low-density limit; the product is now formed only above \texttt{T4\ =\ 1.71e-3}
  and the limit value taken below, byte-identical on \texttt{hydrostatic\_column},
  \texttt{wasp\_full}, \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}. A third site of the same class (the
  stall detector\textquotesingle s \texttt{huge} sentinel) also handed to B3c. \texttt{run\_fcheck.sh}
  states why it builds at -O1.
\item
  \textbf{B4-6c} (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} \texttt{fuv\_lw\_photon\_field}; driver

  carries its own mean instead of a neutral 1. The face rule was 1.0018x on

  in the base cell of a molecular base whose line-center column reaches 1e7
  in one scale height. Consumers: the H2O and OH photolysis in B2 (both

  moves by factors at 45 steps, shown by a control (a 4e-5 perturbation of

  amplification, not the size of the change; the case is not usable as a
  controlled point until it converges. Finding: \texttt{write\_output.f90}\textquotesingle s FUV band
  ledger closed form \texttt{N\_ph\ (1\ -\ exp(-tau))} carries no line factor for B2 and

\item
  \textbf{LW-NORM-B} (user decision 2026-09-06: recommendation (B) adopted;
  landed): ONE Lyman-Werner band, 912-1201 A, with one normalization.

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{The band.} \texttt{oxygen\_rates.f90} carries four FUV bands, not five: the
    Lyman-Werner interval is 912-1201 A and band B1 (1110-1201 A) is gone
    into it. 1110 A was an edge because Draine \& Bertoldi end the Solomon
    process there for interstellar v = 0 gas; at the 700-3200 K of a
    planetary base the vibrationally excited levels pump longward of it, and
    the self-shielding table\textquotesingle s line list always ran to 1200 A. The two
    halves carried the same H2O yield triplet, so no quantum yield moves;
    the band-averaged cross sections are re-formed over the whole interval
    and MEASURED again from the same Photochem files: sigma(H2O)
    8.51337e-18, sigma(OH) 4.74371e-18 cm\^{}2, (H2O) 12.0526 eV, (OH)
    11.3829 eV, = 2hc/(912+1201 A) = 11.7354 eV. They agree to seven
    digits with the merge of the two shipped halves, and they reproduce the
    values the band carried before it was split in 2026-08.
  \item
    \textbf{The key.} \texttt{Stellar\ LW\ flux} now means the 912-1201 A band flux at the
    orbit; \texttt{Stellar\ FUV\ B1\ flux} is retired and a file that states it stops
    the run with a message naming the merge and asking for the two numbers
    added. \texttt{Spectrum\ type:\ Load} integrates 912-1201 A. The resolved dump
    loses \texttt{fuv\_band\_B1\_flux}. The inputs that carried both keys were
    restated to their sum: 343.0 + 137.9 = 480.9 for the HD 209458 b
    spectrum (\texttt{Update\_EXHALE\_stage1.md} records the 912-1201 A integral of
    that spectrum as 481.0 and its halves as those two numbers), and the
    LW-only cases (\texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner}, \texttt{armA\_LW}, \texttt{armD\_D2\_LW},
    \texttt{armD\_D2\_LW\_newton}) from 343.0 to 480.9 so that they keep the same
    star. The README\_HOWTO HD 189733 b recipe goes from LW 600.1 + B1 648.4
    to LW 1248.5.
  \item
    \textbf{The table.} \texttt{src/utils/h2\_shielding\_lbl/lbl\_table.py} normalizes per
    photon of 912-1201 A (\texttt{WL\_HI}, \texttt{wl\_max}, and \texttt{E\_LW\_PHOTON\_ERG} the mean
    photon energy of that band); \texttt{F\_BAND\_ERG} is restated as
    343.0*289/198 = 500.6 with the derivation in the file, and the file now
    records that it cancels out of \texttt{sigma\_pump} exactly. The nine
    \texttt{abs\_T\#\#\#\#.npz} were recomputed (\texttt{run\_abs.py}, 23 to 251 s each) and
    \texttt{h2\_self\_shielding\_table.f90} regenerated. MEASURED: the generator
    reproduced the shipped module byte for byte before the change, so the
    only difference is the band. \texttt{sigma\_pump} at the bottom of the column
    axis is 1.701e-17 (700 K) to 1.887e-17 cm\^{}2, which is DB96\textquotesingle s own
    2.557e-17 per 912-1110 A photon restated per 912-1201 A photon
    (1.676e-17) to 1-13 percent, the same agreement as before.
  \item
    \textbf{The beam loses what the table rates.} The band share A is no longer
    the DB96 eq. (39) equivalent width; it is the column integral of the
    pump cross section \texttt{sigma\_diss/p\_eff} of the same table, in closed form
    (\texttt{h2\_lw\_band\_photon\_fraction\_absorbed}, exact for the module\textquotesingle s own
    interpolant knot interval by knot interval). MEASURED: A at the top of
    the column axis is 0.6903 to 0.9974 over the whole (T, n\_H) grid, where
    it used to reach 1.5154; the closed form agrees with an independent
    20000-step quadrature of the two public accessors to 2e-10. The band
    ledger\textquotesingle s photon count is now the cell mean of the same pump cross
    section (\texttt{lyman\_werner\_band\_absorption\_rate\_cell\_mean}), on the
    quadrature the dissociation rate already used, and not the cell mean of
    \texttt{sigma\_diss} divided by \texttt{p\_eff} at one column: on cells spanning 2.5 H2
    scale heights the second stood 8.0 percent above the beam loss and the
    first closes on it to 1e-6 (\texttt{physics\_probe/lyman\_werner\_cell\_mean}
    sec. 6). \texttt{h2\_band\_equivalent\_width} and eq. (39) are removed; the two
    published self-shielding fits stay, called by nothing, for the
    comparison of \texttt{docs/h2\_self\_shielding\_cloudy.md}.
  \item
    \textbf{The ledger.} The LW row of \texttt{output/FUV\_bands.txt} is a closure like
    B3 and B4, and its label says so. MEASURED on the \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry}
    state, 5 steps: \texttt{unrated\_frac} -3.62e-1 before, -1.18e-2 after, and the
    LW absorbed energy goes from 233.5 against an incident 171.5 (36 per
    cent over the band) to 202.3 against 200.0. The residual left is the
    part of the column above the top of the table\textquotesingle s column axis: this case
    reaches 6.4e21 cm\^{}-2 against an axis top of 5e21, where the clamped edge
    cross section drives A past 1 and the beam is capped while the rate is
    not. The run already warns about that column.

  \textbf{B3c} (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90},
  \texttt{energy\_semi\_implicit.f90}, \texttt{molecular\_reaction\_heat.f90}; new suite
  \texttt{coupled\_source\_step}, 30 assertions in the advisor\textquotesingle s run): rows 7 to 9 of

  composition projection (\texttt{comp\_p\_from\_T} + \texttt{W\_to\_U}, which rebuilt the
  energy row at the new particle count) is gone; the energy row is anchored
  on \texttt{u\_th\_old}, the thermal energy the cell had at its old composition;
  \texttt{rho} is \texttt{intent(in)} to the chemistry and the mass sum is a checked
  diagnostic (worst 6.4e-12, T2.2). The two collisional deposits D0 C25
  named (oxygen O1/O1r/O2/O2r/O6, associative He 2\^{}3S) are in as differences
  of the one formation-energy table. RED reference kept in the suite: the
  removed update changed \texttt{u(3)} by -0.600 with \texttt{heat\ =\ cool\ =\ 0}.
  \textbf{Advisor decision on the worker\textquotesingle s deviation (accepted):} \texttt{Delta\ u\_form}
  is NOT added to the energy row. In this code \texttt{heat} and \texttt{cool} are the net
  THERMAL sources (photoheating deposits \texttt{h\ nu\ -\ E\_th}, collisional
  ionization cooling removes \texttt{E\_th}, every molecular, Penning, Lyman-Werner
  and oxygen reaction heat is a difference of the same table), so the
  formation reservoir is already accounted for and the term would double
  count; MEASURED with it added, \texttt{wasp\_full} dies at step 0 with 368 of 504
  cells at the floor. Consequence: T1.5/T1.6 as an identity on
  \texttt{u\_th\ +\ u\_form} against \texttt{dt(heat\ -\ cool)} cannot close; the B3a instrument
  (\texttt{attempted\_step.f90}) is to be restated as \texttt{d\ u\_th\ =\ dt(heat\ -\ cool)} +
  the transport contribution of the step, or evaluated on the source substep
  alone (item T15-INSTRUMENT, pending). Impact (control = same snapshot with
  the edits reversed): \texttt{wasp\_full} to its \texttt{du} stop, log10 Mdot 13.34 -\textgreater{}
