  \textbf{B3b-CO landed: the CO row carries two published destruction rates}
  (2026-09-06). \texttt{src(ic\_CO)} is no longer \texttt{0}. It is
  \texttt{-(k\_D1\ n(He+)\ +\ k\_CO)\ n\_CO} with \texttt{k\_D1\ =\ 1.60e-9\ cm\^{}3\ s\^{}-1} (UMIST
  RATE22 entry 4068, measured, accuracy better than 25 percent,
  temperature independent, READ) and \texttt{k\_CO} the cell mean of
  \texttt{(F\_LW/\textless{}hv\textgreater{})\ sigma\_CO\ Theta(N\_CO,\ N\_H2)\ exp(-tau\_cont)} on the
  912-1201 A beam. New: \texttt{co\_self\_shielding\_table.f90} (the 12CO block of
  Visser, van Dishoeck \& Black 2009 Table 6, \texttt{T\_ex(CO)\ =\ 50\ K}, with their
  Table 5 beside it for the spread) and \texttt{co\_photodissociation.f90} (the
  point rate, the two-column cell mean, the fragment heat).
  \texttt{fuv\_lw\_photon\_field} gains the CO column and returns \texttt{k\_CO} and \texttt{Theta};
  \texttt{carrier\_photolysis} rebuilds \texttt{k\_CO} from the current carrier columns, so
  the CO layer shields itself inside the relaxation. Constants, all
  MEASURED: \texttt{sigma\_CO\_band\ =\ 1.0160e-17\ cm\^{}2} per beam photon (Heays et al.
  2017 cross section over the 912-1118 A window CO absorbs in),

  bands), \texttt{D0(CO)\ =\ 11.1157\ eV} and \texttt{q(D1)\ =\ +2.2117\ eV}, both differences
  of the one formation-energy table; the deposit is 1.7517 eV per
  dissociation. Both deposits are channels 18 and 19 of
  \texttt{heating\_of\_composition} (17 -\textgreater{} 19 channels) and are formed nowhere else;

  its radius, \texttt{tau\_res/tau\_form} from RATE22 8597, the count of cells above

\item
  \textbf{B3c-COST, the cost of the coupled step measured and the skip specified,
  not implemented} (2026-09-06; \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, \texttt{attempted\_step.f90},
  the \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} and \texttt{attempted\_step} suites). WHERE THE COST IS,
  MEASURED on \texttt{wasp\_full} 300 steps, one thread: the run timers give
  \texttt{ioniz\_eq} 83.6 percent and the energy solve 16.3 percent of the marching
  wall time, and a gprof build puts \textbf{78 percent of the whole marching time
  inside the MINPACK solve of one cell\textquotesingle s chemistry} (one Jacobian and one QR
  per cell per pass; the warm start already reaches each root in one Newton
  step), against about 13 percent in the whole-grid rate and field work a
  pass repeats. So the cost is the number of CELL solves and not the field,
  and reusing the field between passes is worth at most that 13 percent.
  THE CONVERGENCE STUDY CONTRADICTS THE EXPECTATION THE ITEM WAS WRITTEN
  FROM: after pass 2, 308 of the 500 cells are still above the tolerances,
  after pass 5 still 130, and the cell that sets the pass count walks inward
  one cell per pass with the ionization front. Contraction 0.191 median over
  2729 passes. A cell-local skip is therefore worth 2.41x on the cell solves
  with a one-pass rule -\/- but that rule is WRONG: a cell at rest moves again
  on 177 of the 3029 passes, 690 of the 809 wake-ups at pass 8, because the
  column keeps moving a cell after the cell has stopped. \textbf{Two consecutive
  passes of rest: zero wake-ups, 1.96x on the cell solves, 1.55x on the
  step.} The skip has to be a mask in the cell loop of \texttt{ioniz\_eq}, which
  this item does not own (HEATBRK was writing to that file at 20:30 and
  20:35), so it is SPECIFIED and not implemented: the optional
  \texttt{cell\_at\_rest} argument, the class-6 path (\texttt{sys\_x\ =\ x\_entry} straight to
  the extraction) as the body of the skip, and the one consequence that is
  not a pure skip -\/- the acceptance ledger describes the cells the sweep
  SOLVED, so a mask needs an acceptance class stored for each cell and inherited by
  masked cells, or \texttt{n\_cells\_without\_chemical\_root} reads a different
  population from the one it believes. TOLERANCE ANCHORED AND KEPT AT 1e-8:
  the anchor the comment gave (the 1e-6 heat-column gate) stopped depending
  on it when HEATBRK made the two assemblies one; the anchor that binds is
  \texttt{cert\_tol\_carrier} = \texttt{cert\_tol\_element} = 1e-8 on the composition this step
  returns. Measured 1e-10 to 1e-5 on \texttt{wasp\_full} 300 and \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}
  1500, the adopted state moves by about the tolerance and a decade costs 1.3
  passes and 15 percent of the wall time, \textbf{so the tolerance is not the cost
  lever}; the step\textquotesingle s own truncation error (step-doubling, \texttt{phys}, 5.3e-3
  relative) is five decades above the loosest value and does not bind.
  Newton on T with the composition\textquotesingle s response (item 4) stays live, and the
  CHEAP version of it is refuted by measurement: the contraction ratio
  spreads by 122 percent globally and by 200 percent cell by cell over the tail
  of a step, so the sequence is geometric on average over a run and not
  within one step at one cell -\/- which is why B3c\textquotesingle s Aitken failed, and why
  the acceleration has to come from \texttt{dc/dT\ =\ -(dF/dc)\^{}-1\ (dF/dT)} against
  the factorization \texttt{hybrd} already forms, not from the iterates.
  ALSO FIXED, a defect of my own file found outside the task: the run\textquotesingle s cost
  line divided passes accumulated over every ENTRY of the coupled loop by the
  number of adopted STEPS, two different populations, so it could report a
  mean above the worst single entry (MEASURED on the control, \texttt{wasp\_full} 300
  in \texttt{phys} mode: "13.98 passes per step on average, worst 11"). It now
  divides by the entries and names them. ALSO: \texttt{heat\_channel\_state} is in the
  attempted-step checkpoint (save/restore/compare/perturb), the four lines
  HEATBRK\textquotesingle s report asked for, with two assertions RED on the control (the
  moved array is not seen, and the restore leaves it 3.0 off). Tests:
  \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} 36/0 (33 before), \texttt{attempted\_step} 97/0 (95 before).
  Impact, control = the same tree snapshot with this item\textquotesingle s two production
  files reverted: \texttt{wasp\_full} 300, \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} 1500 and \texttt{mol\_metals}
  1500 all \textbf{byte-identical} in \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt} and \texttt{Ion\_species.txt}.

\item
  \textbf{B3b-CO2 landed: the He+ + CO channel now consumes the helium ion}
  (2026-09-06). Row (2) of \texttt{mol\_heh\_rows} (\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol.f90}) loses
  \texttt{k\_D1\ n\_CO\ n(He+)} with \texttt{k\_D1\ =\ rk\_D1\_Hep\_CO()\ =\ 1.60e-9\ cm\^{}3\ s\^{}-1}, and
  its turnover scale (\texttt{set\_mol\_turnover\_rates}) gains \texttt{k\_D1\ n\_CO\ n\_He}; the
  helium leaves neutral, so the free neutral He that closes the helium budget
  receives it and no other row moves. One row serves every path:
  \texttt{System\_HeH\_mol\_metals} and the constrained-equilibrium residual both call
  it, and neither molecular system has an analytic Jacobian to extend
  (\texttt{hybrd1}, finite differences), so the test asserts the difference
  derivative the solver actually builds. CO is a background density of that
  row and not an unknown, because the one-sided model gives it no formation
  and therefore no local equilibrium; the reaction is split across the two
  operators, each reading the other\textquotesingle s frozen value from the one rate, which
  makes the heat of channel 18 the heat of exactly the events the species
  ledger performs. The products needed nothing added: the free-atomic-oxygen
  closure of \texttt{carrier\_source} and \texttt{nC\_free\ -\ n\_CO} in \texttt{carrier\_write\_back}
  already carry the two nuclei and \texttt{element\_census} already counts them in

\item
  \textbf{B4-1} (\texttt{species\_face\_flux.f90} new; \texttt{Reconstruction.f90}, \texttt{PLM\_rec.f90},
  \texttt{RK\_rhs.f90}, \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, \texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90}, \texttt{Makefile},
  new suite \texttt{src/tests/species\_face\_flux/}): the advective half of the ELEMENT
  transport now rides on the hydrodynamic face mass flux, inside the
  Runge-Kutta stages, on the same faces, areas, volumes and time step as the
  mass row. The face composition is the reconstruction of the mass fraction by
  the same limiter as the primitive variables, taken from the side the face
  mass flux selects, and the closing member of the set is one minus the
  others, so \texttt{sum\_s\ F\_s(j)\ =\ F\_rho(j)} holds by construction; the operator
  asserts it at every face of every stage (largest residual MEASURED in the
  unit suite: 2.1e-16 relative, against a tolerance of 64 ulp). The
  cell-velocity upwind advection of \texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90} is gone
  from the marching path: the operator advects only when it is given an
  advecting mass flux, which the fixed-wind relaxation supplies and the
  marching path does not, and the trace-metal solve lost its advective term
  the same way. The carriers of \texttt{photochemical\_transport\_step} are NOT in this
  increment (that file was held by another item): they keep their present
  advective term and are B4-1c.
  Tests, all MEASURED with a private build: a uniform composition is preserved
  to 2 ulp on a compressing flow, an expanding flow and one whose face mass
  fluxes alternate in sign, for PLM and for WENO3; the mass fraction stays in
  {[}0,1{]} on a step profile carried across the domain in 300 steps; the closing
  member of the set differs from an independently reconstructed \texttt{1\ -\ Y} by
  1.1e-16; the face reconstruction converges at order 1.97 (PLM) and 1.92
  (WENO3); the helium nucleus total of the domain changes only by its boundary
  fluxes to 4.9e-17, where the cell-velocity form it replaces leaves 1.6e-9 on
  the same column and the same step (RED and GREEN in one run); the injected
  violation of the face identity stops the operator with the face named; the
  new step is refusable at its injection point and the run goes on. The
  element-diffusion suite \texttt{diffusion\_tests} is 35 of 35 after T11 was rewritten
  onto the new operator (its premise, that a face-averaged upwind decouples a
  cell whose face velocities straddle zero, is what the face-flux form removes).
  Impact, MEASURED on scratch copies, \texttt{OMP\_NUM\_THREADS=1}, \texttt{mol\_*}/\texttt{hp\_*} at
  200 steps and \texttt{mol\_diffusion}/\texttt{lower\_profile} at 1000 rather than the 12000
  their \texttt{maxsteps} pins, \texttt{wasp\_*} at 300: 14 cases byte-identical, every one of
  them without element transport (\texttt{wasp\_full}, \texttt{wasp\_he23off},
  \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}, \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, \texttt{mol\_metals}, \texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner},
  \texttt{mol\_ir\_bands}, \texttt{mol\_sec\_ion}, \texttt{mol\_carrier}, \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry},
  \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed}, \texttt{hp\_trace\_seed}, \texttt{hp\_front}, \texttt{hydrostatic\_column});
  \texttt{mol\_diffusion} moves by at most 7.1e-5 in \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} and 3.6e-3 in
  \texttt{Cooling\_breakdown.txt}, \texttt{lower\_profile} by 1.2e-3 and 1.1e-2. The
  transported quantity, \texttt{n\_He/n\_H}, moves by 1.9e-10 at the base and 3.5e-6 at
  the molecular front of \texttt{mol\_diffusion}; the base-layer mass flux \texttt{rho\ v\ r\^{}2}
  moves by at most 9.2e-7 over \texttt{1.00\ \textless{}=\ r\ \textless{}=\ 1.16}. Those are smaller than the
  design\textquotesingle s estimate because at 1000 steps neither case has separated its
  elements far from the reservoir ratio, and a uniform composition is what both
  forms carry exactly; a converged pair is what would measure the difference at
  the separation those cases reach, and is named as open in
  \texttt{docs/b4\_spatial\_operator\_design\_20260906.md}. No golden was refreshed.

\item
  \textbf{WENO3-ORDER, measurement and fix} (2026-09-07): the ESWENO3
  reconstruction was SECOND order on every grid the code runs on. Probe
  \texttt{weno3\_reconstruction\_order} (physics\_probe; exact volume averages by
  8-point Gauss quadrature, face error against the point value, N = 50 to
  800): production rate 1.997 (uniform in r), 1.988 (the \texttt{define\_grid} Mixed
  production grid), 2.940 on a grid uniform in the volume coordinate, where
  the defect is invisible. Cause, isolated by variants: not the ESWENO
  nonlinear weights (S = 1 gives the same 1.997), not the floor \texttt{eps\ =\ dr\_j\^{}2} (rate 3.000 at kappa 0.1 to 10 once fixed), not the ideal ratios
  \texttt{D1}, \texttt{D2} (1/2 + O(h), harmless); TWO of the four stencil coefficients
  applied the NEIGHBOR\textquotesingle s volume share where cell j\textquotesingle s own belongs:
  \texttt{C1(j)\ =\ c/(b+c)} on the jump to j+1 at the right face and \texttt{C2(j-1)\ =\ a/(a+b)} on the jump to j-1 at the left face, each 1/2 + O(h) where
  1/2 - O(h) belongs, an O(h) coefficient error on an O(h) jump. Inherited
  character for character from ATES v2.0 (\texttt{ATES/ATES-Code-main}). Advisor
  check of the derivation: a linear interpolant of two cell averages in the
  volume coordinate, evaluated at a face dV(j)/2 from cell j\textquotesingle s center,
  weights the jump by dV(j)/(dV(j) + dV(nb)); with the corrected shares a
  profile quadratic in the volume coordinate is reproduced to 5.9e-16.
  \textbf{Fixed (advisor, two symbols in each of \texttt{Reconstruct} and
  \texttt{Reconstruct\_scalar}, \texttt{Reconstruction.f90}):} \texttt{C1(j)\ -\textgreater{}\ C2(j)} on \texttt{dWp}
  in \texttt{WL}, \texttt{C2(j-1)\ -\textgreater{}\ C1(j-1)} on \texttt{dWm} in \texttt{WR}; the probe\textquotesingle s production
  mapping and header updated, the two waiting assertions enabled:
  \texttt{weno3\_production\_third\_order} rate 3.000, \texttt{\_mixed} 2.991 (RED before:
  shortfalls 0.803, 0.812); \texttt{physics\_probe} 603/0. \textbf{Every golden whose case
  reaches the WENO3 stage moves, and not by rounding} (face error down two
  decades at N = 800); PLM-only cases are untouched. Consequence for the
  method: until now the two-stage PLM -\textgreater{} WENO3 recipe was a continuation
  between two second-order schemes with the second having the larger error
  constant (1.3x uniform, 3.8x on the production grid). Mdot values are to
  be quoted from Newton-finished runs after the gate. Attribution of the
  weight form and the floor to Yamaleev and Carpenter (2009, JCP 228, 3025
  and 4248) stays unverified: both papers are paywalled here; requested
  from the user. Also recorded: stale \texttt{.ipynb\_checkpoints} copies of
  \texttt{Apply\_BC.f90} and \texttt{UW\_conversions.f90} under \texttt{src/modules/} define the
  same module names as the live files (not built; to remove, HYG-CKPT).

  caloric\_eos, low\_mach\_dissipation, cross\_sec, UW\_conversions,

\item
  \textbf{COST2} (measurement; the only production change is the T15 hook and
  print in \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, byte-identical on \texttt{wasp\_full} 300 in both
  modes and \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} 60 phys; \texttt{attempted\_step} whole-binary rows
  now run, identity residual -1.8e-10, zero refusals): the brief\textquotesingle s premise
  is refuted twice. (1) MEASURED with T frozen through the coupled loop on
  \texttt{wasp\_full}: the composition sweep alone still takes 10-13 passes with the
  same contraction 0.15-0.20 and the same front walking one cell per pass;
  freezing the lagged coefficients as well collapses it to 2 passes;
  freezing only the radiation field leaves 3-4, only the rate coefficients
  9-13. The pass count is the self-consistency of the photoionization field
  with the composition that attenuates it; the temperature is worth at most
  one pass. (2) A timer inside \texttt{ioniz\_eq} (60 steps, 29.95 s): \texttt{PH\_heat}
  11.40 s (38 percent of the run), the whole cell loop 7.37 s (25 percent),
  pre-sweep \texttt{eval\_cool} 2.44 s, post-sweep assembly 2.40 s; skipping the
  entire cell body for resting cells saves 4.7 percent. B3c-COST\textquotesingle s gprof
  attribution (78 percent in MINPACK) was inverted; the \texttt{csm\_max\_pass}
  comment still carries it (to be rewritten by COST3). (3) The two-pass rest
  mask was implemented, measured (6.0 percent on \texttt{wasp\_full}, 0 on
  \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, state moved 5.5e-7, step 0 of \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} in
  phys turned into an exhausted attempt) and removed. \textbf{The lever: the field
  dependence is one-directional (a cell\textquotesingle s P\_HI is set by the attenuation of
  the cells outside it) and the sweep already runs outside in, so updating
  the column with the composition just produced turns the Jacobi iteration
  into a forward substitution: same fixed point, about one pass instead of
  ten, and \texttt{PH\_heat} no longer repeated per pass.} Assigned as COST3
  (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} \texttt{PH\_heat\_HHe}/\texttt{PH\_heat\_H}, \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}
  sweep order; the OpenMP parallel cell loop becomes sequential in the field
  direction, so the design must weigh 10 parallel passes against 1-2
  sequential ones and measure both at 1 and 16 threads). Also recorded: the
  pre-sweep \texttt{eval\_cool} is run for its coefficients and its cooling
  discarded, then a second traversal forms the cooling (16 percent
  together); \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} in phys refuses 7 of its first 60 steps at
  the fixed point on the unmodified tree.
\item
  \textbf{B4-1c landed: the carriers ride on the species face flux} (2026-09-07):
  the carriers of \texttt{photochemical\_transport\_step} (H2 always, OH/H2O/CO under
  \texttt{Oxygen\ chemistry}, H+ under \texttt{Ionization\ transport}) join the transported
  set of B4-1 and are advected as \texttt{F\_rho\ Y\_c/m\_c} inside the Runge-Kutta
  stages. \texttt{carrier\_set\_init} declares them once the keys are parsed
  (\texttt{advected\_carrier\_register} in \texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90}, which also
  holds the columns and hands them back through
  \texttt{advected\_carrier\_fractions}); \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} is untouched, the existing
  three calls carry both sets. The carriers ride OUTSIDE the normalized set
  (\texttt{n\_norm\ =\ 0}): a carrier is one species inside an element, the element is
  already the closing member, and normalizing it beside the element would
  count that mass twice; what keeps carrier and element consistent is the
  operator\textquotesingle s own write-back, which restores the element totals cell by cell.
  Their rows are advanced from cell 1 (the elements\textquotesingle{} start at 2), with the
  inflow composition taken from the inner ghosts: the handoff\textquotesingle s \texttt{q\_H2\_base}
  where one is stated, the base cell\textquotesingle s own partition where none is, one rule
  for both branches of decision D6 (B4-4a). \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}
  loses the cell-velocity advective term, \texttt{carrier\_advection\_correction} and
  the base Dirichlet branch from its MARCHING rows and their Jacobian; two
  paths keep the advective term, \texttt{carrier\_steady\_residual} (the stationary
  balance is the whole equation) and the fixed-wind relaxation
  \texttt{relax\_photochemical\_composition} (it takes many transport steps with no
  hydrodynamic stage between them, so nothing else is taking that transport),
  through the switch \texttt{carrier\_rows\_advect}. Tests: two rows added to
  \texttt{species\_face\_flux}, \texttt{uniform\_carrier\_partition\_preserved} 0.83 ulp (bound
  8) on a sign-alternating face mass flux and
  \texttt{h2\_nucleus\_total\_changes\_only\_by\_boundary\_flux} 1.36e-15 (bound 1e-12),
  RED before in the strongest sense (the driver does not compile against the
  pre-increment module); suite 15 + 2 GREEN; \texttt{carrier\_retry} 101,
  \texttt{carrier\_returned\_state\_acceptance} 37, \texttt{carrier\_constraint\_attribution}
  11, \texttt{carrier\_reference\_scales} 15, \texttt{acceptance\_classes} 22 GREEN with no
  reference re-derived. Impact MEASURED against a binary built from the same
  tree snapshot with the two files at their pre-increment text: 12 cases
  BYTE-IDENTICAL, every one without \texttt{Molecular\ carrier\ transport}
  (\texttt{wasp\_full}, \texttt{wasp\_he23off}, \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}, \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff},
  \texttt{mol\_metals}, \texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner}, \texttt{mol\_ir\_bands}, \texttt{mol\_sec\_ion},
  \texttt{mol\_diffusion}, \texttt{lower\_profile}, \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry},
  \texttt{hydrostatic\_column}); the four that do transport carriers move.
  \texttt{mol\_carrier} at 1000 steps: \texttt{Ion\_species} 5.0e-1 (H3+ at \texttt{r\ =\ 1.100}),
  \texttt{Cooling\_breakdown} 6.2e-1, \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz} 2.6e-2 (\texttt{v} at \texttt{r\ =\ 1.186});
  \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed}/\texttt{hp\_trace\_seed}/\texttt{hp\_front} at 100 steps: 8e-3 to 1.1e-1,
  \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz} 1.3e-3 to 1.8e-3. \textbf{The front does not move; its tail does.}
  The \texttt{x\_H2\ =\ 0.5} crossing of \texttt{mol\_carrier} is \texttt{r\ =\ 1.0698} at 1000 steps
  before and after and \texttt{x\_H2(1.05)} is 0.7308 against 0.7310, while
  \texttt{x\_H2(1.10)} falls from 3.19e-3 to 2.02e-3 (-37\%): the molecular tail the
  cell-velocity form was carrying by its own numerical diffusivity, which is
  where the design said the difference lives, and every large entry above is
  a consequence of that column. Every carrier interval covered and the
  element limiter moved no cell, in both runs. Recorded and not fixed:
  \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} has \texttt{carrier\_transport} on by default with the oxygen
  chemistry, but at 200 steps in initialization mode 201 of 201 intervals are

  was refreshed. Also in this increment: the stale clause saying the carrier
  checkpoint restores "the cumulative CO record" removed from two comments in

  default 0 = whole grid). MEASURED on \texttt{wasp\_full} 300: the substitution
  stops the front\textquotesingle s walk (worst-moving cell 206 -\textgreater{} 177 over the passes on
  the lagged scheme, 202 -\textgreater{} 196 with the columns carried) and cuts the pass
  count 10.10 -\textgreater{} 7.13, but a pass costs the same field work either way, and
  the field of a whole grid is one loop sixteen threads solve at once: block
  width 8 buys 1.43x at one thread (136 -\textgreater{} 103 s) and LOSES 2.8x at sixteen
  (17.0 -\textgreater{} 42.5 s); width 64 keeps the parallel front and saves nothing. The
  default therefore stays the whole grid, and the width is not tied to the
  thread count (the widths reach states differing by 1.5e-7 at the 1e-8
  tolerance, the thread count by 7e-14). \textbf{The remaining lag is the cell\textquotesingle s
  OWN optical depth}, which enters its own field only on the next pass
  (contraction 0.17): closing it means the cell residual carries its own
  field, \texttt{System\_HeH*}, i.e. B5. Folded: the pre-sweep \texttt{eval\_cool} run for
  its coefficients only is now \texttt{chemical\_rate\_coefficients} (-3.6 percent
  \texttt{wasp\_full}, -5.6 percent \texttt{mol\_metals}, +3.5 percent \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff},
  the last from the 27 metal slots looped with metals off: guard on
  \texttt{thereis\_metals}, HYG-COL). Recorded: \texttt{docs/openmp\_parallelization.md}
  (2.07x at 16 threads, bit-identical across threads) is stale: measured 8.2x
  and round-off 6.6e-14 (DOCS-OMP). A \texttt{cp\ -irp} of another item blocked on an
  interactive prompt since 2026-09-06 was killed by PID (23818, cwd checked).

  \textbf{WENO3-REF} (comments only, \texttt{Reconstruction.f90} and the probe\textquotesingle s
  References block; the user supplied the publisher PDFs, read with
  \texttt{pdftotext\ -layout}): the nonlinear weights are EXACTLY Yamaleev and
  Carpenter 2009, JCP 228, 3025, Eqs. (18), (20), (21), (22) (published
  \texttt{tau} already squared; \texttt{d\_0\ =\ 2/3}, \texttt{d\_1\ =\ 1/3} entering as the ratio 1/2
  that \texttt{D1}, \texttt{D2} carry); the scheme is NOT their ESWENO: the energy-stability
  dissipation of Eq. (48) is absent, their analysis is a uniform-grid
  finite-difference flux reconstruction, and for systems only in
  characteristic variables (Section 5 quoted), so the code inherits the
  accuracy result (Eq. 24) and not the energy estimate; the geometry
  (\texttt{C1,\ C2,\ D1,\ D2} on the stretched finite-volume grid) is this code\textquotesingle s own.
  \texttt{eps\ =\ dr\_j\^{}2}: a defensible stand-in, not a defect: the published h\^{}2
  scaling of Eqs. (62), (64) without the solution scale of Eq. (65);
  MEASURED on the production grid the face error is flat to every digit for
  kappa \textgreater= 0.1 over four decades (rate 2.991) and deteriorates below 1e-2
  per Eq. (57), so in smooth regions the departure is not measurable; where
  it acts is the ENO biasing (b\_r/eps spans 2.2e-6 to 8.4e3 over the
  \texttt{wasp\_full} grid; the published floor would make the base LESS nonlinear),
  a candidate experiment for the base sound-wave work, not a change here.
  Case label and comments now say "third-order WENO"; identifiers unchanged.
  Advisor: \texttt{paper/ms.tex} 144 "the energy-stable ESWENO3" reworded to the

\item
  \textbf{REF-ZHANGSHU} (comments, \texttt{docs/p49\_front\_energy\_mode.md} 6.1, probe
  \texttt{positivity\_limiter\_scaling} 15 assertions; user-supplied PDF read): the
  citation was wrong as stated: \texttt{positivity\_limited\_faces} is not the
  limiter of Zhang and Shu 2010 (JCP 229, 8918) but one built on the same
  idea, departing on three axes (primitive, not conserved, variables; one
  theta per face state, not per cell, Eqs. (2.4)-(2.5), (2.10)-(2.12); a
  relative one-ulp floor, not their absolute 1e-13). What carries over:
  Lemma 2.5 (convex combination with an admissible anchor); what does not:
  Theorem 2.1 (the next cell average admissible under their Gauss-Lobatto
  set and CFL, N = 2 covers k = 1 only; Remark 2.6 names finite-volume WENO
  as open) and conservativity of the reconstruction (a theta taken
  face by face moves the cell\textquotesingle s end-point mean by 8.3e-2 where a theta taken
  cell by cell gives 0). Along
  their conserved path p(t) is concave, so the code\textquotesingle s theta is never larger
  than their t\_eps (MEASURED 0.856 against 0.949 at Mach 60): the anticipated
  negative-internal-energy failure cannot occur. \textbf{Defect found and fixed
  (advisor applied the worker\textquotesingle s proposal):} with eps one ulp of the average
  the bare update \texttt{q\_avg\ +\ theta\ (q\_rec\ -\ q\_avg)} is a cancellation of the
  size of the floor and lands on EXACTLY ZERO for 1013 of 200000
  reconstructed values crossing zero (never negative), the value the floor
  exists to prevent (division in v = m/rho and in the sound speed);
  \texttt{positivity\_limited\_faces} now clamps the scaled density and pressure to
  eps after the update (same number in exact arithmetic). Moves only runs in
  which the limiter fired, by one ulp except where the zero would have
  divided; goldens at the gate. Probe: \texttt{bare\_update\_reaches\_zero} (the
  reason), \texttt{production\_limiter\_never\_delivers\_zero} (the production routine
  over 4000 grids of zero-crossing face states) GREEN. A theta taken cell by cell
  (Eq. 2.11), which would restore the reconstruction average, is recorded as
  a change of scheme, not applied.
\item
  \textbf{REF-LODI} (documentation, \texttt{docs/phaseC\_characteristic\_base\_bc.md}

  decision with a third option). One gap stated: the source projection
  l\_1 C (gravity and the spherical divergence, Thompson 1990 footnote 1 and
  Eq. (52); the steady form is L\_1 = -rho c g\_1, not 0) survives only
  because both states of (C-) sit at the same radius; any reformulation
  relating cell 1\textquotesingle s center to the face must restore it. Carlson\textquotesingle s "but not U
  and p" marked unverified against the primaries.

  OH(A) bound recorded; Tully 1975\textquotesingle s 2.87e-10 is a 300 K statistical-model

\item
  \textbf{REF-NEUFELD} (comments and documents; \texttt{lya\_rt.f90} machine code
  identical before/after; \texttt{physics\_probe} 1118/0): the Ly-alpha
  escape-probability closure does NOT use Neufeld 1990 or Harrington 1973.
  \texttt{beta\_esc\ =\ pi\^{}(-1/4)\ sqrt(a/tau)} is the one-flight wing escape chance
  under complete redistribution; Ly-alpha scatters with partial
  redistribution and the published static-slab damping-wing solution is
  Harrington 1973 eq. (40), \texttt{\textless{}N\textgreater{}\ =\ 0.9093161\ B} scatterings for a mid-plane
  source, so \texttt{beta\ =\ 1/\textless{}N\textgreater{}}, proportional to 1/tau and independent of a;
  MEASURED on the stored \texttt{wasp\_full} column the code exceeds it by 234x at
  the base and 14x at 1.20 Rp, crossing at 1.42 Rp. Neufeld contains no
  expression of the code\textquotesingle s form (his escaping fractions are escape against
  destruction, eq. 2.25 partitions the two faces; the code\textquotesingle s two-face
  PRODUCT is unpublished); the escape frequency scales as (a tau)\^{}(1/3)
  (Harrington eqs. 37, 47; Neufeld eq. 4.32), not the code\textquotesingle s (a tau)\^{}(1/2);
  Neufeld\textquotesingle s validity \texttt{(a\ tau)\^{}(1/3)\ \textgreater{}\ 10} holds in 320 of 504 cells (inside
  1.156 Rp); Neufeld is static, the Sobolev factor is Sobolev 1960 radial-ray.
  The header\textquotesingle s \texttt{J\_int} formula lacked the (1 - beta) factor the code applies
  (fixed). \textbf{Advisor decision: adopt the published solution (LYA-BETA,
  launched):} beta = 1/ of eq. (40) joined to the thin limit, the face
  partition of eq. 2.25 in place of the product, the (a tau)\^{}(1/3) escape
  frequency, a validity record, \texttt{A\_2p1s} 6.3e8 -\textgreater{} 6.2648e8 (spectroscopic),
  every case with \texttt{Jlya\ escape-prob:\ True} moves (expected at frozen state:
  \texttt{J\_int}/\texttt{n\_2p} up to 90x higher inside 1.02 Rp, 14x at 1.20 Rp, up to 7x
  lower beyond 1.47 Rp; Mdot to be measured to the du stop). The same
  question for the metal lines\textquotesingle{} \texttt{line\_escape\_probability\_one\_face}
  (\texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}) is LYA-BETA-METALS, after REF-METALS.
\item
  \textbf{REF-METALS} (\texttt{cross\_sec.f90}, \texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}; probes
  \texttt{metal\_photoionization\_fits}, \texttt{recombination\_coefficient\_fits}, 255
  assertions; advisor rebuild green apart from LYA-BETA\textquotesingle s in-progress probe):
  all 132 fit parameters of the seventeen metal photoionization cross
  sections match Verner et al. 1996 Table 1 (\texttt{verner\_photo/photo.dat}) digit
  for digit and \texttt{metal\_photoion\_sigma} reproduces Verner\textquotesingle s own \texttt{phfit2}
  exactly wherever the fit is the one \texttt{phfit2} uses; 220 Badnell RR and DR
  parameters (fourteen daughters; \texttt{cfout\_K} witness within 0.045 dex), the
  H/He rows and the Mao and Kaastra n = 1 rows match. \textbf{Two fixes:} the
  Mg II turn-on gated at Verner\textquotesingle s rounded 15.04 eV while the grid edge is
  the NIST 15.035 (\texttt{e\_th\_MgII} now; byte-identical on \texttt{wasp\_full}, the band
  geometric means miss the sliver); \texttt{alpha\_rr\_metal(\textquotesingle{}CaI\textquotesingle{})} used Verner
  \texttt{rrfit.f}\textquotesingle s \texttt{rrec(:,20,19)}, the row that FORMS Ca II (second index = the
  recombined ion\textquotesingle s electron count, fixed by \texttt{rnew(:,1,1)} = H+ + e and
  \texttt{rnew(:,2,2)} = He+ + e), 6.05x the Ca I row \texttt{(1.120e-13,\ 0.900)} at 1e4 K;
  corrected. Impact (worker, scratch): \texttt{wasp\_full} 300 Ca I 0.87 relative at
  its 1.196 Rp maximum, Ca II +2.8 percent at the base, T 4.3e-3, v 9.9e-3;
  \texttt{mol\_metals} 400 Ca II/III x2.5 at 1.38 Rp, cool 2.9e-2; \texttt{wasp\_full},
  \texttt{mol\_metals}, \texttt{lower\_profile} move at the gate through calcium. \textbf{Two
  physics items opened:} (a) the outer-shell fit is evaluated on the whole
  photon grid to 1240 eV while every row carries an \texttt{E\_max} (34-558 eV)
  above which \texttt{phfit2} hands over to the 1995 subshell fit and adds the
  inner shells: the code carries 0.1-12 percent of the true metal
  photoabsorption there, and Fe I\textquotesingle s row (\texttt{Q} = 1.54) RISES with energy to
  49x the truth at 1240 eV; the handover of \texttt{phfit2} itself is adopted
  (METALS-OUTER, launched); (b) the inner-shell absorption and its Auger
  cascade (an atom two or more stages up) cannot be represented by the
  three-stage metal ladder: \texttt{To\_be\_determined\_by\_user\_20260906.md} item 7.
  Also: \texttt{verner\_photo/README.md} corrected (\texttt{photo.dat} is the 1996 Table 1,
  \texttt{table1.dat} the 1995 fits); rev3 line 944 closed; \texttt{e\_th\_MgI}/\texttt{e\_th\_MgII}
  provenance lines in \texttt{parameters.f90} to be annotated (METALS-OUTER, after
  LYA-BETA releases the file).
\item
  \textbf{METALS-OUTER} (\texttt{cross\_sec.f90}; generator \texttt{src/utils/metal\_photoion\_table.py}
  from \texttt{verner\_photo/photo.dat} + \texttt{table1.dat}; probes
  \texttt{metal\_photoionization\_fits} 188 and \texttt{metal\_photoion\_table\_transcription}

  \texttt{physics\_probe} green): the seventeen \texttt{sigma\_XX} routines are one

  the 1996 outer-shell fit below \texttt{E\_max}, the 1995 fit of the same shell at
  and above it (MEASURED: \texttt{einn} of \texttt{phfit2} equals \texttt{photo.dat}\textquotesingle s \texttt{E\_max}
  for all sixteen metal rows; the production routine reproduces \texttt{phfit2}\textquotesingle s
  outer shell to 1e-10 at 17 ions x 12 energies where 110 of 204 rows
  disagreed before, worst Fe I at 1240 eV 24.9 Mb against 0.00172 Mb, a
  factor 14464). Inner shells still absent by decision item 7 (option (a)
  recommended; the measured sizes now in that item). Impact (scratch,
  metals-off \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} byte-identical): \texttt{wasp\_full} 300 v 0.15, T
  4.7e-2, heat 7.8e-2, Fe III 0.99, C III 0.60 relative (hard photons now
  reach the base, the top stages fall at small radius; metal share of the
  heating at the base 17.4 -\textgreater{} 8.5 percent); \texttt{mol\_metals} 400 v 0.56, heat
  0.42; \texttt{lower\_profile} 200 heat 8.3e-3; log10 Mdot unchanged to 0.01 dex in
  the bounded snapshots. Both Mg thresholds already agree with NIST to their
  digits (item 4 needed no move; provenance note pending \texttt{parameters.f90}).
\item
  \textbf{LYA-BETA-METALS} (measurement by the worker, fix applied by the
  advisor; \texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}, probe \texttt{fine\_structure\_escape\_probability},
  \texttt{docs/resonance\_line\_trapping.md} sections 6.1 and 10; publisher scans of
  de Jong, Boland and Dalgarno 1980 and Osterbrock 1962 fetched into
  \texttt{references/}): \texttt{line\_escape\_probability\_one\_face} is de Jong et al. 1980
  eq. (B-7), both branches to round-off, applied to the eight ground-term
  fine-structure lines only; NO metal resonance line uses an escape
  probability (Mg II h\&k, Ca II H\&K, Na I D, Fe I/II at beta = 1, justified
  in that memo\textquotesingle s sections 1-5 by \texttt{n\_crit,eff} = beta A/q \textasciitilde{} 1e10-1e15 against
  n\_e \textless= 4.3e9), and the transit post-process uses none. The fine-structure
  lines have a \textasciitilde{} 1e-8 and \texttt{a\ tau\ \textless{}=\ 1e-9}, so the Ly-alpha correction does
  not transfer. \textbf{Defect:} (B-7) is written in the frequency-integrated
  depth \texttt{sqrt(pi)\ tau\_0} (normalized profile; equal to Hollenbach and McKee
  1979 eq. (5.10) once the convention is matched, 3.4 percent at worst over
  tau = 3 to 1e6) while the caller passed the line-center column: beta too
  large by 1 (thin) to 2.11 (branch point) to 1.77 (asymptotic). \textbf{Fixed:}
  each cell\textquotesingle s depth is now \texttt{sqrt(pi)} times the line-center depth in
  \texttt{fine\_structure\_line\_transfer}. MEASURED regime on the shipped columns:
  largest line-center depth 0.14 ({[}O I{]} 63 um at the hot-Uranus base), so the
  factor there is 1.12 and the change of the total radiative losses is below
  0.1 percent (\texttt{mol\_metals} 0.096 percent), but a factor of two in any base
  thick in these lines and in the \texttt{Base\ IR\ field} incident term the same
  beta scales. \texttt{physics\_probe} 1291/0 after the fix. Metals cases move at
  the gate at that level.
\item
  \textbf{Decision item 7, literature check} (2026-09-07; the user supplied
  Locci 2022 PSJ 3, 1; Locci 2024 PSJ 5, 58; Cecchi-Pestellini 2006 A\&A
  458, L13 and 2009 A\&A 496, 863; Fox 2008 SSRv 139, 3; all in
  \texttt{references/}): the published exoplanet X-ray photochemistry models take
  the inner-shell absorption as opacity summed over all ionization channels
  (Cecchi-Pestellini 2009 Eq. 1 with Verner 1993 cross sections), put the
  photoelectron and the Auger electrons into the secondary-electron cascade
  (Locci 2022 Eq. 3), and carry singly charged ions only, i.e. option (b) of
  the item with the charge under-count accepted; no escape hydrodynamics
  code includes inner shells. Recommendation revised from (a) to (b) in
  \texttt{To\_be\_determined\_by\_user\_20260906.md}; the user decides.
\item
  \textbf{METALS-INNER} (decision 7 option (b), user 2026-09-07; \texttt{cross\_sec.f90}
  subshell block from \texttt{phfit2.f}\textquotesingle s \texttt{PH1} data (its thresholds differ from
  \texttt{table1.dat} in six rows, \texttt{phfit2} being the reference),
  \texttt{set\_energy\_vectors.f90} (every subshell turn-on of an active metal ion a
  bin edge; bin count unchanged, 17 -\textgreater{} 60 threshold cuts on \texttt{wasp\_full}),
  \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} comment only (the existing rate \texttt{sigma/e\_v} and heat
  \texttt{photoelectron\_share(E\_th)\ sigma} integrals already implement "one stage,
  one electron of h nu - E\_th(outer) to the SvS85/Dalgarno cascade" once the
  cross section is the total); generator \texttt{metal\_photoion\_table.py} \texttt{-\/-check};
  probes 392 metal assertions, \texttt{physics\_probe} 1479/0; docs
  \texttt{photoion\_cross\_sections.tex} (+PDF), \texttt{code\_status} new L row): the metal
  photoabsorption is the SHELL SUM \texttt{phfit2} returns, reproduced to 1e-10 at
  17 ions x 12 energies (130 of 204 rows disagreed before). It also closed a
  cliff METALS-OUTER\textquotesingle s handover had left at \texttt{E\_max} where the 1996 fit stood
  for a valence complex the 1995 outer row does not cover (Fe I x0.012 and
  Fe II x0.0066 steps, now 1.23 and 1.13; genuine K edges C/N/O x19-21), and
  put \texttt{E\_max}, a step of the cross section, on a bin edge (it was not).
  Approximation stated at the code site: ion advanced one stage, one
  electron of \texttt{h\ nu\ -\ E\_th(outer)} to the cascade (MEASURED with the
  degradation routine: 0.95-1.09x the heat and 1.10-1.37x the secondary H I
  ionizations of the true two-electron event; the un-spent I\_2 is 24-35 eV
  of a 300-550 eV photon), fluorescence neglected (with \texttt{e\_top} = 1240 eV
  only C, N, O, Na have K holes; the high-Z ions contribute L shells only).
  Impact (worker, same-snapshot control; metals-off \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}
  byte-identical): \texttt{wasp\_full} 300 the wind 1-3 percent hotter and faster,
  base heating +26 percent, T at most +9.5 percent, Fe III/Mg III/N III at
  the base x74-160 from vanishing values, \textbf{Mg II falls 84 percent at r =
  1.178} (the h\&k transit line); \texttt{mol\_metals} base heating +26 percent, T
  1.4e-3; \texttt{lower\_profile} heating +21 percent at the base, T 2e-5; metals\textquotesingle{}
  share of the 100-1240 eV absorption 2.2 -\textgreater{} 3.5 percent (\texttt{wasp\_full}), the
  band 0.38 percent of the whole; log10 Mdot unchanged to 0.01 dex in the
  snapshots. Krause 1979 (JPCRD 8, 307) K/L fluorescence yields could not
  be obtained (NIST 503, AIP 403): requested from the user; no yield is
  quoted anywhere.
  Advisor verification: \texttt{physics\_probe} 1479/0, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 93/0;
  \texttt{spectrum\_type} segfaulted in \texttt{loaded\_sed\_threshold\_edges} (its threshold
  array was sized \texttt{n\_mion\ +\ 16} = 43 while the active list now reaches 126),

  (\texttt{n\_thr\_max} was a local parameter of \texttt{set\_energy\_vectors}; hoisted to
  module scope, public, so every caller of \texttt{ionization\_thresholds\_active}
  sizes its array by the one bound; \texttt{spectrum\_type} 154/0, \texttt{physics\_probe}
  1479/0, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 93/0 after.)
\item
  \textbf{METALS-INNER addendum, Krause 1979 yields} (comments and documents
  only; user-supplied \texttt{Krause\_1979\_JPCRD\_8\_307.pdf}, read from page images
  where the OCR is noisy, alignment checked on omega\_K(Fe) = 0.340): Table 3
  omega\_K for C/N/O/Na 2.8e-3/5.2e-3/8.3e-3/0.023, Mg/Si/S/K/Ca/Fe
  0.030/0.050/0.078/0.140/0.163/0.340; Table 5 effective L1/L2 yields
  (Coster-Kronig included per the text, nu\_3 = omega\_3) for Mg to Fe all
  below 6.5e-3; Table 2 uncertainties (omega\_K 40-10 percent at Z 5-10,
  footnote (a) on molecules and solids). With \texttt{e\_top} = 1240 eV only C, N,
  O and Na I can hold a K hole, so the neglected radiative branch is at most
  2.3 percent of a K-hole event (under 1 percent for the three coolants) and
  under 0.7 percent of any L-shell event; raising \texttt{e\_top} past 1.3 keV (Mg,
  Si K) or 4 keV (Ca, Fe K) is the condition under which the approximation
  must be revisited, stated at the code site, in \texttt{photoion\_cross\_sections.tex}
  (new \texttt{tab:kedge}), \texttt{code\_status} and decision item 7.
\item
  \textbf{LYA-BETA landed, advisor verification} (\texttt{lya\_rt.f90}, \texttt{hydrogen\_n2\_rates.f90}
  (\texttt{A\_2p1s} 6.3e8 -\textgreater{} 6.2649e8, the nonrelativistic dipole value times the
  reduced-mass factor, = NIST ASD\textquotesingle s Wiese and Fuhr 2009 value),

  Neufeld 1990 eq. (3.27) at zero destruction = Harrington 1973 eq. (40)
  \texttt{(4\ sqrt(6)/pi\^{}2)\ u\_2\ B\ =\ 0.909316\ B} for a mid-plane source (the
  \texttt{0.9093161} of the REF-NEUFELD entry was a transcription slip), \texttt{beta\ =\ 1/(1\ +\ \textless{}N\textgreater{})} (exact at both limits: a generated photon is emitted 1 +
  times, and the budget \texttt{n2p\ A\ beta\ =\ P} wants the escape chance per
  emission), the two faces sharing one escaping population by Neufeld eq.
  (2.25) instead of the former product, \texttt{Phi(xi)} summed in closed form; the
  Voigt parameter has left the escape probability. \texttt{a} still enters only
  \texttt{lya\_wing\_penetration\_coefficient} (where an incident stellar photon
  crosses in one flight, a different question, judged at the code site).
  Defect found in passing: \texttt{beta\_tot\ =\ 1\ -\ (1-beta\_esc)(1-beta\_sob)} lost

  end-to-end identity. \textbf{Impact, \texttt{wasp\_full} to its du stop (2 h 59 m):
  log10 Mdot 13.34 -\textgreater{} 13.34 (+1.6 percent, 0.0067 dex); \texttt{Jint}/\texttt{n\_2p} x84
  at the base, x73 at 1.05, x37 at 1.10, x10 at 1.20, x0.75 at 1.50 R\_p; T
  within 2 percent; the old \texttt{min(1,.)} cap had zeroed the internal field
  from 1.15 R\_p outward.} H(n=2) chord column x31.5 at b = 1.00, x2.8 at
  1.20, x1.005 at 1.45: \textbf{every H-alpha/H-beta transit number in this
  repository from a \texttt{Jlya\ escape-prob:\ True} run is superseded} (He 10830
  not affected). Validity \texttt{(a\ tau)\^{}(1/3)\ \textgreater{}\ 10} holds in 349 of 504 cells,
  out to 1.193 R\_p, exactly where the change is an order of magnitude.
  Advisor follow-ups applied: the second \texttt{A\_2p1s} in \texttt{exhale\_transit\_lib.py}

  \textbf{THREAD-DET} (advisor, 2026-09-07; \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}): the thread count

  \texttt{\_ZGVbN2vv\_pow} (gfortran -O3 vectorizes the rate-coefficient loops), and
  the vector variants agree with the scalar libm to one ulp, not bitwise;
  inside a block of cells the loop takes lane pairs from \texttt{j\_lo} and an odd
  remainder by the scalar call, so where a block STARTS decides which cell
  meets which variant. \texttt{chemical\_rate\_coefficients} and \texttt{eval\_cool} cut the
  grid into \texttt{2*omp\_get\_max\_threads()} blocks, so the block boundaries, and
  with them the last bit of every rate, moved with the thread count (the
  6.6e-14 of COST3/HYG-MAIN). Fixed: both sweeps use blocks of the fixed
  even length \texttt{xuv\_rate\_block\ =\ 32} (16 blocks on the 504-cell grids); the

  \textbf{B5c} (\texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90}; suite \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 60/0;

  takes both halves of the helium row from \texttt{composition\_residual} and the

  itself solves, so the fixed point is the row\textquotesingle s zero by construction;
  \texttt{solve\_trace\_element\_in\_hydrogen} is a deferred-correction loop that takes
  one pass, term for term the old direct solve, when the advection is off
  (the marching path). MEASURED on a synthetic column relaxed to its own
  convergence: the row reads 8.65e-13 (He alone), 8.45e-13 / 5.38e-13 (He +
  ten trace elements) of its terms, the advection carrying 0.993 of them;
  the replaced form leaves 6.4e-3 on the same state. The smoothed-flux
  rationale does not survive: it repaired a form (the element equation minus
  X times the mass equation) that is gone, and its consequence is now a
  measured property of the equation: on a face mass flux that does not
  conserve mass the conservative balance has no bounded steady composition
  (X driven onto 0 and 1). Marching path byte-identical (\texttt{mol\_diffusion},

  byte-identical, and its outer pass never reaches the relaxation (the solve

\item
  \textbf{HYG-T9} (advisor, 2026-09-08; \texttt{src/tests/diffusion\_tests.f90}): T9\textquotesingle s
  ambipolar-limit bound sat exactly on the arithmetic\textquotesingle s noise floor, so the
  suite passed at -O3 and failed at -O1 with checks (DIFT-LINK\textquotesingle s finding).
  MEASURED at both levels: the neutral limit is exact (no electron term),
  the H+ plasma reads 8.207e-13 (-O3) and 7.740e-13 (-O1), the He++ plasma
  7.083e-13 and 1.098e-12. Mechanism, written at the site: the limits are
  exact algebra in the masses, so the deviation is arithmetic, and the
  ambipolar term of the plasma limits comes from a centered difference of
  \texttt{ln\ rho} over \texttt{dr} whose logarithm is O(b0/r), so each ulp reaches
  \texttt{dm\_eff} amplified by 1/dr. The bound is now one decade above the largest
  measured (1e-11), the anchoring rule the certification tolerances use, and
  the test still catches a wrong ambipolar limit by ten decades (a wrong
  limit misses its target by 0.1 to 1) with the neutral limit still asserted
  exact. 35/0 at -O3 and at -O1 -fcheck.
\item
  \textbf{B5d} (\texttt{steady\_newton.f90} only; suite \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 77 rows;

  expected the element rows to fail because an element unknown moves the

  REFUTED}: the scaled Jacobian column of an element unknown carries at
  most 6.3 percent of its 2-norm outside the band, the same order as a
  hydrodynamic column (mass 0.0018, momentum and energy 0.0000), so the
  diagonal correction, the rank-one column-integral update and the identity
  block the brief offered would each have repaired a defect that is not
  there; none was written. What does break the model, located by the same
  measurements and fixed: (1) the base element row was structurally EMPTY,
  cell 1 being the Dirichlet reservoir of the element operator, so the
  banded model was singular by construction (its LU pivot grew to 8.413e19
  against a largest band entry of 5.229e3; now 1.433e6) and it now carries
  the boundary condition the operator states; (2) the element ROW SCALE
  collapses on the abandoned iterate (helium rows spanning 1.277e-29 to
  2.360, so an ordinary \texttt{dF/dY\ =\ 46.7} became a scaled band entry of
  4.890e22 and \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}grad\ merit\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 1.104e44}), and the row scale is now
  floored at the flow-time rate of the quantity the row transports, the
  hydrodynamic convention with no chosen constant. Result: the same case
  reaches \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 1.723} against 1.974 at outer iteration 18 instead of 8
  with \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}grad\ merit\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 4.813e3}, 40 decades lower and a number the model\textquotesingle s
  own entries can produce. \textbf{The element solve still does not converge and

  \texttt{mol\_diffusion} 300 steps byte-identical; \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}

\item
  \textbf{COST4} (measurement; NOTHING in the repository changed by the item, its
  four candidate files byte-identical to the snapshot it took; every
  experiment a private binary in a scratch tree whose no-switch build is
  byte-identical to the control): \textbf{the coupled loop\textquotesingle s pass count is a
  ladder of nested modes and equals the SLOWEST of them, not their sum.}
  MEASURED on \texttt{wasp\_full} 300, one thread, removing one composition coupling
  at a time: the production loop 10.09 passes; the stellar field\textquotesingle s
  dependence on the composition removed, 7.16; the temperature removed as
  well (the energy solve replaced by its own converged answer), 4.94; the He
  recombination rates too, \textbf{2.00} (which reproduces, with a different
  probe, the 2 passes COST2 measured with every composition-dependent input
  frozen). Both hypotheses of the brief are refuted: the two diffuse
  couplings are worth at most 0.08 of the 10.09 (Balmer off 10.09, He rec
  off 10.01, both off 10.01, He rec frozen after pass 1 10.01, and on
  \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} all three read the control\textquotesingle s 7.19 to the last digit),
  and \textbf{the Balmer continuum is not a lag at all}: \texttt{excited\_H\_update} fills
  its arrays once BEFORE the loop, so they are constants of it, and
  refreshing it every pass costs 1.5 percent of wall for +0.01 passes. The
  stellar lag closed IN FULL for the first time (a one-cell block and eight
  self-field passes): 8.18 passes for 218 s against 10.09 for 139 s, the
  next rung, with freezing the field entirely giving 7.16. The temperature
  is not the residual either: the composition sweep alone, at the
  temperature the step converged to, takes 10.94 passes (6.32 against 7.19
  on \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}). \textbf{Decision: change nothing} (no loop-internal
  Balmer refresh, no self-consistent He recombination coupling, no default
  moved); to go below seven passes every rung must come out at once.
  Advisor: the three statements this refutes are corrected in place, the
  \texttt{csm\_max\_pass} comment of \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, the FIELD-SELF entry above
  (marked superseded) and open item 1 of the 2026-09-08 handoff. \textbf{The one
  remainder, now open item 1}: on \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} the ladder is flat
  (freezing the field RAISES the count 7.19 -\textgreater{} 7.41) and everything the item
  could reach accounts for 0.6 of its 7.19 passes; the named candidate, READ
  and not measured, is \texttt{n\_tot}, the third-body density of the three-body
  molecular reactions, built by \texttt{calc\_ntot} from the ENTRY composition, and
  with it the H2 vibrational quench fraction and bound energy. Also
  recorded: \texttt{xuv\_field\_block\_cells} and \texttt{xuv\_self\_field\_passes} are
  compile-time parameters, so measuring the two together needs a rebuild;
  \texttt{heating\_of\_composition} calls \texttt{he\_rec\_coupling} a second time whole-grid
  every pass for its heating alone (both calls together bounded at 1.7
  percent of wall); on \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} the He recombination coupling is
  what keeps step 0 inside the pass cap.
\item
  \textbf{COST5} (measurement; NOTHING in the repository changed by the item, its
  four candidate files byte-identical to its own snapshot; probe binaries
  byte-identical to the control at their default): the molecular remainder
  COST4 left is named, quantified, and the ladder reaches its bottom rung.
  \textbf{The count is arithmetic, and the law is measured and then tested}:
  \texttt{passes\ =\ 2\ +\ log10(the\ step\textquotesingle{}s\ own\ excursion\ /\ the\ stopping\ tolerance)\ /\ (decades\ per\ pass)}. On \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} the composition contracts by
  0.171 a pass and the temperature by 0.072 (0.77 decades a pass) from an
  excursion of 7.0e-5 to a tolerance of 1e-8: 7.00 predicted, 6.93 MEASURED;
  on \texttt{wasp\_full} 0.222 and 0.199 from an excursion seven times larger, 9.2
  predicted, 10.09 measured (so the molecular cases are the cheap ones, not
  the pathological ones). Tested, not fitted: the law says the count depends
  on the stopping tolerance through one logarithm at 1.30 passes per decade,
  and moving both tolerances together gives 8.06 / 6.96 / 5.68 / 4.04 at
  1e-9 / 1e-8 / 1e-7 / 1e-6, i.e. 1.34 over three decades; and it is not the
  cell solve, six decades of hybrd1 tolerance leaving the count at 6.97 for
  up to 1.99x the wall. Of the four candidates only the named one is an
  iterate: \texttt{n\_tot}, the third body of R12/R13/R15 (the ONLY composition
  dependence of the molecular network outside the field: of seventeen
  hoisted coefficients exactly two see it, both linearly). Holding it:
  \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} 7.19 -\textgreater{} 6.03 at 1500 steps, \texttt{mol\_metals} 6.99 -\textgreater{} 6.01;
  it limits the composition mode\textquotesingle s RATE (0.171 -\textgreater{} 0.073) but the count falls
  only 6.93 -\textgreater{} 6.11 because the temperature mode then binds. The other three
  are entry state through the operator-split field (vibrational pair 6.96,
  electron density 6.94, Lyman-Werner 6.96, against 6.96), and the metal
  element totals are invariant across a sweep by construction (6.99 against
  6.99, exact). With every input AND the temperature held the sweep needs
  \textbf{2.01 passes}, reproducing COST2\textquotesingle s and COST4\textquotesingle s atomic bottom rung on the
  molecular path. \textbf{Decision: nothing closed.} The mode that sets the count
  is the temperature/composition alternation (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90},
  \texttt{energy\_semi\_implicit.f90}, not that item\textquotesingle s files); closing the \texttt{n\_tot}
  lag exactly needs two sites in \texttt{System\_HeH\_mol.f90} (also not its), and
  the in-file alternative costs a second cell solve per pass, which the
  cell-tolerance scan bounds at more than the 0.80 passes it returns. The
  production treatment is not wrong: at the fixed point the entry and
  returned compositions are one state, so the third body the network is
  solved with is the third body of the accepted state, to \texttt{csm\_comp\_tol}.
  Advisor: the law is now in the \texttt{csm\_max\_pass} comment; the misleading
  indentation of \texttt{composition.f90}\textquotesingle s \texttt{calc\_ntot} branches fixed. Recorded:
  the loop\textquotesingle s stopping test compares an ABSOLUTE mass-fraction change against
  \texttt{csm\_comp\_tol}, so it is a relative 1e-8 on H2 and no constraint on a
  1e-12 species. \textbf{The one lever that keeps both the fixed point and the
  tolerance is sequence extrapolation} on the (T, c) pair after the third
  pass (the decay is geometric with a ratio stable from pass 2 and close
  across cases): ESTIMATED at three passes of seven on the molecular cases
  and four of ten on \texttt{wasp\_full}, item COST6.
\item
  \textbf{HYG-4} (advisor, direct; the four hygiene items of the 2026-09-08
  handoff): (i) \texttt{he\_ground\_singlet\_density}\textquotesingle s run-wide clamp counter is
  incremented with \texttt{!\$omp\ atomic\ update} inside the function, so the named
  critical section around its one call inside a parallel region
  (\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}, self-consistent field loop) is gone and the
  three comments that justified serial column formation by the counter are
  reworded (the columns are still formed before the blocks are solved, for
  the ordering, not for the counter); (ii) \texttt{element\_transport\_residual} no
  longer takes the cell velocity it never read (four call sites:
  \texttt{certification.f90}, \texttt{steady\_newton.f90}, two test rows), the balance
  being the divergence of the FACE mass flux handed in; (iii) the outer
  Picard loop of \texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion} halved the element
  under-relaxation \texttt{omega} whenever \texttt{comp\_drift} failed to fall, but
  \texttt{comp\_drift} is the worse of the element drift and the carrier drift while
  \texttt{omega} damps the element relaxation alone (the carrier pass is bounded by
  \texttt{carrier\_trust}), so a carrier drift that failed to fall slowed the
  element alternation for nothing; the damping test now reads the element
  relaxation\textquotesingle s own drift (\texttt{elem\_drift}) and the exit test keeps the worst of
  the two. No regression case runs both \texttt{He\_diffusion} and the carrier
  transport, so the matrix cannot move. (iv) \texttt{f\_vib\_quench}/\texttt{e\_vib\_bound}
  staying at the entry composition inside the self-field loop is CLOSED BY
  MEASUREMENT (COST5): they are entry state through the operator-split
  field, and holding them at the first-pass value is worth 0.00 passes
  (6.96 against 6.96). Suites: \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 74/0, \texttt{certification}
  29/0, \texttt{diffusion\_tests} 35/0.
\item
  \textbf{B5e} (steady solve globalization; \texttt{steady\_newton.f90}, the
  \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} suite, design doc section 17, contract section
  11.5): \textbf{the first stationary solve with a species row that meets its own
  target.} MEASURED on \texttt{mol\_diffusion} reloaded with \texttt{Coupled\ carrier\ solve:\ True} (one element row, \texttt{nvar\ =\ 4}): the scaled trust region takes \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}}
  from 1.986 to \textbf{4.345e-09} against a target of 1e-08 in 66 outer
  iterations (54 accepted), where the pseudo-transient line search aborts at
  iteration 18 on "no descent direction exists for the banded model" at
  1.702. Two defects were named by instrumenting the eleven exits of
  \texttt{trust\_region\_step} and repaired where the measurement pointed: (i) the
  radius collapse was the CAUCHY LEG ASCENDING the model, because its
  gradient is the transpose of the BANDED \texttt{A} and has no sign (MEASURED
  \texttt{r0\ .\ A\ g\ =\ -2.1e13}, \texttt{-2.2e19} at the collapsing iterates, \texttt{pred}
  negative and exactly linear in the radius); where \texttt{r0\ .\ A\ g\ \textless{}=\ 0} the leg
  is now dropped and the dogleg reduces to the Krylov leg cut to the ball,
  which always predicts a reduction (GMRES gives \texttt{r0\ .\ A\ sN\ \textless{}\ -\textbar{}\textbar{}A\ sN\textbar{}\textbar{}\^{}2/2});
  and \texttt{pred} is formed as \texttt{-(r0\ .\ As)\ -\ \textbar{}\textbar{}As\textbar{}\textbar{}\^{}2/2} instead of the
  cancelling difference of two squared norms, whose sign on a short step was
  rounding; (ii) the best-iterate ledger ranked on \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} (three
  hydrodynamic rows) while the state is judged on those AND every species
  row, so it could hand back the iterate with the worse element balance; it
  now ranks on \texttt{distance\_from\_certification}, the largest of the judged row
  measures over their tolerances, formed once inside \texttt{eval\_residual} under
  an optional argument; the three-unknown branch keeps its comparison
  character for character. \textbf{Refused by measurement}: bounding the STEP by
  the judged rows (implemented in all three step controls) stalled the
  element solve at \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 1.92} on 13 refusals; and the banded-gradient

  same solve crawling at 1.8e-4 after 81 iterations against 4.3e-9 in 66
  with the original length, so the original stays, recorded at the site with
  both numbers. \textbf{Default moved}: \texttt{use\_tr\ =\ (nspec\_row\ \textgreater{}\ 0)}, the trust
  region is the step control of the coupled route (\texttt{EXHALE\_TRUST\_REGION=0}
  restores the line search for measurement); no regression case sets
  \texttt{Coupled\ carrier\ solve:\ True}, so no golden can move. \textbf{Anchor}: the

  want a ladder (the same case at \texttt{Resid\ tol} 1e-9 and 1e-10, not run) to

  certified: its cell-1 hydrodynamic mass row reads 6.2e-11 against 3e-12, a

  \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} byte-identical (it 6, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 8.131e-09}, info 0,

\item
  \textbf{COST6} (extrapolation of the coupled loop\textquotesingle s geometric decay;
  \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} +532 lines, \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} +3 whole-binary rows;
  \textbf{default OFF}, \texttt{EXHALE\_CSM\_EXTRAP=1} turns it on): implemented, guarded,
  measured. The object is the GLOBAL pair (T, f\_sp), which is exactly the
  loop\textquotesingle s iterate (the energy solve rewrites \texttt{u(3,:)} and \texttt{W(3,:)} in full
  from T and the entry anchor each pass, so an undo of the pair is exact,
  and the guard-forced-refusal row reproduces the off run byte for byte);
  one Rayleigh-quotient ratio per block over all cells and species, the
  projection onto the dominant mode COST5 measured stable, and one damped
  ray, so no cell can move in a direction the global mode lacks. That is how
  it differs from COST2\textquotesingle s Aitken taken cell by cell (12.00 against 11.05 passes): a
  ratio taken cell by cell spreads 200 percent across the grid and cannot be
  tested for validity; the global one is tested (\texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}d\_k\ -\ theta\ d\_\{k-1\}\textbar{}\textbar{}\ \textless{}=\ 0.5\ \textbar{}\textbar{}d\_k\textbar{}\textbar{}}) before use. Admissibility is by construction (fractions in
  {[}0,1{]}, He 2\^{}3S inside its He I column, T inside the energy solve\textquotesingle s own
  bracket, all linear in the step, so the largest admissible multiple is
  taken); MEASURED, the worst violation is identically zero on every case at
  300 and 1500 steps, 1 and 16 threads, and the step was never damped below
  0.95. The stopping test and its tolerances are untouched: a candidate is
  kept only if the pass taken FROM it meets them. Three things the
  measurement taught: the mode ALTERNATES (cosine of consecutive increments
  -0.85 to -1.00 on \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, +0.71 to +0.98 on \texttt{wasp\_full}; the
  first implementation demanded theta \textgreater{} 0 and refused 1188 of 1188
  candidates); the one-mode model does not reach back into early passes
  (the ratio drifts 0.77, 0.47, 0.31, 0.22, 0.17, 0.14, 0.11 over one
  \texttt{wasp\_full} step), hence a REACH guard, take the limit only where the
  modelled remaining error is already inside the tolerance ball (reach scan
  6.61 / 6.15 / 6.36 / 7.81 / 7.74 / 7.75 at 1/2/3/10/30/100, control 6.96);
  and undoing a mediocre candidate costs more than it saves (7.29 strict
  against 6.94 lenient, control 6.99 on \texttt{mol\_metals}). MEASURED, sweeps
  control -\textgreater{} on: \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} 1500 10780 -\textgreater{} 10102 (\textbf{-6.3 percent},
  matched pair one binary 285.4 -\textgreater{} 268.0 s), \texttt{mol\_carrier} 300 -2.3,
  \texttt{mol\_metals} 400 -0.75, \texttt{wasp\_full} 300 -0.63, \texttt{hydrostatic\_column} 0;
  state movement 8.3e-8 to 5.0e-7 relative, log10 Mdot unchanged. \textbf{The
  brief\textquotesingle s estimate of three passes of seven is NOT reachable, and the reason
  is the item\textquotesingle s real finding}: the stopping test measures the INCREMENT,
  and the error is \textbar theta/(1-theta)\textbar{} times it, one to two decades smaller,
  so the loop is one to two decades stricter than its tolerances say and
  spends 1.0 to 1.6 passes on that; the extrapolation\textquotesingle s ceiling is the last
  factor 1/\textbar theta\textbar. Redefining the test on the estimated error is a change
  of what the loop returns and is put to the user as decision item 8 of
  \texttt{docs/To\_be\_determined\_by\_user\_20260906.md} (recommendation (b)). Tests:
  \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} RED 2 rows against the control, GREEN 39/0
  (\texttt{extrapolation\_lowers\_the\_pass\_count} 6.09 against 6.96 at 300 steps;
  \texttt{refused\_extrapolation\_costs\_nothing} identical at 6.80;
  \texttt{mean\_passes\_within\_bound} re-measured 6.80, unchanged); \texttt{physics\_probe}
  1491/0. Worker-introduced defect fixed inside the item: a ledger \texttt{write}
  with six integer descriptors for seven items. Recorded, not fixed:
  \texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90} does not echo the switch; \texttt{n\_csm\_x\_kept} can only
  count candidates the stopping test did not meet and reads 0 everywhere
  (the number that matters, met on the very next pass, is printed beside
  it); the \texttt{csm\_max\_pass} comment block is now some 60 lines of measured
  tables and belongs in a \texttt{docs/} memo.
  Advisor: the measured tables and the item history that had grown to some
  230 comment lines around the coupled-step constants are moved to
  \texttt{docs/coupled\_source\_loop\_cost.md} (sections 1-7, decision item 8 linked);
  the code site keeps each constant\textquotesingle s conclusion and a section pointer. The
  setup report does not echo environment switches (none of them, by
  design), so the switch is reported by the run counter line alone. Advisor
  verification: \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} 39/0 and \texttt{physics\_probe} 1491/0 on the
  advisor build; default-off byte-identity against the GOLDENS at matrix
  length (which checks B5e, HYG-4 and COST6-off together):
  \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} 12000 steps and \texttt{wasp\_full} to its own stop, both
  \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt} and \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} IDENTICAL to the goldens (both
  runs end with status 2, "not certified", exactly as the golden runs do).
\item
  \textbf{COST7} (decision item 8, option (b), user 2026-09-08; \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90},

  7-8): \textbf{the coupled source loop stops on the ESTIMATED REMAINING ERROR of
  the pair (T, composition), not on the last increment.} The estimate is
  \textbar theta/(1 - theta)\textbar{} times this pass\textquotesingle s increment in each of the two norms
  the test uses, theta the global Rayleigh-quotient ratio, formed in ONE

\item
  \textbf{ADV-STATIC} (\texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90}, new suite \texttt{adv\_static\_limit},
  \texttt{docs/postprocess\_advection\_validity.md}): \textbf{the brief\textquotesingle s premise is
  refuted and the 0.78 K of \texttt{hydrostatic\_column}\textquotesingle s \texttt{\_adv} file is named.}
  MEASURED: the flow where it sits is Mach 0.23 to 0.82 (the case is a
  300-step relaxation snapshot, not a static column: \texttt{rho\ v\ r\^{}2} spans seven
  decades); the IONIZATION correction is held at equilibrium in 503 of 503
  cells (\texttt{Ion\_species\_adv.txt} equals \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} to the bit) and its
  Damkohler condition already gives the continuous v -\textgreater{} 0 limit the brief
  asked for, so nothing there was touched. The number is the exact solution
  of the equation the \texttt{\_adv} ENERGY solve poses: the steady internal-energy
  equation with the enthalpy flux of the mass-flux divergence DROPPED,
  \texttt{rho\ v\ de/dr\ -\ p\ v\ dln(rho)/dr\ =\ heat\ -\ cool}, which equals the exact
  \texttt{...\ +\ h\ div(rho\ v)\ =\ heat\ -\ cool} only where \texttt{rho\ v\ r\^{}2} is stationary;
  with the radiation off it reduces to the discrete adiabat \texttt{T\_j/T\_\{j-1\}\ =\ rho\_j/{[}rho\_j\ -\ (gamma-1)(rho\_j\ -\ rho\_\{j-1\}){]}}, and integrating that on the
  run\textquotesingle s density reproduces the reported temperature to 6e-11 over the 347
  cells before the first fallback (1138 K -\textgreater{} 0.78 K at 1.396 R\_p; the
  dropped term exceeds the kept ones in 480 of 502 cells). With heat/cool
  negligible the equation is homogeneous in v, so no v -\textgreater{} 0 condition can
  change its answer (99.96 percent departure at \textbar v\textbar{} \textless= 1.4e-3 c\_s). Landed:
  a validity condition of the energy solve\textquotesingle s own, the thermal Damkohler
  number \texttt{Da\ =\ t\_cross\ \textbar{}heat\ -\ cool\textbar{}\ /\ u\_th} (new pure
  \texttt{thermal\_damkohler\_number}; above one the cell keeps the run\textquotesingle s own T),
  the counterpart of ionization condition (ii) that the bare \texttt{v\ \textless{}=\ 0} sign
  test lacked; the stationary-mass-flux assumption stated at the site with
  its exact dropped term and MEASURED at run time (largest ratio, where, how
  many cells exceed one; nothing acts on it yet); the first \texttt{eval\_cool}
  writes a named \texttt{tcool\_in} instead of a discard slot; the stale "is not
  evaluated" sentence of the validity memo rewritten with both tables.
  Impact: byte-identical everywhere measured (\texttt{hydrostatic\_column} full case
  all four files; \texttt{wasp\_full}, \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, \texttt{lower\_profile} post-
  processed from the same golden state by both binaries, all four files;
  max thermal Da over tested cells 0.951 / 0.534 / 0.055 / 3e-19, 0 cells
  above one anywhere). Suite RED/GREEN (rows 1-7 cannot link before; row D
  \texttt{measured=silent} before), advisor 10/0. Also MEASURED: no matrix case has
  a stationary mass flux by the cell-to-cell measure (\texttt{rho\ v\ r\^{}2} over
  outflowing cells spans 1.27x its median in \texttt{wasp\_full}, 2.2x in
  \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, 83x in \texttt{lower\_profile}); the \texttt{du} stop is taken on a
  restricted window and does not bound this. \textbf{Follow-up decided by the
  advisor under the physical-correctness rule: restore the dropped term
  (item ADV-ENERGY)}, since an energy equation that is exact only for a
  stationary mass flux is wrong on every snapshot and at the 1e-3 level on
  every converged wind; MEASURED worth on the static column 569 K at 1.40
  R\_p and 121 K at 2.96 R\_p in place of 0.78 K and 6.0 K. The alternative,
  refusing the column where the assumption fails, would rewrite 162 of 502
  \texttt{\_adv} temperatures of \texttt{wasp\_full} (median 5 percent) and is decision 14
  (T8.1) of the target-system document, left to the user. Advisor fixed the
  stale provenance paragraph of the case README.
\item
  \textbf{ADV-ENERGY} (\texttt{T\_equation.f90}, \texttt{ion\_cell\_state.f90},
  \texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90}, \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} +7 rows,
  \texttt{docs/postprocess\_advection\_validity.md}): the advection post-process\textquotesingle s
  energy equation is now the EXACT steady internal-energy equation. The
  enthalpy flux of the mass-flux divergence, \texttt{h\ div(rho\ v)} with \texttt{h\ =\ e\ +\ p/rho}, enters \texttt{T\_equation} through a new zero-default field of the cell
  record, \texttt{teq\_cell\%div\_rhov}, as a separate additive term in both branches
  (caloric \texttt{mum\ dr\ div\_rhov\ (E(x\_H2,T)\ +\ T)}, monatomic \texttt{gamma\ mum\ dr\ div\_rhov\ T}; \texttt{mum}, \texttt{mup}, \texttt{coeff} untouched; zero field an exact zero,
  tested to the bit). \texttt{div(rho\ v)} is the mass row\textquotesingle s own operator \texttt{(A\_p\ F\_p\ -\ A\_m\ F\_m)/dV} over the control volume between the two points the upwind
  difference uses. MEASURED corrections to the briefs: \texttt{T\_equation} has no
  caller outside \texttt{post\_process\_adv} (no marching solve to protect; the
  bitwise property holds anyway); and ADV-STATIC\textquotesingle s 569 K prediction had put
  back \texttt{p\ div(v)} alone, not \texttt{e\ div(rho\ v)}; the whole term gives \textbf{1.7e7 K
  at 1.40 R\_p and 1.6e9 K at 2.96 R\_p} on the static-column snapshot, the
  closed form \texttt{w\ \textasciitilde{}\ rho\^{}(gamma-1)\ F\^{}(-gamma)} with \texttt{F\ =\ rho\ v\ r\^{}2} falling 8
  percent from cell to cell. Tests RED (nine "not a member" compile errors
  before) / GREEN (closed-form solution \texttt{T\ \textasciitilde{}\ r\^{}(-gamma)} on a diverging
  column to 6.9e-4 at 400 cells, first-order convergence 5.59e-3 / 2.79e-3 /
  1.39e-3 / 6.95e-4, the adiabat departing by 2.17); advisor
  \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} 17/0, \texttt{physics\_probe} 1491/0. \textbf{Impact, MEASURED, same
  golden state post-processed by both binaries}: marching files bitwise in
  every case; T\_adv movement \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} 1.4 percent (the only
  Newton-converged state, He 2\^{}3S 1.9 percent), \texttt{wasp\_full} (du stop) 16

  percent, \texttt{lower\_profile} snapshot 456 percent (He 2\^{}3S 238x),

  kept ones: \texttt{wasp\_full} 67x at 1.033 R\_p in 161 of 502 cells,

  \texttt{\_adv} temperature of a state that violates continuity is now unbounded

  to output there is decision 14 of the target-system document, now put to
  the user as decision item 9 with a recommendation. Goldens: every \texttt{\_adv}
  file moves by design; refresh deferred by the user to this item\textquotesingle s gate.

  turn the transport on for it (\texttt{input\_read.f90}, not the item\textquotesingle s).

  the Krylov action probe a RELATIVE change), the region asymmetric in the
  density (\texttt{n\ -\textgreater{}\ n\ exp(+-Delta)}), and \texttt{ln\ 0}. \textbf{A real finding at the

  its controls (allocated inside \texttt{carrier\_steady\_residual}, first reached
  by the opening \texttt{eval\_residual}), so the first implementation silently
  fell back to the density unknown on every coupled run; the opening state is

\item
  \textbf{B5l} (\texttt{steady\_newton.f90} +392/-82; \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}
  +51/-6, the headroom accessor only; \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} suite +161 and
  +108; \texttt{docs/steady\_solver\_design.md} section 21; \texttt{certification.f90},
  \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, \texttt{steady\_residual.f90}, \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90},
  \texttt{input\_read.f90} untouched): \textbf{the element budget is a face of the unknown
  box and the projection treats it exactly.} \texttt{freeze\_species\_unknown\_box}
  is now the one place the admissible set of a species unknown is written
  down (one lower and one upper bound per unknown of the flat vector,
  formed once per outer iteration from the iterate and from the element
  budget the carrier operator froze at that iterate); the three routines
  that had each computed their own bounds
  (\texttt{species\_unknowns\_outside\_their\_bounds}, \texttt{fix\_active\_species\_bounds},
  \texttt{largest\_step\_inside\_the\_species\_box}) read it. \textbf{A drift between the
  three was found and fixed on the way}: the projection had read the
  carrier ceiling from the trial\textquotesingle s own density slot while the other two
  read it from the iterate, so the set a trial was written onto and the set
  the Krylov cycle was sampled under were not the same set. Faces of a
  carrier unknown: below, zero (\texttt{carrier\_log\_floor} in \texttt{ln\ n}); above, the
  cell density and the element budget (\texttt{carrier\_headroom}, for H2 half the
  hydrogen the carriers may take), the tighter of the two. The budget is
  frozen for the step, not evaluated at the trial, which is the carrier
  operator\textquotesingle s own choice (\texttt{headroom\_car} assigned under \texttt{weno\_mode\ ==\ 1},
  the one evaluation that IS the iterate; refreshing it per trial was tried
  there and is worse), so box and screen describe one state. The budget
  face is raised to the iterate where the iterate stands outside it
  (counted, \texttt{n\_budget\_face\_at\_the\_iterate}; 0 on both coupled cases with
  the default step control); it is stored one floating-point step inside
  the budget (\texttt{budget\_face\_inside\ =\ 1\ -\ 8\ eps}, representability in \texttt{ln\ n},
  not a margin). An unknown whose box is a point (zero budget) is held
  without asking the gradient. \texttt{EXHALE\_SPECIES\_BUDGET\_FACE=0} restores the
  entry text\textquotesingle s box (measurement switch, default on) {[}retired by N4b: the
  budget became a constraint row, \texttt{EXHALE\_ELEMENT\_CONSTRAINT\_ROWS=0} is the
  measurement switch now{]}. \texttt{pgmres} reports the
  relative residual it REACHED against the tolerance asked; \texttt{EXHALE\_GM\_M}
  overrides \texttt{gm\_m} (the caller\textquotesingle s 40 stays the default and the literal at
  \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} line 5343 is untouched). \textbf{MEASURED on \texttt{mol\_carrier}
  (coupled, \texttt{ln\ n}), face off vs on: 66 vs 52 outer iterations, both ended
  by the stall detector; \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} handed back 0.9957 vs 1.174; trials halved
  3 or more times 38 of 68 vs 0 of 53; residual samples refused by the
  element budget 157 vs 0; trials written onto a face 0 vs 29; adopted
  carriers above their own budget 0 vs 0 (largest ratio 0.9886 / 0.9878);
  iterations ending "the model promises no reduction" 0 vs 19; worst Krylov
  relative residual reached 0.938 vs 0.569 where 0.1 was asked; \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 10.63 both.} The halvings and refusals against the budget are gone
  exactly; \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} does not improve; the limit moved to a named place, the
  Krylov leg of the dogleg. \textbf{The Krylov budget is not the limit}: \texttt{gm\_m}
  40 / 80 / 160 give \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1.174 / 1.103 / 1.271, worst relative residual
  reached 0.569 / 0.630 / 0.614, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}s\textbar{}\textbar{}} of the last accepted steps
  0.04-0.24 in all three, \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 10.63-10.64; the cycle spends every
  product and stops at the same 0.6 of its right-hand side, so it stagnates
  on this operator (preconditioner or operator, not subspace). On
  \texttt{mol\_diffusion} (H2 carried) the face does the same to the step control
  (halved 6-11 times in 50 of 50 vs 0 of 41; budget refusals 388 vs 0) and
  the solve stalls where it always has (H2 front, cell 212, element row at
  1.000 of its scale; \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1.982 vs 1.943; \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 7.97 both), with
  the probe showing instead 419 backward-side products, 26 unknowns held on
  a bound, 12 Cauchy-only steps and 18 truncated cycles. A defect in the
  item\textquotesingle s own first measurement was found and fixed (the adopted carrier
  was recorded against the PREVIOUS iterate\textquotesingle s budget, which had reported 67
  adopted carriers above budget, worst 1.058; the call now follows the
  iterate\textquotesingle s own residual evaluation, arithmetic unchanged iteration for
  iteration). \textbf{Gates}: \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} bitwise against the control
  binary (\texttt{Ion\_species.txt} bitwise, \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt} identical but for
  the provenance line, every \texttt{(JFNK)} line identical, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 4.299E-09},
  \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30, 13 iterations); marching bitwise on \texttt{mol\_carrier} 200
  steps; \texttt{EXHALE\_SPECIES\_BUDGET\_FACE=0} reproduces the entry-text binary
  exactly; \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 118/0 with the new rows RED without the
  face and GREEN with it; \texttt{physics\_probe} PASSED. \textbf{Advisor}: the five
  diffs match the tree against the worker\textquotesingle s entry snapshots; forbidden-word
  scan clean; the current tree builds warning-free (\texttt{build\_lwv}); the
