  itself moves nothing on the matrix path. No golden refreshed. \textbf{Recorded,
  not repaired (outside this item\textquotesingle s files)}: \texttt{carrier\_element\_headroom}
  states a box side per carrier but no joint bound for carriers sharing an
  element (the physical constraint \texttt{2\ n(H2)\ +\ n(OH)\ +\ 2\ n(H2O)\ +\ n(H+)\ \textless{}=\ nH\_avail} is a hyperplane the code does not state), and OH/H2O hold
  hydrogen nuclei their own headroom does not mention, so a state can pass
  every headroom and still leave the write-back\textquotesingle s hydrogen remainder
  negative (the branch that zeroes H I, H II, H2+, H3+); neither reaches
  the \texttt{\{H2\}} carrier set both measured cases use. Also: the epsilon-active

  ("leave running things alone, stop code modification for now") no

  \texttt{src/tests/attempted\_step/\{run.sh,attempted\_step\_tests.f90\}}; user
  2026-09-09 "N16a 병행"): \textbf{the sampled step-doubling comparison is one
  transaction over one macro-interval \texttt{H}}: the full pass covers \texttt{H}, the
  two half passes \texttt{0.5\ H} each and end at \texttt{t\ +\ H} by construction (pass 3
  no longer inherits whatever \texttt{dt} held); a refusal inside any pass, a
  finite estimate above one, or a nonfinite estimate rejects the WHOLE
  macrostep (\texttt{as\_reject\_int\_error} now issued by the production caller),
  restores \texttt{as\_entry}, shortens \texttt{H} by the controller and recomputes both
  trajectories; exhaustion of the transaction\textquotesingle s retry budget
  (\texttt{n\_macro\_retry}, cap 8) is a named failure that stops with status 2,
  names the pass and prints the checksum of the restored state
  (\texttt{stop\_on\_exhausted\_retry\_budget}, one report for both exits). The clock,
  the accepted count and the physical accumulations move only at the
  adoption boundary below the macrostep loop. \textbf{The order of the complete
  split update is MEASURED as \texttt{p\ =\ 1}}: at one fixed state the estimate
  scales as \texttt{H\^{}2} over five decades (\texttt{p\ +\ 1} = 1.964, 1.993, 1.9985,
  1.9997, 1.9999, 2.0004, 2.0000 for \texttt{H} from 2e-2 s to 2.6e-7 s; the
  coarsest 1e-1 s is outside that regime and excluded), so
  \texttt{attempted\_step\_error\_estimate} now returns the retained full step\textquotesingle s
  error \texttt{Delta/(1\ -\ 2\^{}-p)\ =\ 2\ Delta} (\texttt{err\_order\_p},
  \texttt{err\_full\_step\_factor}, convention at the declaration) {[}superseded by
  N16d/N16e: p = 1 was the base-face leak; the conservative and electron/T
  rows are p = 3, factor 8/7, the species rows p = 1{]} and the reduction
  is \texttt{0.9\ e\^{}(-1/(p+1))}. The brief\textquotesingle s first procedure (one interval by 1, 2,
  4, 8 prescribed steps, ratios of the output rows) was INCONCLUSIVE, \texttt{p}
  0.4 to 4.0, because the third difference (2.2e-8 relative) sits at the
  coupled source step\textquotesingle s fixed-point tolerance: the inner error is not
  below the temporal error there (review F3\textquotesingle s condition, not yet
  enforced). Test hooks, default off: \texttt{EXHALE\_AS\_ERR\_INJECT{[}\_COUNT{]}},
  \texttt{EXHALE\_AS\_REJECT\_IN\_PASS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_AS\_FIXED\_DT} (seconds; in

  stated at the hook). \texttt{EXHALE\_STEP\_CLOCK=1} now also prints an \texttt{err-pass:}
  line per pass and \texttt{state=} (checkpoint checksum) per accepted step.

  \texttt{\#\ adv\_stationarity\_operator\ face\_flux\_mass}, \texttt{\#\ adv\_product} (a ONE-WAY

  species carry more of the cell\textquotesingle s particle count than the carried ones

  item}: the ratio screen and the mass row are not interchangeable and the
  ratio is the weaker: on the analytic outflow fixture the ratio is 1.1e-13
  everywhere while the mass row stands at 2.1e-7 (RED of the old
  criterion); on \texttt{wasp\_full} the ratio refused 297 of 503 rows, the mass row

  503 (0.245 at r 4.21); \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} (the one certified state) 2 vs

  product IS the run\textquotesingle s own state (\texttt{T\_adv} equal to \texttt{T} in 504 of 504 rows;
  the old product differed by up to 6.4 and 207 percent); on the certified

  \texttt{\_adv} golden will move. \textbf{Advisor decision (N11b, launched)}: the
  criterion is the right OPERATOR but the wrong TOLERANCE for a conditional
  correction. \texttt{cert\_tol\_mass\ =\ 3e-12} is the tolerance of a certified
  stationary state; the advective correction is a first-order local
  correction whose own error is of the order of the mass row\textquotesingle s measure
  (the fractional change of the face mass flux across the cell), so a
  du-stopped or snapshot state with a mass row of 1e-3 is exactly what the
  tool was built for and is what review R6 calls the "approximate local
  use" with "a tolerance tied to the required error". N11b writes the mass
  row measure itself as a column of \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz\_adv.txt}, sets
  \texttt{adv\_conditional\_tol\ =\ 1e-2} (the correction is described as accurate to
  that fraction, stated in the header beside \texttt{adv\_input\_certified}), and
  keeps the certification verdict as the separate statement it is; the
  product is then a CONDITIONAL correction with its condition written in
  every row. Advisor: two \texttt{per-field} and two \texttt{percent} (one in a printed
  message) fixed. \textbf{Reported, not fixed}: \texttt{assemble\_residual} leaves
  \texttt{R(:,1-Ng)} undefined (\texttt{RK\_rhs} assigns \texttt{2-Ng..N+Ng} only; no caller reads
  it today; \texttt{RK\_rhs.f90}/\texttt{steady\_residual.f90}); \texttt{README.md} 154,
  \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} 959 and the manual section 4 still describe the single
  \texttt{adv\_status} column (N11b updates them with the final schema); a suite row
  for \texttt{corrected} needs a converged fixture.

  \textbf{N22} (the predicted decrease stops scaling below \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}s\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1e-7;
  \texttt{steady\_newton.f90} +195/-10, \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} +16 rows): \textbf{all three
  candidates of the brief refuted by measurement, and the cause named}:
  the trials that break the scaling are exactly the trials whose species
  unknown was written onto a face of its box (\texttt{nout\ =\ 1}), and that write is
  a move of the state no model of the step contains (11 of 11 such trials
  refused with a fixed ascent of 3e-3 over five decades of step; 10 of 10
  others accepted at ratio 1.000). When the radius is cut the SCALED step
  quarters but the step in the unknowns the residual reads, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}D\ s\textbar{}\textbar{}},
  moves 0.4 percent, so the model image and \texttt{pred} are unchanged to seven
  digits; \texttt{r0\ .\ A\ s\ =\ -\textbar{}\textbar{}A\ s\textbar{}\textbar{}\^{}2} to every digit (\texttt{pred\ =\ 0.5\ (r0\ .\ A\ g)\^{}2\ /\ \textbar{}\textbar{}A\ g\textbar{}\textbar{}\^{}2}, radius-free, the interior minimizer along the leg: not a
  defect); a fresh Jacobian action at the same step reproduces \texttt{pred} to
  every digit (no stale cache); the probe displacement is \texttt{sqrt(eps)(1\ +\ \textbar{}\textbar{}Y\textbar{}\textbar{})} = 1.8e-7 whatever the direction, nine decades above the
  residual\textquotesingle s reproducibility (no floor; the reverse: the stalling step is
  2.7e-3 of the probe arc). \textbf{The fix}: \texttt{hold\_the\_step\_where\_it\_leaves\_the\_\ species\_box} zeroes the step at an unknown it would carry out of its box
  (the iterate is feasible, so the trial is, and the step is one the
  operator can be sampled along); \texttt{probe\_length\_of\_the\_jacobian\_action} is
  the one definition of the probe arc; a diagnostic line for each trial under
  \texttt{EXHALE\_ELEM\_DIAG}. \textbf{Atomic reload, cap 300, before -\textgreater{} after}: 227 -\textgreater{}
  113 iterations (both stagnation), accepted/refused 170/55 -\textgreater{} 91/19,
  negative ratios 49 -\textgreater{} 14, best \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 2.353e-2 -\textgreater{} \textbf{1.333e-2}, handed
  back 2.671e-2 -\textgreater{} \textbf{1.356e-2}; the period-two cycle is gone (iterations
  95-98: \texttt{pred} doubles with the step, all accepted at 0.998 to 1.001, steps
  1e-2 to 1e-1 instead of 1e-9); the closing refusals are ray/floor verdicts
  at POSITIVE ratios. Carrier reload BITWISE (the branch never fires);

  IMAGE, not the length ratio (N7b\textquotesingle s finding at a length the threshold

\item
  \textbf{N23} (the approximate-gradient leg admitted by its share of the model
  image; \texttt{steady\_newton.f90} +220/-5, \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} +16 rows): two
  constants \texttt{cauchy\_leg\_image\_share\_max\ =\ 0.5}, \texttt{cauchy\_leg\_step\_share\_max\ =\ 0.1}, two pure routines (\texttt{cauchy\_leg\_shares\_of\_step\_and\_image},
  \texttt{cauchy\_leg\_is\_image\_without\_step}: image share above the first AND step
  share below the second, so a leg that IS the step keeps its image), and a
  branch in the dogleg\textquotesingle s trial loop that DROPS such a leg from the step and
  its image and rebuilds the dogleg at no extra product (drop, not rescale:
  a rescale would either shorten \texttt{A\ sU} without \texttt{sU}, which is not a step of
  the operator, or move the step off the dogleg path); the model slope
  handed to \texttt{ray\_slope\_verdict} is the slope of the step taken in both
  branches. \textbf{MEASURED, the reading (\texttt{{[}diag\ 9{]}})}: at the reload\textquotesingle s second
  solve quartering the radius moves the promise by 0.9959 with the leg
  admitted and by 0.2501 with it dropped (six iterates: 0.9959 to 0.8938
  against 0.2501 to 0.2517), so the promise follows the radius again where it
  did not. \textbf{Honest impact: the new rule fires ZERO times on both fixtures

  \texttt{dgbcon} 1.25e-6, step ratio 0.9998); what fails is the linear model: 40

  was not falling (1.337e-2 -\textgreater{} 1.333e-2 over 32 iterations). \textbf{The
  image-only leg test} (\texttt{EXHALE\_CAUCHY\_LEG\_BY\_IMAGE=1}, default off)

  cell N through the write-back. \textbf{A control that multiplies the same three
  ghost quantities by \texttt{1\ +\ 2e-16} and poses no boundary at all breaks the reload
  as badly} (\texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} at iteration 40: caller\textquotesingle s ghosts 3.212e-2 = N26 to the
  digit; refill 1.199; one-ulp control 1.125; the three part at outer 10-11
  and from each other). Where it amplifies: the Krylov leg at its one-digit
  tolerance (relative residual reached 8.86e-2 / 8.89e-2 / 8.93e-2 at outer
  10, \texttt{pred} 0.4 percent apart, then 2.7x apart at outer 11, then different
  branches of the acceptance at outer 12: accepted 0.874 / REFUSED -1.98 /
  accepted 85); the solve\textquotesingle s own merit reproducibility prints 1.4e-14, a
  hundred times the ghost movement. \textbf{The refill is physically right and is
  reverted anyway}: no version of it can be bit-inert on the production
  caller, and the reload\textquotesingle s acceptance is a trajectory no last-bit change
  preserves. Delivered text reproduces N26\textquotesingle s atomic reload line for line over
  252 records (\texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 3.719e-4, judged 6.22e4, He of cell 246, 27543
  samples, exit 2) and N26\textquotesingle s carrier reload over 63 (0.2519, 4.806e7, H2 of
  cell 205); \texttt{mol\_diffusion} marching bitwise; suites \texttt{certification} 29/0,

  6/0, 29/0, 189/0, 174/0. \textbf{The finding that matters beyond this item}:
  the atomic element reload is NOT a reproducible quantity; its \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} at a
  fixed iteration count is set by which Arnoldi vector crosses a one-digit
  tolerance at the last bits of the residual, so "reproduces the previous
  run" rejects correct changes as readily as wrong ones, and what the reload
  can carry is named outcomes (binding rows, refusal counts, flux spread),
  not \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} at iteration N. Recorded in ISSUES 3.1 (d) and in the fixture\textquotesingle s
  README. The asymmetry of the element operator (the relaxation twins write

\item
  \textbf{N29} (the element projection does not conserve mass;
  \texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90} +258/-113, \texttt{element\_operator} suite 6 -\textgreater{} 14
  rows): \textbf{the leak is the mass the write-back is told to hand back, not the
  write-back.} \texttt{project\_elements} took the mixture mass as \texttt{msum\ =\ m\_1\ n\_H\ +\ m\_He\ n\_He} with \texttt{m\_1\ =\ mass\_per\_H\_nucleus\_without\_He()}, the RESERVOIR mass
  per hydrogen nucleus (the trace metals at \texttt{melem\_ab}), while the cell
  carries the metals at whatever the trace transport left; the projection
  then wrote the difference into hydrogen at every call. MEASURED at one cell
  of the certification column (metal/H 0.024401 against the reservoir\textquotesingle s
  0.028227): \texttt{sum\ m\_i\ f\_i} 1.0000000 -\textgreater{} 1.0000636 after one step, helium\textquotesingle s
  gain exceeding the hydrogen and metal loss by 6.4e-5: mass created. A
  second leak of the same kind: the trace-metal diffusion wrote metal densities
  with no counter-flux in the hydrogen it diffuses through (3.0e-3 on the
  suite column with the metals frozen). \textbf{Fix}: \texttt{mixture\_mass\_split} reads
  the mass the cell\textquotesingle s species actually carry (\texttt{bsp\_mass} and \texttt{melem\_A}, no
  species weight restated), \texttt{project\_elements(f\_sp,\ Xhe,\ msum,\ scale\_metals)}
  scales component 1 as a whole, every helium-free species included (CO
  too), the metals written first and the hydrogen group closing the mixture;
  one projection after the metal loop puts the metals\textquotesingle{} mass change back on
  hydrogen; \texttt{settling\_coefficient} takes its component-1 mass from the same
  split. Metals off is bitwise by construction (\texttt{m\_1\ =\ m\_H\ =\ 1} exactly).
  \textbf{MEASURED}: certification column closure after the relaxation 3.37e-3 -\textgreater{}
  2.3e-15; the handed-back atomic candidate 6.44e-3 -\textgreater{} 1.94e-9 (the file\textquotesingle s
  own precision, the three-unknown control\textquotesingle s 1.93e-9); the writer-to-loader

  1.1123 on the SAME row, against 3.2e-2 vs 0.4385 on different rows before:
  memo section 6 closed); \texttt{mol\_diffusion} and \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} BITWISE
  (metals off / no element row); \textbf{\texttt{lower\_profile} MOVES and should}: its
  stored golden is 3.6e-4 out of closure (O/H 4.61e-4 against the reservoir
  4.90e-4), a 1500-step cold start goes 6.77e-3 -\textgreater{} 3.14e-9 in closure with
  T at 1.6 Rp moving 1.3 percent and H I 6.3 percent: \textbf{a golden that moves

  element reload with the new hook \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_ADDITIVITY=1}
  (\texttt{{[}diag\ 13{]}}, at outer 1, 20 and the last of a capped run, on the first
  two Arnoldi directions and on two directions off the basis, at a tenth,
  one and ten times the probe arc): the defect of the Arnoldi pair scales
  as the arc to the power \textbf{-1.026} at outer 1 and \textbf{-0.999} at outer 40
  (rounding), the off-basis pair as \textbf{+1.125} and \textbf{+2.109} (curvature,
  and 3.2e-8 and 2.1e-8, negligible), and at outer 20 both are curvature at
  cells 1 and 2. The second difference of the residual on the
  defect-holding rows confirms it directly: ratios 0.774 and 1.676 at outer
  1 where a smooth residual gives 4 and 4, and the floor stands \textbf{9.5e5}
  (outer 1) to \textbf{2.0e9} (outer 40) times \texttt{epsilon} times the residual on
  those same rows, so it is not the arithmetic of the assembly. READ, the
  only tolerance in the atomic path that can set a floor of that size:
  \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90} line 969 hands \texttt{hybrd1} an xtol of
  \texttt{sqrt(dpmpar(1))} = 1.49e-8 (lines 1432, 2110, 2114), a statement about
  the step and not the residual, so the composition is a piecewise map of
  the state; \texttt{eval\_residual} fixes the number of composition passes but not
  \texttt{hybrd1}\textquotesingle s own iteration count. \textbf{A decision of the feasible set is ruled
  out}: on all six probes of the three iterates, components blocked 0,
  forward side, and the step taken equal to the nominal step. \textbf{The
  localization gives no unknown class to fix}: at outer 1 the squared
  defect is 0.31 mass, 0.64 energy, 0.03 momentum, 0.02 helium, under 1e-4
  in the eight trace elements, and nine tenths of it sits in 35 of the 500
  cells between 1 and 75. The scale reading is a uniform shortfall: the
  largest unknown of the state is displaced by 2.2e-8 of itself (about
  \texttt{sqrt(epsilon)}) and almost every other by 1e-10 to 6e-9, one to two
  decades short, with no unknown near a bound (nearest face 2.9e-5 against
  a displacement of 4.5e-14). The model that follows, defect \textasciitilde{} floor /
  (eps \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}Jv\textbar{}\textbar{}}), predicts both pairs at outer 1 within a factor three.
  \textbf{Two options, both default off.} \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_COLUMN\_SCALE=1}
  (\texttt{probe\_step\_on\_the\_column\_scales}, \texttt{eps0\ =\ sqrt(eps)(1\ +\ \textbar{}\textbar{}Dc\^{}-1\ Y\textbar{}\textbar{})\ /\ \textbar{}\textbar{}Dc\^{}-1\ v\textbar{}\textbar{}}) is the brief\textquotesingle s remedy and it is WORSE, for the reason the
  diagnosis gives: the Krylov direction\textquotesingle s weight sits on unknowns of small
  column scale, so the rule SHORTENS the arc by 42 and the defect rises
  8.430e-6 -\textgreater{} 1.466e-4 at exponent -0.965, the 1/arc law reproduced by a
  change of the arc made for another reason. \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_PROBE\_ARC=\textless{}x\textgreater{}}
  (\texttt{jv\_probe\_arc\_scale}, default 1) is the lever the measurement points at,
  and it wins the linear question and loses the Newton by the same change:
  at x = 100 the defect of the Arnoldi pair falls 8.430e-6 -\textgreater{} 2.240e-6, the
  exponent turns +1.139 and the second differences become 4.065 and 4.022,
  so the rounding regime is gone and the quotient is truncation-limited,
  while the atomic reload then ABORTS at outer 33 (\texttt{info\ =\ 2}, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}}
  1.504, flux spread 5.902e-2, 12 steps refused by the model with the
  actual reduction 26.4 times the predicted, radius 2.028e-7) where the
  control runs 144 iterations to 3.641e-4 and 5.557e-6 with no model
  refusal. At x = 20 it aborts at outer 43 at 1.524 with 21 model
  refusals and a radius of 2.076e-13; the carrier reload runs its 40
  iterations at both values and hands back 1.006 at x = 100 and 9.076e-1
  at x = 20, each with 2 model refusals, against the control\textquotesingle s 9.572e-2
  with none. \textbf{At x = 5, which
  does NOT stall}, the binding regime is reached (outer 58, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}}
  3.087e-3, element Fe of cell 146, against the control\textquotesingle s 3.386e-3 and
  element Fe of cell 147 at outer 60) and THERE the Krylov cycle still
  spends 40 of 40 products at a relative residual of 0.898 to 0.980
  against the 0.1 asked, indistinguishable from the control\textquotesingle s 0.935 to
  0.959. \textbf{The substance of the item}: the additivity of the linear
  operator wants a LONGER arc (defect \textasciitilde{} floor / arc) and the trust
  region\textquotesingle s model wants a SHORTER one (truncation \textasciitilde{} arc, and the ray test
  refuses a step whose model and true slope differ in size), the arc
  already sits at the Newton\textquotesingle s balance point, and every direction was
  measured to lose, so NO probe rule fixes this. What can is lowering the
  residual\textquotesingle s own floor, which is not double precision.
  Column-scale named outcomes at cap 40, option against control:
  atomic \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} at 40 1.674 against 7.607e-2, handed back 1.633 against
  5.377e-2, flux spread 4.985e-1 against 3.555e-3, 30/10 against 32/8;
  carrier \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} at 40 1.357e-1 against 1.070e-1, handed back 1.263e-1
  against 9.572e-2, flux spread 7.718e-2 against 4.635e-2, 31/9 against
  33/7, SAME binding row (carrier H2 of cell 205), no new
  \texttt{gm\_no\_direction\_sampled} and no refusal of any class in either. The one
  thing the column scaling improves is the count of Krylov cycles reaching the
  tolerance asked (40 of 40 against 26 of 40), which is the REDUCED
  problem\textquotesingle s residual on an operator that is not the Jacobian and is
  precisely why that count must not be read as progress. NEITHER OPTION IS
  ADOPTED: both fail the first clause of the acceptance rule outright.
  \textbf{Default path unchanged}: with every hook off the atomic reload\textquotesingle s 40
  iteration lines are identical to the control\textquotesingle s to the digit, the carrier
  reload\textquotesingle s binding row and \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} at 40 are the control\textquotesingle s, and
  \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} is BITWISE but for the provenance timestamp; with
  \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_ADDITIVITY=1} on, the 40 iteration lines are still identical,
  so the hook is inert armed as well as disarmed. Tests: \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg}
  RED on the control objects (seven symbols absent), GREEN 190/0;
  \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 192/0, \texttt{certification} 57/0, \texttt{element\_operator}
  14/0, \texttt{residual\_determinism} 5/1 with the same row failing by design in
  the control build. \textbf{Fixture pinned (deliverable 0)}:
  \texttt{backup/regression/carrier\_elem\_newton} now holds the carrier reload that
  existed only as a scratch recipe, with \texttt{run.sh}, a README and the control
  of the unmodified tree at cap 40 (\texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1.969, 1.984, 1.921, 6.800e-1,
  1.070e-1 at 1, 5, 10, 20, 40; handed back 9.572e-2; flux spread 4.635e-2;
  \texttt{info\ =\ 1}; 33 accepted, 7 rejected; 40 of 40 products every cycle;
  binding row carrier H2 of cell 205; 2754 samples admitted, none refused).
  A capped run of that fixture never reaches a mass-loss rate. \textbf{Defect
  fixed on the way}: the new probe rule was first written with a local
  \texttt{integer\ ::\ n}, which is the grid size \texttt{N} of \texttt{global\_parameters} under
  Fortran\textquotesingle s case-insensitive rules, so its own size guard fell through on
  every call; the new suite rows caught it at once and the local is now
  \texttt{nunk}. Not claimed: that the floor is \texttt{hybrd1}\textquotesingle s xtol -\/- that is READ
  from the source and not measured, and the experiment that closes it
  (tighten that xtol, re-measure the floor) belongs to
  \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}, which this item does not own. The comment
  of the suite row \texttt{the\_probe\_arc\_stands\_above\_the\_residual\_noise} was
  restated: the arc IS nine decades above the residual\textquotesingle s reproducibility as
  a length of the whole vector, but the conclusion drawn from it about the
  quotient does not follow. Advisor: rebuild on \texttt{build\_lwv} (one linker warning, pre-existing), \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 190/0, \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0, \texttt{residual\_determinism} one row FAIL by design; atomic reload at cap 40 with every hook off, 8 threads: 1.888, 1.891, 1.819, 1.583, 1.861, 8.964e-1, 7.607e-2 at 1, 5, 10, 15, 20, 30, 40, handed back 5.377e-2, flux spread 3.555e-3, identical to the worker\textquotesingle s control; the carrier fixture\textquotesingle s README control checked against its own \texttt{run.log}; the fixture README is the reference for every later carrier-reload number (the 0.25 quoted after N26 was a scratch-recipe run, not this fixture). Accepted. \textbf{Next item N32}: the residual\textquotesingle s floor (the inner solves of the composition and of the temperature stop on step or bracket tolerances, \texttt{newton\_dense}, \texttt{hybrd1} xtol \texttt{sqrt(eps)}, \texttt{brent\_root} 1e-10); measure whether the additivity floor follows them.
\item
  \textbf{N32} (which inner tolerance sets the residual\textquotesingle s non-smoothness floor;
  two default-off tolerance hooks and a widened \texttt{{[}diag\ 13{]}},
  \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90} +47/-2, \texttt{T\_equation.f90} +42/-3,
  \texttt{steady\_newton.f90} about +130 inside the additivity hook,
  \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 190 -\textgreater{} 203 rows): \textbf{NEITHER. The floor follows
  neither the composition solve\textquotesingle s stopping tolerance nor the temperature
  bracket\textquotesingle s, and it is not in the chemistry at all: it sits in the
  HYDRODYNAMIC rows of the residual.} New hooks \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_TOL=\textless{}x\textgreater{}}
  (replaces \texttt{tol\ =\ sqrt(dpmpar(1))} for the run; new function
  \texttt{composition\_solve\_tolerance}, cached once by \texttt{ioniz\_eq}, announced only
  when armed) and \texttt{EXHALE\_TEQ\_TOL=\textless{}x\textgreater{}} (the bracket of \texttt{brent\_root}, now a
  module variable at its old 1e-10 through
  \texttt{temperature\_bracket\_tolerance}). MEASURED on the atomic element reload
  at outer 1 (cap 40, 8 threads, one entry state for every run): the
  additivity defect of the Arnoldi pair is 8.174e-6 at exponent -1.063
  (default 1.49e-8), 7.452e-6 at -0.995 (1e-10), 9.348e-6 at -1.059
  (1e-12) and 9.546e-6 at -1.064 (1e-14), and the second difference on the
  defect rows stays on its floor at 5.6e-12 to 1.6e-11 with no trend in
  the tolerance: \textbf{the law is FLAT}, over six decades. The measurement is
  exactly reproducible (three control runs, and 1 thread against 8, give
  8.174e-6 and 1.362e-11 to every printed digit), so the +-17 percent
  between runs is a real movement of the state and not a fall.
  \texttt{EXHALE\_TEQ\_TOL=1e-13} is inert to the bit through all 40 iterations,
  which confirms as MEASURED what was READ, that \texttt{brent\_root} is reached
  only from \texttt{post\_process\_adv} and never from \texttt{eval\_residual}. \textbf{Why the
  composition cannot be the floor}: quadratic convergence pins its root
  at the SQUARE of the step tolerance. On the reported cell the accepted
  \texttt{x(H+)} is 2.037266074305163e-12 at the default and
  2.037266074305300e-12 at 1e-14, agreeing to 13 significant digits (7e-14
  relative), and the pinned fixture\textquotesingle s own run.log records an accepted
  reaction residual of 2.91e-16 at maximum for the steady sweeps; the
  floor implies a jitter of about 1e-9 relative, five decades above that.
  The tightest tolerance costs 30 percent more wall time per residual
  evaluation (0.090 s against 0.066 s at 4 threads, 17 evaluations each)
  and buys nothing. \textbf{Where the floor IS}, from the widened hook: the
  energy SOURCE, which is where \texttt{excited\_hydrogen}, \texttt{lya\_rt}, the cooling
  table interpolation, the write-back clamps and \texttt{comp\_T\_from\_p} all end
  up, is smooth on the arc and five decades below the floor (second
  difference of \texttt{heat\ -\ cool} on the defect cells 3.039e-17, 2.474e-16,
  1.046e-15 at half the arc, the arc and twice it, ratios 8.1 and 4.2,
  textbook h squared, against the residual\textquotesingle s own 1.362e-11 on the same
  rows); by row class over the whole column the floor is mass 1.801e-11,
  energy 3.090e-11, momentum 5.946e-12 against element He 4.269e-12 and
  every trace element at 1e-16 to 2e-13; nine tenths of it sits in 29 of
  500 cells between cell 1 and cell 58. The mass row carries no source at
  all, so no part of the chemistry can be its cause. The competing
  explanation, that the row is a small difference of large terms and its
  floor is their rounding, is REFUTED: the largest of heat and cool on
  those cells is 3.424e-3 against \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}F\textbar{}\textbar{}} 5.507e-3, a ratio of 0.62, and
  epsilon times it is 7.6e-19. \textbf{Deliverable 2 (the stationary-solve-only
  polish) was NOT BUILT}: the brief conditions it on deliverable 1
  naming a tolerance, and none is named. \textbf{A correction to the brief and
  to N31}: on this fixture \texttt{solve\_ieq} and \texttt{newton\_dense} never run.
  The helium triplet is on (the fixture\textquotesingle s \texttt{EXHALE\_setup.out}) and
  \texttt{metals.inp} is present, so the sweep calls
  \texttt{hybrd1(ion\_system\_HeH\_TR\_metals,\ ...)} directly; MEASURED \texttt{nt\_calls} 0
  over the whole hook and no "ioniz-eq solver" line in the fixture\textquotesingle s
  reference run.log. \texttt{hybrd1} returns \texttt{info\ =\ 1} at the default, at 1e-10
  and at 1e-14 alike, so there is no \texttt{info\ =\ 2/3} and no fallback to
  count, and \texttt{newton\_dense}\textquotesingle s hardcoded step test \texttt{1e-11*xscale} is not in
  this path. \textbf{Default path unchanged}: \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} BITWISE in
  \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} and identical but for the provenance timestamp in
  \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt}, every \texttt{(JFNK)} line identical; the atomic reload\textquotesingle s 40
  iteration lines identical to the control\textquotesingle s with every hook off. Tests:
  \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} RED on the control objects (both new functions
  absent), GREEN 203/0 with 13 rows added; \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0,
  \texttt{certification} 57/0, \texttt{element\_operator} 14/0, \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} 53/0,
  \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} 30/0, \texttt{physics\_probe} 1491/0,
  \texttt{residual\_determinism} 5/1 with the same row failing by design as in the
  control. Noticed, not fixed: the fixture README does not say the helium
  triplet is on, which is what selects the direct \texttt{hybrd1} branch and is
  what sent this item\textquotesingle s brief to the wrong routine; and \texttt{newton\_dense}
  applies the caller\textquotesingle s tolerance to \texttt{fnorm} while its step test uses a
  hardcoded \texttt{1e-11*xscale} no caller can set, which would be the real
  floor on a fixture that does reach it. \textbf{Next item}: the floor is in
  the flux assembly, not in \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90} -\/- the candidates
  left are the reconstruction where the limiter is not the frozen WENO3
  weight, the Riemann wave-speed estimate, \texttt{Apply\_BC}, and the transport
  coefficients of the diffusion operator, which are the one remaining way
  the composition reaches a hydrodynamic row. Advisor: rebuild on \texttt{build\_lwv}; \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 203/0, \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0, \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} 53/0, \texttt{residual\_determinism} the same single row FAIL by design; atomic reload at cap 40 with every hook off, 8 threads: the N31 ladder to the digit (1.888, 1.891, 1.819, 1.583, 1.861, 8.964e-1, 7.607e-2; handed back 5.377e-2, spread 3.555e-3), so the worker\textquotesingle s 1.112 at iteration 40 is the thread-count sensitivity of N26c, not a change of the path. The atomic fixture README now states the triplet is on and which branch that selects; \texttt{newton\_dense}\textquotesingle s hardcoded step test recorded in ISSUES 3.7. Accepted; deliverable 2 correctly not built. \textbf{Next item N33}: the jump itself, scanned along the Arnoldi direction across four arcs and split by term and operator (base boundary, reconstruction, Riemann flux, caloric inversion, composition read, transport sources).
\item
  \textbf{N33} (where the residual\textquotesingle s non-smoothness floor is: the term, the
  operator and the branch; a default-off multi-scale jump scan
  \texttt{{[}diag\ 14{]}}, \texttt{steady\_newton.f90} +710, \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 203 -\textgreater{} 220
  rows): \textbf{THERE IS NO BRANCH. The floor is the ROUNDING OF THE FLUX
  ASSEMBLY, raised above the last bit of the row by two cancellations
  that compound.} The Roe flux of a face is built from the JUMPS of the
  two reconstructed states, and in the nearly hydrostatic base layer
  those jumps are 3.2e5 to 5.9e6 times smaller than the states
  themselves, so the interface flux carries the last bit of O(1)
  quantities and not of its own value; the row is then the flux
  difference divided by the cell volume, \texttt{r\^{}2/dV} = 5.12e3, one over the
  1.955e-4 width of a base cell. The bound
  \texttt{epsilon\ x\ (face\ state)\ x\ r\^{}2/dV} is what a correctly rounded assembly
  cannot go below, and MEASURED it IS the floor: 1.675e-12 against
  1.240e-11 at cell 1, 2.520e-13 against 1.644e-12 at cell 84, 4.326e-15
  against 2.366e-14 at cell 167, 4.241e-17 against 4.409e-16 at cell 251
  -\/- the bound falling 4.6 decades with the measured floor following it,
  ratio 5.5 to 12 throughout, and the ten cells holding the largest
  second difference are cells 1 to 23, every one a base cell. Beyond
  cell about 250 the bound falls below the residual\textquotesingle s own curvature and
  stops binding, which is what a floor does. New hook
  \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_JUMP\_SCAN=1} samples F at 65 points of \texttt{Y\ +\ t\ v} on ten
  windows, each a quarter of the one before, from two probe arcs down to
  two arcs over 4\^{}9; one window is not enough, because at the standard
  arc every interval already holds thousands of steps and none stands
  above its neighbors. \textbf{The measurement}: on the atomic element
  reload at outer 1 the tracked row is the energy row of cell 1, its
  median first difference falls by exactly four whenever the spacing
  does (the smooth part is a straight line), and its second difference
  does not fall at all -\/- 1.0e-11 to 1.6e-11 over 5.4 decades of
  spacing, 1.8e-5 down to 6.9e-11 -\/- which is a step of F and not a kink
  or curvature. Isolated steps appear once the smooth increment per
  interval falls below the floor: 28 of 64 intervals at 3.05e-5 arcs and
  61 of 64 at 7.63e-6 arcs, at least 4.0e6 stepping intervals per probe
  arc, the largest moving its row by 2.625e-12, 2.749e-9 of the row.
  \textbf{The term and the operator}, at the largest step (cell 9, mass row):
  \texttt{S} of the mass row is identically zero and the whole step is in \texttt{dF};
  every quantity upstream of the flux -\/- the ghosts, \texttt{T}, \texttt{n\_tot\ +\ n\_e}
  and the four reconstructed face states -\/- moves by one to eight units
  in the last place of an O(1) number with no outlier, in exact
  multiples of 2.220e-16; the first quantity that steps is the interface
  flux, by 4.803e-16 where its neighbors move by 3.2e-17, and
  4.803e-16 x 5116.3 = 2.457e-12 against the row\textquotesingle s measured 2.625e-12.
  \textbf{No branch fires anywhere near it}: faces falling back from Roe to
  HLLE 0 and unchanged, face states the positivity limiter scaled 0 and
  unchanged, so the base boundary\textquotesingle s Newton, \texttt{characteristic\_branch\_weight},
  the Mach cap, the ghost quadrature, the stencil selection, the
  wave-speed branch and \texttt{comp\_T\_from\_p} are all continuous here. The
  off-basis direction carries the SAME floor, 9.1e-12 to 1.4e-11 flat
  over 5.4 decades, and isolates no step at any window, because its
  smooth increment never falls below it: the floor is a property of the
  residual and not of the direction, and what the preconditioned
  direction does is shrink the numerator of the difference quotient by
  five decades. The carrier reload reproduces the law (floor 1.532e-11,
  bound 1.382e-12, ratio 11.09), so it is the discretization at the base
  and not one fixture. \textbf{N31 corrected}: the floor\textquotesingle s size stands, but
  "five to nine decades above the rounding of its own rows" compared it
  against \texttt{epsilon\ \textbar{}\textbar{}F\textbar{}\textbar{}} on the row, which is the wrong reference for a
  row built as a flux difference over a cell width; against the right
  one it is a factor of ten. N32\textquotesingle s localization is confirmed and made
  quantitative. \textbf{Deliverable 2 NOT BUILT}: the brief conditions the
  deliverable on a branch being named and there is none. The treatment the
  measurement does name, for the advisor, is a WELL-BALANCED flux
  difference at the base -\/- assemble the rows from the departure of the
  face states from the local hydrostatic isentrope, which
  \texttt{base\_boundary.f90} already integrates, so the balance cancels
  analytically instead of in floating point -\/- in \texttt{Num\_Fluxes.f90},
  \texttt{RK\_rhs.f90} and \texttt{Source.f90}, none of them this item\textquotesingle s files; the
  cheaper partial lever is the base cell width, on which the floor
  depends as one over it. \textbf{Default path unchanged}: the atomic reload
  at cap 40 gives all 338 \texttt{(JFNK)} lines identical to the control\textquotesingle s
  through \texttt{done\ info=1\ \textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}=\ 1.112E+00}; with the scan armed the
  outer-1 line is the control\textquotesingle s to every digit; \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}
  BITWISE in \texttt{Ion\_species.txt}, \texttt{Ion\_species\_adv.txt} and the three
  breakdown files, differing only in the provenance timestamp in the two
  hydro files, \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30 on both. Tests: \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg}
  RED on the control objects (\texttt{resid\_jump\_scan\_on} absent), GREEN 220/0
  with 17 rows added; \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0, \texttt{element\_operator}
  14/0, \texttt{certification} 57/0, \texttt{residual\_determinism} 5/1 with the same
  row failing by design and the same measured 3.354e+01 on the control
  binary. Noticed, not fixed: \texttt{global\_parameters} declares
  \texttt{integer\ ::\ count}, which shadows the intrinsic \texttt{COUNT} in every scope
  that uses the module, so \texttt{count(mask)} written anywhere fails to
  compile -\/- the same class as the \texttt{t0} and \texttt{g2s} incidents and worse in
  that the shadowed name is an intrinsic; renaming a public global was
  out of scope. Advisor: rebuild on \texttt{build\_lwv}; \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 220/0, \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0; atomic reload at cap 40 with every hook off, 8 threads: the N31 ladder to the digit (7.607e-2 at 40, handed back 5.377e-2, spread 3.555e-3), no \texttt{{[}diag\ 14{]}} line; two British spellings in the new comments fixed. Accepted; deliverable 2 correctly not built (there is no branch to smooth). The finding is structural and reaches the marching discretization, so it is put to the user as DECISION 23 (well-balanced flux differencing recommended; quad accumulation of the flux difference as the control experiment; coarser base cells; accept the floor). The \texttt{count} global shadowing the intrinsic recorded in ISSUES 3.7 (HYG). \textbf{Next item N34} (runs without a decision, changes no default): the control experiment, quad accumulation of the face fluxes and their difference inside the stationary residual only, as a measured option.
\item
  \textbf{N34} (the control experiment of decision 23: the hydrodynamic rows of
  the stationary residual in quadruple precision). \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_QUAD=1}
  sends the STATIONARY evaluations of \texttt{assemble\_residual} -\/- the tag
  \texttt{ieq\_sweep\_state\_kind} puts on an iterate or a probe; the marching
  stages reach \texttt{RK\_rhs} directly and are untouched -\/- through a
  quadruple-precision instantiation of the whole flux assembly, from the
  conserved state through the caloric EOS, the PLM limiter and the WENO3
  weights, the Roe flux with its entropy fix and HLLE fallback, the
  geometric and gravitational sources and the division by the cell volume,
  rounded to double once, at the row. One source text
  (\texttt{hydrodynamic\_rows\_body.inc}, 1111 lines, kind-generic) instantiated
  twice by \texttt{hydrodynamic\_rows.f90}; the composition, the heating and the
  cooling stay double and enter converted exactly. \textbf{The double
  instantiation is the production operator BIT FOR BIT}, asserted three
  ways: a suite row on a stated grid (largest difference exactly zero in
  \texttt{dF}, \texttt{S} and the four face states, tolerance 0), and two whole
  40-iteration reloads under \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_QUAD=2} -\/- the atomic (all 355
  \texttt{(JFNK)} lines identical) and the carrier, which is the molecular one
  and so exercises the generic caloric EOS (all 364 identical). The
  quadruple instantiation reaches the analytic mass row of a uniform state
  to 1e-30 before its rounding, where the double one stands at its own.
  \textbf{Verdict: the floor is confirmed and is NOT removed.} On the atomic
  reload at outer 1 the tracked row\textquotesingle s non-smoothness floor falls only
  1.240e-11 to 5.917e-12, and cell by cell it lands on
  \texttt{epsilon\_DOUBLE\ x\ face\ state\ x\ r\^{}2/dV} -\/- ratio 0.93 to 1.4 over the base
  cells against 5.7 to 8.9 in the control -\/- and not on the quadruple
  bound, which is 1.224e-30, 4.8e18 below what is measured. So N33\textquotesingle s
  amplifier (the face cancellation 3.2e5 times the \texttt{r\^{}2/dV} 5.1e3) is
  confirmed a second way and its attribution completed: four fifths of the
  floor is the arithmetic INSIDE the assembly, the rest is the double
  representation of what the assembly is handed and hands back -\/- the
  ghosts \texttt{Apply\_BC} writes, the sweep\textquotesingle s temperature and particle count,
  and the double row itself -\/- which precision inside one operator cannot
  reach. The detector isolates no step at any of the ten windows (control:
  28 of 64 and 61 of 64 at the two narrowest), but the second difference is
  still flat over 5.4 decades and the row still changes sign 39 times in
  64 intervals: a staircase with a smaller rise. \textbf{What the quadruple-precision accumulation does buy
  is the fidelity of the Krylov model}: the additivity defect falls
  8.174e-6 to 1.009e-6 with the exponent in the arc still negative
  (-1.063 to -0.901, so still rounding and not curvature), and at the
  binding iterate of the atomic reload at cap 40 the relative gap of the
  Arnoldi image falls 1.188e-1 to 3.813e-3, a factor 31, the returned
  relative residual then being the true one to four digits (0.2114 against
  0.2114; the control returned 0.1122 for a true 0.1617). The element rows,
  which do not pass through the assembly, are unmoved to four digits
  (C 1.367e-13 to 1.366e-13), which is the control inside the control.
  \textbf{The Newton does not converge and the Krylov cycle still stalls.}
  Atomic cap 40, quad accumulation on: 40 of 40 products at 0.21 against the requested
  0.10, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 8.413e-1 handed back against the control\textquotesingle s 1.112 with
  trajectories parting at iteration 9 (the solve is chaotic at the ulp level,
  so that is not by itself a result). Atomic cap 250: both stop on the
  stagnation detector, control at iteration 167 at 3.047e-4, quad at 136 at
  4.096e-4, both with 40 of 40 at 0.983 to 0.989, both binding on the
  element Fe rows of cells 132 to 137, both NOT CERTIFIED with 11 entries
  of the inventory refusing. Carrier cap 40: unchanged in every named
  outcome -\/- binding row the H2 carrier row of cell 205 throughout, 40 of
  40 at 0.9886 against 0.9729, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 9.336e-2 against 9.427e-2 -\/- as it
  must be, since that row is assembled by \texttt{carrier\_steady\_residual} and
  never sees the flux. Cost MEASURED: the flux assembly alone 46x at 8
  threads and 74x at one (500 cells, 3.628e-5 s to 1.681e-3 s), the whole
  atomic reload at cap 40 1.11x (271 s to 302 s), because a stationary
  residual is mostly the ionization sweep. \textbf{Nothing adopted, no default
  changed}: \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} bitwise but the provenance timestamp,
  \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30 on both; the atomic and carrier reloads identical
  line for line with the option off. Tests: \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} RED on the
  control objects, GREEN 232/0 with 12 rows added; \texttt{steady\_species\_rows}
  194/0, \texttt{element\_operator} 14/0, \texttt{certification} 57/0, \texttt{energy\_update}
  53/0, \texttt{acceptance\_classes} 22/0, \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} 53/0,
  \texttt{species\_face\_flux} 1/0, \texttt{spectrum\_type} 154/0, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 146/0,
  \texttt{attempted\_step} 70/0, \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} 30/0,
  \texttt{residual\_determinism} 5/1 with the same row failing by design and the
  same 3.354e+01 on the control binary. One file outside the brief\textquotesingle s list:
  \texttt{caloric\_eos.f90} gained three read-only accessors (the mixture ratios
  and the H2 rovibrational table nodes) with no arithmetic and no behavior
  change, because the generic pipeline cannot evaluate the molecular
  caloric EOS without them; the molecular whole-run bitwise comparison is
  the evidence that they changed nothing. Noticed, not fixed:
  \texttt{backup/regression/atomic\_elem\_newton/README.md} still names the helium
  row of cell 246 as the binding row at cap 250 from N26/N27, where the
  measurement on this tree is the element Fe rows of cells 132 to 137 at
  iteration 167 and \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 3.047e-4. \textbf{For decision 23}: extra precision
  confirms the diagnosis and does not cure it; the amplifier has to go
  (well-balanced flux differencing, or a coarser base cell, which buys one
  factor of the width), and even with the hydrodynamic rows made
  model-faithful the two rows that actually bind -\/- the element rows of the
  atomic reload and the H2 carrier row of the carrier reload -\/- are not
  hydrodynamic rows and are untouched by any of it. Advisor: rebuild on \texttt{build\_lwv} (the new \texttt{hydrodynamic\_rows.f90} in \texttt{SRC}); \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 232/0, \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0, \texttt{energy\_update} 53/0; atomic reload at cap 40 with every hook off, 8 threads: the N31 ladder to the digit (7.607e-2 at 40, handed back 5.377e-2). The \texttt{caloric\_eos.f90} accessors (read-only, outside the brief\textquotesingle s list) accepted on the molecular reload\textquotesingle s bitwise evidence. Accepted; nothing adopted. Decision 23 restated with this result: option (b) retired as a remedy, (a) still the right discretization but not by itself the cure of the species-row solve; the atomic fixture README gained a 2026-09-10 state section (binding rows Fe of cells 132 to 137 at cap 250 on this tree). \textbf{Next item N35} (runs without a decision): the conditioning of the preconditioned operator on the element and carrier rows at the binding iterate, and what the banded preconditioner misses there.
\item
  \textbf{N35} (what holds the Krylov cycle on the species rows once the
  rounding floor is removed: the conditioning of the preconditioned
  operator and what the banded preconditioner misses). Three default-off
  measurement hooks on the linear system of one outer iteration, adopting
  nothing: \texttt{EXHALE\_KRYLOV\_SIZE\_SCAN=1} (\texttt{{[}diag\ 16{]}}) reruns the SAME cycle
  at 40, 80, 160 and 320 products and the last two again with the basis
  orthogonalized twice, with \texttt{EXHALE\_GM\_HISTORY=1} printing the reduced
  residual after every product and the true one every twenty;
  \texttt{EXHALE\_PRECOND\_SPECTRUM=1} (\texttt{{[}diag\ 15{]}}) takes 200 Arnoldi products of
  \texttt{A\_z\ M\^{}-1} from a deterministic start, diagonalizes the Hessenberg
  (\texttt{dgeev}) and reports the Ritz values of the whole operator and of its
  compressions onto the species and the hydrodynamic rows, with the
  localization of the three smallest Ritz vectors;
  \texttt{EXHALE\_BAND\_DIFFERENCE=1} (\texttt{{[}diag\ 17{]}}) measures \texttt{(A\ -\ A\_band)\ v} on
  those Ritz vectors and on the first two Arnoldi directions, splits the
  columns they live on into the part inside the band, the band\textquotesingle s
  disagreement there and the part outside, with the radiation live and
  frozen, and reads the binding row\textquotesingle s band row by class as a fraction of its
  diagonal. \textbf{Verdict: the band misses nothing, and the operator itself is
  near-singular on the species rows.} Its action agrees with the full
  finite-difference one to 4.0e-5 to 1.5e-3 relative on every direction
  tested on both fixtures; the columns hold 99.4 to 100.0 percent of their
  norm inside the band (the largest outside part is 0.6 percent, \texttt{element\ He\ of\ cell\ 308}); and what remains is not on the rows that bind at all,
  but 0.999 in the momentum row of the LAST cell (carrier) and 0.83 to
  0.89 momentum plus 0.11 to 0.17 energy in five to seven cells (atomic),
  the element and carrier rows taking 0.0000 to 0.003 of it. So the two
  remedies the brief conditioned on -\/- a rank-few radiation correction by
  the Woodbury identity, or a wider \texttt{kl\_jac} -\/- would each repair something
  already right, and \textbf{nothing was built}. \textbf{What IS wrong, MEASURED at
  the binding iterate of both fixtures.} (1) The preconditioned operator
  is well conditioned on the hydrodynamic rows and near-singular on the
  species rows: the Ritz spectrum of the compression onto the hydrodynamic
  rows has a magnitude ratio 43.4 (carrier, \texttt{carrier\ H2\ of\ cell\ 205}
  binding) and 158 (atomic, \texttt{energy\ of\ cell\ 52} binding), none and three of
  200 below 1e-2; onto the species rows, 6.56e5 (carrier, smallest
  1.493e-6, nineteen below 1e-2, one below 1e-4) and 1.06e4 (atomic,
  smallest 9.366e-5). The smallest Ritz vector is 0.71 to 0.91 carrier rows
  on the front cells 199 to 205 and 458 to 466 (carrier) and 0.9955 element
  rows over 145 cells (atomic), and the carrier fixture\textquotesingle s near-zero part is
  a complex pair, not a real null direction. The attribution is there from
  outer 1 (2.17e5 against 39.6). And the discretization statement behind
  it: the band row of \texttt{carrier\ H2\ of\ cell\ 205} has diagonal 1.2396e-1
  against off-diagonal magnitudes 4.1026e2, \textbf{3.304e3 times the diagonal to
  the hydrodynamic unknowns of its own stencil and 5.65 times to the
  neighboring carriers}, so the binding row\textquotesingle s own unknown is three
  decades below its coupling to another class and there is no diagonal for
  a preconditioner to stand on. (2) \textbf{A larger subspace makes the step
  worse, not better}, which is neither of the brief\textquotesingle s two alternatives.
  The reduced least-squares residual falls monotonically with the size
  while the TRUE residual of the returned step stops falling at 60 to 80
  products and rises: atomic with quad accumulation on, reduced 2.658e-1,
  1.781e-1, 1.144e-1, 9.958e-2 at 40, 80, 160 and 182 products against TRUE
  2.851e-1, 2.575e-1, 3.006e-1, 3.203e-1 -\/- the cycle announces its
  tolerance reached at 182 and hands back a step leaving 0.32, three times
  what was asked; carrier, reduced 9.677e-1, 8.001e-1, 4.639e-1, 1.434e-1
  against TRUE 9.731e-1, 8.638e-1, 9.668e-1, \textbf{1.472e+00}. With quad
  accumulation off the true residuals are a factor 3 to 6 worse at every size
  (atomic 7.856e-1, 3.905e-1, 7.580e-1, 2.004e+00) and the shape is the
  same. Reorthogonalizing the basis changes nothing at any size, the
  measured loss of orthogonality being 0.000e+00, and the
  Arnoldi image of each column at the same iterates is exact to 3.30e-16 and 3.77e-16: the
  recursion is not the defect. What fails is additivity -\/- the action of
  the sum of the first two directions departs from the sum of their actions
  by 1.357e-3 (atomic) and 7.601e-5 (carrier), the assembled 40-product
  step\textquotesingle s image by 1.068e-1 and 2.256e-1 -\/- because the matrix-free action
  is the secant of a nonlinear residual over a fixed arc and the step it is
  extrapolated to grows from 0.31 to 11.4 while the arc stays 5.2e-6. Tests:
  \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 251/0 with 19 rows added (RED on the control objects,
  which do not carry the routines), among them the Ritz values of a stated
  upper triangular 6 by 6 spanning six decades reproduced to 1.171e-12 with
  the recursion carried to the dimension of the space, the column split
  summing to the whole difference with an empty band and with an exact one,
  and the four hooks off by default; \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0,
  \texttt{element\_operator} 14/0 (the three suites whose driver links
  \texttt{steady\_newton}). Default path: the atomic reload at cap 40 with every
  hook off, new binary against my control, all 354 \texttt{(JFNK)} lines
  IDENTICAL through \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ 5.377E-02,\ flux\ spread\ 3.555E-03}, the N31
  ladder to the digit; \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} bitwise but the provenance
  timestamp, \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30 on both. Cost MEASURED: the size ladder is
  1082 products per diagnostic iteration (the carrier reload at cap 40 from
  about 3.5 to about 9 minutes at 8 threads), the spectrum 600 and the band
  difference about 15. Noticed, not fixed: the frozen-radiation column of
  \texttt{element\ He\ of\ cell\ 410} carries 7.267e-4 of its 2.816e-1 norm outside
  the band where the WENO3 stencil says it should carry none, 0.26 percent
  and two decades below anything in this item\textquotesingle s conclusions; the likely
  route is the base boundary state recomputed from the iterate, not
  confirmed. Also corrected in the reading: the preconditioner is
  \texttt{build\_banded\_jac\_full}, a coloring of the FULL residual, not of the
  frozen one -\/- \texttt{build\_banded\_jac} on \texttt{frozen\_residual} is called only by
  the standalone check in \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} -\/- so "inside the band" carries
  the coloring\textquotesingle s contamination by the non-local response, and the frozen
  column is a separate control rather than the band\textquotesingle s own approximation.
  \textbf{For the advisor}: the next question is not the preconditioner. The
  species rows\textquotesingle{} row scaling (the binding row\textquotesingle s diagonal 3.3e3 below its
  hydrodynamic coupling is set by the certification row scale of decision
  20 a divided by the column scale, and using one scale for the merit and
  another for the model is a decision); the cycle has an optimal length
  around 60 to 80 products on both fixtures and the step control asks for a
  tolerance the operator does not honor; and \texttt{gm\_resid\_rel} is verified
  against the operator only at a breakdown or a rank loss, where the
  measurement says it needs verifying whenever the cycle ran more than a
  few tens of products. Advisor: rebuild on \texttt{build\_lwv}; \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 251/0, \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0; atomic reload at cap 40 with every hook off, 8 threads: the N31 ladder to the digit (7.607e-2 at 40, handed back 5.377e-2). Accepted; deliverable 4 correctly not built. The two levers named are the LINEAR model\textquotesingle s row scaling (a freedom decision 20 a did not fix: the merit and gate keep the certification scales, the linear inner product may differ) and a Krylov cycle that returns the step its TRUE residual chooses (the optimum lies at 60 to 80 products; the reduced residual lies past it). \textbf{Next item N36}: both as measured options, default off.
\item
  \textbf{N36} (the two levers N35 named: the row scaling of the LINEAR model,
  separate from the merit\textquotesingle s, and a Krylov cycle that returns the step its
  TRUE residual chooses). Two default-off options on the linear solve,
  adopting nothing. \texttt{EXHALE\_MODEL\_ROW\_EQUIL=1}
  (\texttt{unit\_infinity\_norm\_row\_scaling\_of\_the\_band}) forms the diagonal \texttt{E}
  that brings every row of the banded model to unit infinity norm as a
  power of two, applies it to the band that is factored and, inside
  \texttt{pgmres}, to the right-hand side and to every action of the operator,
  and maps the image and the relative residual BACK to the certification
  scales at every exit, so that the merit, the gate, the trust region\textquotesingle s
  predicted decrease and the certification keep the scales decision 20 a
  fixed and only the linear model\textquotesingle s inner product changes; \texttt{ab} itself is
  untouched, so the merit\textquotesingle s gradient is where it was.
  \texttt{EXHALE\_GM\_TRUE\_RESIDUAL=1} makes the cycle measure its candidate step
  against the operator from 20 products on, every 10 and at every point
  where it would otherwise end, keep the best, and stop on two consecutive
  rises or on the tolerance IN THE TRUE NORM; what comes back is that
  step, its own image from the product that measured it, and the true
  residual as \texttt{gm\_resid\_rel}, under a new eighth outcome
  \texttt{gm\_true\_residual\_turned}. \textbf{Verdict: both options work as specified, both
  fail the acceptance, both stay off.} (1) \textbf{The row scaling is a real
  freedom and taking it is worse.} The cycle then minimizes a functional
  the merit does not read, and the two disagree by a factor 1.2 to 673
  MEASURED (\texttt{{[}row\ equil{]}}, equilibrated against certification: atomic
  9.956e-2 / 4.111e-1 at outer 2 and 9.937e-2 / 1.218e-1 at outer 40;
  carrier 2.353e-1 / 5.730e+1 and 1.286e-1 / 8.657e+1), so the cycle
  announces the 1e-1 it was asked for while handing back a step that
  leaves 86.6. It cannot touch what binds: the preconditioned operator
  \texttt{A\_eq\ M\_eq\^{}-1\ =\ E\ (A\ B\^{}-1)\ E\^{}-1} is SIMILAR to the unequilibrated one,
  and the measurement at the same iterate agrees (hydrodynamic Ritz ratio
  39.62 against 39.60 carrier, 57.02 against 56.36 atomic; the count of
  species-row Ritz values below 1e-2 unchanged at 18 and 2), while the
  banded factorization is bit for bit the same matrix because
  \texttt{equilibrate\_the\_preconditioner} already equilibrates both sides; and a
  row scaling leaves every ratio WITHIN a row where it was, so the binding
  carrier row\textquotesingle s diagonal 1.2396e-1 against 4.1026e2 of coupling, 3.304e3
  times the diagonal to the hydrodynamic unknowns of its own stencil, is
  exactly N35\textquotesingle s number still. What it costs: the carrier reload at cap 40
  goes from \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 9.572e-2, info=1, binding \texttt{carrier\ H2\ of\ cell\ 205} to
  1.955, \textbf{info=2 with four entries of the inventory refusing}, and the
  true residual of the returned step at 40 products from 0.973 to 1.050e2.
  (2) \textbf{The true-residual cycle is the right instrument and it does not
  turn the solve.} At the binding iterate it caps the cycle where the
  step stops improving: on the ladder 40/80/160/320 the control\textquotesingle s returned
  step gets WORSE with size (atomic TRUE 2.851e-1, 2.575e-1, 3.006e-1,
  3.203e-1; carrier 9.731e-1, 8.638e-1, 9.668e-1, \textbf{1.472e+00}) while the
  true-residual cycle stops at 80 and at 120 products and returns 2.660e-1 and 8.244e-1,
  never worse than the control\textquotesingle s best at any size and at fewer products.
  Two cycles of the atomic reload at cap 40 name the new outcome. But the
  best true residual any subspace reaches is 0.27 and 0.82, the Newton
  does not descend past its plateau, and \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} handed back at cap 40 is
  1.060 against the control\textquotesingle s 5.377e-2 (atomic; the trajectory parts at
  iteration 15, the first cycle to pass 20 products) and IDENTICAL on the
  carrier, where a subspace of 40 always makes the last candidate the best
  and the option changes only the number reported (true 9.870e-1 against
  reduced 9.839e-1). \textbf{Acceptance, by the named clauses}: the true
  residual below 0.1 within 80 products on both fixtures fails for both
  options (option 1 atomic 8.79e-2 but carrier 7.51e+1; option 2 atomic 2.66e-1,
  carrier 8.64e-1), and the refusal count at cap 40 does not fall by half
  (atomic 9 to 10 for both options; carrier 8 to 7 and 8 to 8), with no new
  refusal class and no new \texttt{gm\_no\_direction\_sampled} anywhere. Corrected
  mid-item and re-measured: the first true-residual cycle left the reduced
  tolerance in place as a stop and so discarded a step it never measured
  (carrier outer 1, the 80-product cycle ended at product 49 and handed
  back product 40\textquotesingle s step at TRUE 1.259e-1 where product 49 reaches
  9.724e-2); with the reduced residual no longer ending a cycle, the
  numbers above are the corrected cycle\textquotesingle s. Tests: \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} RED on
  the control objects (nine symbols absent), GREEN 264/0 with 13 rows
  added, among them the equilibrated solve returning the same step as the
  unequilibrated one to 9.90e-16 on a stated dense system solved over the
  whole space, the equilibrated cycle reporting the CERTIFICATION residual
  of a truncated solve to eight digits, and a stated operator whose action
  stops being one linear map after 40 products, on which the cycle names
  \texttt{gm\_true\_residual\_turned} and stops before its subspace is spent;
  \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0, \texttt{element\_operator} 14/0,
  \texttt{residual\_determinism} 5/1 with the same row failing by design and the
  same measured 3.354e+01. \textbf{Default path}: the atomic reload at cap 40
  with every hook off, all 354 \texttt{(JFNK)} lines IDENTICAL to my control
  through \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ 5.377E-02,\ flux\ spread\ 3.555E-03}; the carrier reload all
  364 identical; \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} bitwise but the provenance timestamp,
  \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30. With the options ON \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} stays CERTIFIED:
  option 1 \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 3.043e-9 against 1.667e-9 with a largest relative movement
  5.2e-10 in the output, option 2 bitwise in \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} with 8 extra
  residual evaluations, its cycles never reaching the 20 products the
  needs. \textbf{For the advisor}: what the two options rule out is the linear
  algebra. The norm the cycle minimizes is free and wrong to change; the
  length of the cycle is free and already at its optimum; the operator\textquotesingle s
  species block is near-singular at every scaling and the step it can
  produce leaves 0.27 to 0.82 of the residual whatever the subspace. The
  remaining lever is the one N35 named and N34 confirmed the arithmetic
  cannot reach: the DISCRETIZATION of the rows that bind. Advisor: rebuild on \texttt{build\_lwv}; \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 264/0, \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0; atomic reload at cap 40 with every hook off, 8 threads: the N31 ladder to the digit (7.607e-2 at 40, handed back 5.377e-2). Accepted; both options stay off. \textbf{The linear algebra is exhausted as a lever} (N31 to N36: probe rule, inner tolerances, extended precision, band coverage, row scaling of the linear model, cycle length). STOPPED here for the user: decision 23, and the next question is the discretization of the species rows that bind at the front (the carrier row of cell 205 whose own diagonal is 3.3e3 below its hydrodynamic coupling). \texttt{code\_status\_20260910.md} section 5 task 1, ISSUES 3.1 and 5, the handoff and decision 23 restated accordingly.
\item
  \textbf{N37} (decision 23 (a): a well-balanced flux difference for the
  near-hydrostatic layer, as the default-off option \texttt{Well\ balanced:}).
  With the key on the reconstruction, the Riemann jumps and the pressure
  force carry the DEPARTURE from the cell\textquotesingle s own local hydrostatic
  equilibrium instead of the state: within cell j that equilibrium is the
  constant-density one through its own (rho\_j, p\_j),
  \texttt{p\_eq,j(r)\ =\ p\_j\ -\ rho\_j\ (phi(r)\ -\ phi(r\_j))}, which assumes no thermal,
  entropy or compositional stratification and so preserves ours (Kaeppeli
  and Mishra 2016, A\&A 587, A94, sections 2.1.1 and 2.1.3; the momentum
  source as the face-pressure difference of the cell\textquotesingle s own equilibrium is
  their 2014 paper, J. Comput. Phys. 259, 199, eq. 2.26). The pressure
  stencil acts on the neighbor\textquotesingle s pressure measured against that
  equilibrium continued THROUGH the face (this cell\textquotesingle s density up to the
  face, the neighbor\textquotesingle s beyond it), which is what vanishes on the discrete
  equilibrium; the two cell pressures are differenced first, while that is
  exact, and the hydrostatic terms added to the difference. The momentum
  row is then assembled from the face pressure measured against the same
  equilibrium and \texttt{source} returns \texttt{S(2)\ =\ 0}: with
  \texttt{A+\ P\_up\ -\ A-\ P\_dn\ -\ (A+\ -\ A-)\ p\_j\ =\ -rho\_j\ {[}A+\ (phi\_i(j)\ -\ phi\_c(j))\ +\ A-\ (phi\_c(j)\ -\ phi\_i(j-1)){]}} identically, the equilibrium\textquotesingle s flux
  difference and its gravitational source cancel in the ALGEBRA and the
  cell\textquotesingle s own O(1) pressure never enters the row. Mirrored in the
  kind-generic copy, so \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_QUAD=2} is the production operator
  bit for bit under both key values (suite row, largest difference exactly
  zero, tolerance 0). \textbf{Exact preservation, MEASURED}: on the scheme\textquotesingle s own
  discrete equilibrium (the isothermal solution of the face-matching
  condition, the spherical form of their eq. 42) the momentum row falls
  from 7.0e-5 (PLM) and 2.5e-3 (WENO3) of the local weight rho g at
  N = 250 to 4.1e-14 and 3.9e-14, and stays at 4.5e-14 to 5.5e-14 at
  N = 500, 1000, 2000 while the base scheme falls at its design order; the
  mass and energy rows reach 1e-16 against 1e-8. Twelve new assertions in
  \texttt{hydrostatic\_residual}, plus eleven rows in \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} (with no
  gravity the well-balanced option is the base scheme to 8e-16 on a uniform state and to
  1.4e-16 on a Sod tube, for both reconstructions; and the same exactness
  on a second grid and geometry). \textbf{What it does NOT reach, and this was
  written into the design before the code}: N33\textquotesingle s non-smoothness floor
  does not move (energy row of cell 1, 1.009e-11 with the option against
  1.046e-11 without; cells 84, 167, 251 the same to a factor 1.3; six to
  ten times \texttt{epsilon\ x\ face\ state\ x\ r\^{}2/dV} either way), because a cell
  pressure is itself a rounded O(1) double and every jump built from two of
  them steps by its last bit whatever the grouping. What does move is the
  additivity defect at ten times the probe arc, 2.410e-7 with the option
  against 3.151e-5 without. \textbf{The stationary solves are worse with the key
  on, and the reason is not the option}: \texttt{store\_row\_terms} scales the
  momentum row by \texttt{max(\textbar{}dF(2)\textbar{},\ \textbar{}S(2)\textbar{},\ \textbar{}Smom\textbar{})}, the option returns
  \texttt{S(2)\ =\ 0}, so the row IS its own scale and every normalized momentum
  residual is exactly 1 (MEASURED on the carrier reload as loaded: k=2 is
  \texttt{1.000000E+00} with the option against 1.954 without, while the mass row is
  the same number to seven digits and the energy row to four). The merit
  then carries no momentum information: \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}Fs\textbar{}\textbar{}2} rises from 16.5 to 28.1
  at the same state, and 28.1\^{}2 - 16.5\^{}2 = 517 is the 500 momentum rows
  pinned at unity. The atomic reload at cap 40 reaches 1.415 against the
  control\textquotesingle s 1.112 and the carrier reload 1.732 against 9.34e-2; at cap 250
  the option\textquotesingle s solve aborts at iteration 61 on "no descent step in 12

  \texttt{\textbar{}rho\_j\ (A+\ (phi\_i(j)\ -\ phi\_c(j))\ +\ A-\ (phi\_c(j)\ -\ phi\_i(j-1)))\textbar{}/dV},
  which is two multiplications from what \texttt{RK\_rhs} already holds but sits in

Advisor: rebuild on \texttt{build\_lwv}; \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 279/0, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 162/0 (four rows FAIL when the shell rows are pointed at the stale shared \texttt{EXHALE.x}: pass \texttt{EXHALE\_EXE} with \texttt{EXHALE\_OBJDIR}), \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 194/0, \texttt{certification} 57/0, \texttt{element\_operator} 14/0, \texttt{residual\_determinism} the same single row FAIL by design; atomic reload at cap 40 with every hook off and the key absent, 8 threads: the N31 ladder to the digit (7.607e-2 at 40, handed back 5.377e-2). The regression comparison strips comment lines, so the new \texttt{wellbal} token in the \texttt{\#\ options} header is invisible to \texttt{make\ check}. Accepted as a default-off option; the momentum-row reference scale in \texttt{store\_row\_terms} (the option\textquotesingle s \texttt{S(2)\ =\ 0} makes the row its own scale) is recorded in ISSUES 3.7 as the prerequisite of any default-on decision; the publisher PDFs of the two Kappeli and Mishra papers remain wanted (the institutional versions were read in full).
\item
  \textbf{N38} (the discretized element and carrier rows at the front: why the row
  that binds is nearly independent of its own unknown). One default-off
  measurement hook, adopting nothing. \texttt{EXHALE\_FRONT\_ROW=1} (\texttt{{[}diag\ 18{]}})
  takes the species row that carries the largest scaled residual and its two
  neighbors and prints, at the selected outer iteration: every TERM of the
  row on its certification scale, with the advective term split per FACE by

  exactly zero where the mass flux it multiplies is, so the masked call
  leaves one face standing alone and the SAME operator is used, never a
  copy); the reconstructed face fraction read back out of that masked
  divergence beside the donor cell\textquotesingle s own; the chemical source of a carrier
  from the sweep\textquotesingle s own rates; the band\textquotesingle s entries for the row, to its own
  unknown, to the same unknown in the neighbors and to every hydrodynamic
  unknown of the three cells, each as a number and as a multiple of the
  diagonal; those same entries SPLIT BY TERM, by one-sided differences of
  the row\textquotesingle s own terms along the same column with the same step
  \texttt{build\_banded\_jac\_full} uses; the cell Peclet number, the logarithmic
  gradient and the chemical against the advective time scale; and the column
  scale of every unknown of the binding cell beside the unknown itself.
  \textbf{Verdict: the binding row\textquotesingle s diagonal is NOT small -\/- it is the size of the
  row -\/- and the 3.3e3 of N35 is a CHOICE OF COLUMN SCALE that the Krylov
  cycle cannot see.} (1) \textbf{Every hydrodynamic entry of the row is its
  ADVECTIVE term}, on all three fronts measured: \texttt{carrier\ H2\ of\ cell\ 205}
  (carrier reload, outer 40) \texttt{momentum\ of\ cell\ 205} -7.40478e+1 of which the
  diffusive part is 2.0e-5 and the chemical -2.3e-5; \texttt{element\ He\ of\ cell\ 191}
  (atomic, outer 1) \texttt{momentum\ of\ cell\ 190} -1.86758e+4 with a diffusive part

  rows N34 named) \texttt{momentum\ of\ cell\ 136} -1.25980e+4 with 2.0e-6. Summed
  over the reported stencil the hydrodynamic columns carry \textbf{3389, 4176 and
  2424 diagonals} and the neighboring species unknowns 5.855, 1.052 and
  1.005, which is N35\textquotesingle s 3.304e3 and 5.646 reproduced. Each species
  sub-block is meanwhile a clean diffusive tridiagonal (15.385/-7.772/-7.612
  helium, 17.432/-8.861/-8.564 iron): N27\textquotesingle s reading of an interior element
  row holds at the front too, and it is the wind columns that bury it. (2)
  \textbf{The size of that coupling is one over the Mach number}, because the
  momentum column scale is \texttt{\textbar{}rho\ v\textbar{}\ +\ rho\ c\_s}, the SOUND speed in a deeply
  subsonic layer: MEASURED \texttt{scale\ over\ unknown} \textbf{3.839e+2} (carrier front,
  momentum 6.83274e-5 against scale 2.62332e-2), \textbf{1.361e+5} (helium front)
  and \textbf{5.149e+4} (iron front), against 1.000 for the mass, the energy and
  every species unknown. The arithmetic closes to a factor 1.4 to 1.6: one
  face carries -0.5322 of the carrier row and 383.9 x 0.5322 = 204 against
  the measured 129; one face carries -0.1969 of the helium row and
  1.361e5 x 0.1969 = 2.68e4 against the measured 1.87e4. (3) \textbf{The three
  other candidates are measured and are not the cause.} The species column
  scale\textquotesingle s reservoir floor is INACTIVE everywhere it was looked at (the
  carrier unknown is \texttt{ln\ n} with column scale exactly 1.0, the default; the
  helium and iron unknowns\textquotesingle{} scales are their own magnitudes to five digits),
  so raising or lowering \texttt{element\_column\_scale\_floor} by a decade changes the
  diagonal by nothing. A stiff advective/chemical cancellation in the
  diagonal is a factor 1.8 and not decades (carrier diagonal 1.19228e-1 =
  diffusive +2.17367e-1 and advective -9.89614e-2, with the chemical
  derivative +9.44223e-4, 0.8 percent, although the chemical time scale is
  220 times the advective one), and there is no cancellation at all in the
  helium diagonal. The limiter is a live switch (the reconstructed face
  fractions stand at 0.587 and 0.657 of the donor at the carrier front, and
  frozen at first order the advective part of the diagonal changes SIGN,
  -9.89614e-2 to +2.50409e-1) but it moves the ratio by less than 2, because
  freezing it raises the momentum coupling by the same kind of factor. N5\textquotesingle s
  two-root front was not re-run: cell 205 is not one. (4) \textbf{The test that
  settles it}: the existing default-off column equilibration
  (\texttt{EXHALE\_COLUMN\_EQUIL=1}) is exactly the remedy branch (i) names, and
  MEASURED at outer 1, where both runs stand at the same iterate, it brings
  the binding row\textquotesingle s hydrodynamic coupling from 3389 to \textbf{0.993} (carrier)
  and 4176 to \textbf{2.926} (atomic) while the Krylov cycle returns the SAME step
  at the SAME residual to every printed digit (carrier 40 of 40, reduced
  1.257e-1, TRUE 1.259e-1; atomic 8 of 40, 7.821e-2, TRUE 7.824e-2) and the
  species-row Ritz count below 1e-2 is unchanged at 18 and 2. It cannot be
  otherwise: a column scaling \texttt{E} is applied to the operator and to the band
  built FROM it, and \texttt{(A\ E)(band(A)\ E)\^{}-1\ =\ A\ band(A)\^{}-1} IDENTICALLY. N36
  showed a row scaling is a similarity; this shows a column scaling is an
  identity, so \textbf{both sides of the scaling are now exhausted}. What the column equilibration
  costs, since it also reshapes the trust-region ball and the probe steps:

  and 5.377e-2. Acceptance by the named clauses: the Ritz ratio below 1e3

Accepted; deliverable 3 correctly not built. N35\textquotesingle s sentence "no diagonal for a preconditioner to stand on" is withdrawn: the diagonal is the size of the row, the 3.3e3 is the momentum column scale (the sound speed) against a layer Mach number of 1e-3 to 1e-5, and column scalings are invisible to the cycle, so the near-singularity of the species-row compression is a fact of its own.

\subsection{8. PLAN\_20260911: the partitioned stationary route (2026-09-11)}\label{8-plan_20260911-the-partitioned-stationary-route-2026-09-11}

Plan of record: \texttt{docs/PLAN\_20260911\_partitioned\_solver.md}. User instruction
of 2026-09-11: read \texttt{docs/solver\_approach\_analysis\_20260910.md}, its review
\texttt{docs/solver\_approach\_analysis\_20260910\_review.md} and
\texttt{docs/solver\_partition\_experiment\_20260911.md}, correct the errors they
establish, run the solver experiments, and adopt the partitioned route in the
production solver if it measures better than the coupled species-row solve.
This reopens the stage-2 solver program that the 2026-09-10 instruction had
closed after N37 and N38. Items P1 to P9, then P10 to P12 on the same day\textquotesingle s
further instruction to fix the open items the plan had left to the user, then
P14 and P15 on the two findings of that afternoon\textquotesingle s Codex adversarial review
and P16 to P18 on the user\textquotesingle s two decisions of the same afternoon, then P20 and
P21 in the evening and the terminology sweep A1 to A4 on the user\textquotesingle s instruction
of that evening (P13, P19 and P22 are the record itself); the rules every worker
on this tree follows are \texttt{docs/worker\_rules.md}.

\subsubsection{The items}\label{the-items}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{P1 (defect D1): a failed element composition solve is no longer handed
  back as an update} (\texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90} +311/-23,
  \texttt{src/tests/element\_operator/element\_operator\_tests.f90} +190/-13; every line
  count of this section is MEASURED from \texttt{git\ diff\ -\/-numstat} on the tree at
  the close).
  \texttt{solve\_mass\_fraction} returns an explicit success result and no longer
  accepts the last halved trial of a line search that never descended;
  \texttt{element\_diffusion\_step} takes an optional status and, when asked,
  restores the composition it was handed unless the candidate is finite,
  inside \texttt{{[}0,1{]}} to \texttt{1e-12} before the range clip, and no farther from the
  density the step held fixed than the entry composition was, to \texttt{1e-10};
  \texttt{relax\_element\_composition} steps on a copy, discards a refused step,
  retries at half the length within a bounded budget and reports three
  outcomes by name. MEASURED (P1) on the archived D1 reproduction
  (\texttt{atomic\_elem\_newton}, species mode, omega 1): a returned composition
  missing rho by \texttt{1.768571764708571E-01}, a nonfinite chemical refresh and
  exit status 1 become the entry composition restored at
  \texttt{1.046888214610824E-15}, an admissible refresh at \texttt{1.10297235E-15} and
  exit status 0. The first inadmissible operation of that failure, which the
  experiment report left unlocated, is now named: the composition solve
  drives \texttt{X} out of \texttt{{[}0,1{]}} and the range clip used to absorb it
  (\texttt{element\_step\_out\_of\_bounds}), and the mass discrepancy is downstream of
  the clip. Tests: \texttt{element\_operator} 14/0 at entry, \textbf{24/0} after, \textbf{22/2}
