\item
  \textbf{P4: the momentum row\textquotesingle s reference scale under \texttt{Well\ balanced:\ True}}
  (\texttt{RK\_rhs.f90} +90/-2, \texttt{steady\_residual.f90} +59/-2,
  \texttt{src/tests/grid\_and\_gates/hydrostatic\_residual.f90} +318/-11, that suite\textquotesingle s
  \texttt{README.md} +44/-1). Under the well-balanced option \texttt{store\_row\_terms} scaled the momentum row by
  \texttt{max(\textbar{}dF(2)\textbar{},\ \textbar{}S(2)\textbar{},\ \textbar{}Smom\textbar{})} with \texttt{S(2)\ =\ 0} by construction, so every
  scaled momentum residual was exactly 1 (N37, ISSUES 3.7). The reference is
  now \texttt{max(\textbar{}dF\_2\textbar{},\ \textbar{}S\_2\textbar{},\ \textbar{}S\_visc\textbar{},\ equilibrium\_pressure\_force(j))}, the last
  term the magnitude of the pressure force of the cell\textquotesingle s own hydrostatic
  equilibrium, which is the right-hand side of whichever cancellation
  identity the assembled row belongs to (PLM
  \texttt{\textbar{}rho\_j\ {[}A+\ (phi\_i(j)\ -\ phi\_c(j))\ +\ A-\ (phi\_c(j)\ -\ phi\_i(j-1)){]}\textbar{}/dV}, WENO3
  \texttt{\textbar{}rho\_j\ (phi\_i(j)\ -\ phi\_i(j-1))\textbar{}/dr}; Kaeppeli and Mishra 2016, A\&A 587,
  A94, eq. 16 in spherical geometry). It has one definition,
  \texttt{equilibrium\_pressure\_force\_of\_state}, called by \texttt{RK\_rhs}, by
  \texttt{positivity\_limited\_fluxes} and by \texttt{assemble\_residual} (the last so that
  the kind-generic \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_QUAD} assembly, which returns the well-balanced
  row but not the term it is read against, does not scale the row by a stale
  weight). The numerator is untouched; with no gravity the term is zero and
  the scale is the row\textquotesingle s own terms exactly (MEASURED 0 for all four
  scheme/solver pairs). MEASURED (P4): \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} reloaded with the
  key ON is \textbf{NOT CERTIFIED} on the entry text on exactly one entry, the
  momentum row at \textbf{1.000E+00} at cell 1 (which reproduces the N37 record to
  the digit, spread 1.539E-15, energy 9.911E-12, mass 1.228E-13, Mdot 13.30),
  and \textbf{CERTIFIED} after, \texttt{info\ =\ 0}, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 9.719E-10, momentum row
  9.719E-10 against 1.0E-08 at cell 500, \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30. Tests:
  \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} \texttt{hydrostatic\_residual} \textbf{28 PASS / 12 FAIL} on the
  entry-text objects (the twelve failures at exactly 1.000 for all four
  pairs) and \textbf{40/0} after, of which 16 are new rows measuring the row
  divided by \texttt{residual\_row\_scale(2,...)}, the one expression the
  certification reads; the whole suite 162/0 at entry (READ, N37) and \textbf{178
  PASS / 0 FAIL} after; \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 279/0 and \texttt{certification} 57/0
  unchanged. Default path (key absent): \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} reload and
  \texttt{mol\_carrier} at 300 steps byte-identical on both binaries apart from the
  provenance line, every \texttt{(JFNK)} line identical, key-off \texttt{info\ =\ 0}, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}}
  1.667E-09, Mdot 13.30. The option\textquotesingle s effect on the two solver fixtures is large
  and in one case negative, and both are recorded: the carrier reload\textquotesingle s
  handed-back \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} falls from 1.721 to \textbf{1.026E-01}, the key-off level
  (READ: N37 9.34E-02), with a measured momentum row of 1.404E-02 at cell 499
  instead of a pinned 1; the atomic reload stops earlier (\texttt{info\ =\ 2} at
  iteration 24) and hands back 1.702 against the control\textquotesingle s 1.415 at cap 40,
  because the merit no longer carries 500 momentum rows pinned at one
  (\texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}Fs\textbar{}\textbar{}2} 17.1 against 28.2). A defect found while there and fixed: the
  allocation guard in \texttt{RK\_rhs} tested \texttt{face\_flux} for the well-balanced
  arrays \texttt{face\_q\_up}/\texttt{face\_q\_dn} as well, and \texttt{face\_flux}/\texttt{face\_p} are also
  allocated by \texttt{store\_the\_interface\_fluxes}, so a stationary evaluation
  reached before any marching stage could write an unallocated array; the
  guards are now separate and each tests its own array, with no arithmetic on
  any path.
\item
  \textbf{P5: the analysis document corrected} (\texttt{docs/solver\_approach\_analysis\_20260910.md},
  +463/-115, 114 to 592 lines). All nine corrections the review established
  are in place, each with an inline \texttt{{[}corrected\ 2026-09-11:\ ...{]}} mark citing
  the review section or the experiment measurement behind it: the spatial
  truncation error is not a lower bound on the algebraic residual (the
  frozen-background H2 wind row measures 2.449021e-13 on the same 500-cell
  grid where the withdrawn argument put a 5e-4 floor); Ritz-magnitude ratios
  of a masked compression are neither condition numbers nor the species Schur
  complement; partitioning moves the coupled iteration outside the Krylov
  solve with outer matrix \texttt{(1\ -\ omega)\ I\ +\ omega\ D\^{}-1\ C\ A\^{}-1\ B} and the outer
  loop is not new code (\texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion}); the CETIMB
  route is renamed as informed by CETIMB and gains the two conditions the
  review adds; the well-balanced option removes the equilibrium\textquotesingle s algebraic
  cancellation and not the floor; Koskinen et al. 2013 lower boundary is
  section 2.1.1; the three-unknown path uses its existing globalization and
  not the trust region (\texttt{use\_tr\ =\ (nspec\_row\ .gt.\ 0)}); section 7 recast with
  the 2026-09-11 numbers; a new section 9 indexing the twelve changes and a
  new section 5.5 on partitioned components inside a coupled method (Knoll
  and Keyes 2004, J. Comput. Phys. 193, 357). Three numbers of the original
  disagreed with their sources and are corrected, one of them wrong: the N4b
  row for the carrier reload read "1.4 -\textgreater{} 0.09" where the measurement is 0.151 -\textgreater{}
  0.090. Citations were verified independently in the published text
  (Koskinen 2013 section headers by \texttt{pdftotext\ -layout}; the Knoll and Keyes
  bibcode by the ADS API).
\item
  \textbf{P6: the outer iteration as a stated contract} (\texttt{src/EXHALE\_main.f90},
  +448/-131). \texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion} now says in its own header
  which equations are alternated with the wind and why (a species the run
  moves by an operator-split transport step, the diffusing elements always
  and the molecular carriers where \texttt{Coupled\ carrier\ solve} is off, has a
  stationary balance that no hydrodynamic solve touches), and it accepts a
  state only by the certification of the FULL set of active equations
  evaluated on the refreshed state it would hand back. A hydrodynamic
  \texttt{info\ =\ 0} while an alternated species row refuses is not an accepted
  state, and a nonzero hydrodynamic flag no longer ends the loop by itself,
  because both \texttt{info\ =\ 1} and \texttt{info\ =\ 2} hand back a state the composition
  update still makes progress from. The endings are named
  (\texttt{outer\_certified}, \texttt{outer\_state\_not\_finite},
  \texttt{outer\_element\_update\_refused}, \texttt{outer\_no\_progress}, \texttt{outer\_pass\_budget}),
  the P1 status is consumed, and the quantity the passes are controlled by is
  the worst gated species row of the certification: where it fails to fall
  over a pass both updates are shortened (the element under-relaxation omega
  halved, floor 0.125; the carrier movement bound halved, floor
  \texttt{carrier\_trust\_floor\ =\ 1.0d-3}) and \texttt{outer\_no\_fall\_max\ =\ 3} consecutive
  passes without a fall end the loop. The last pass takes no composition
  update, because an update exists to be consumed by the next hydrodynamic
  solve and taking it would replace a stationary wind by a state the update
  itself spoiled. The composition drift and the volume-weighted carrier
  residual are reported and are not tests. With no alternated species the
  body is one steady solve and the state refresh, and the flag is the solve\textquotesingle s
  own, which is what an atomic run and a coupled carrier solve both do.
  Default path MEASURED on the P6 run pair (\texttt{wasp\_full\_newton.ctrl} against
  \texttt{.new}): \texttt{info\ =\ 0}, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1.667E-09, flux spread 6.233E-14, CERTIFIED,
  \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30, and \texttt{output/Ion\_species.txt} and
  \texttt{output/Hydro\_ioniz.txt} byte-identical between the two.
\item

\item
  \textbf{P10: the best-iterate ledger reads the hydrodynamic rows against their own
  certification tolerances} (\texttt{steady\_newton.f90} +131/-55,
  \texttt{src/tests/krylov\_and\_dogleg/krylov\_and\_dogleg\_tests.f90} +135/-1, READ from
  the P10 report; the whole uncommitted diff of the two files now stands at
  +335/-84 and +268/-1, MEASURED by \texttt{git\ diff\ -\/-numstat}, P3\textquotesingle s share
  included). Items P10, P11 and P12 were added after the plan had run, on the
  user\textquotesingle s instruction of 2026-09-11 to fix the open items the session had left
  to the user ("fix all of them", and "the pressure term too"); P10 is the
  third entry of the plan\textquotesingle s "what remains".
  \texttt{distance\_from\_certification} divided the hydrodynamic slot by the run\textquotesingle s
  \texttt{Resid\ tol}, so the ledger that chooses the state handed back could not see
  a mass row above 3e-12 while \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} was below 1e-8. The slot now arrives as
  a distance formed by ONE new public routine,
  \texttt{hydrodynamic\_distance\_from\_certification}, \texttt{max(mass/3e-12,\ momentum/1e-8,\ energy/1e-6)} on the windows \texttt{resid\_relnorm} uses, so \texttt{d\ \textless{}\ 1} is exactly the
  row condition the loop-top stop of P3 and the acceptance at return impose,
  and \texttt{keep\_this\_iterate} ranks on that distance on every route including the
  three-unknown one (the \texttt{nspec\_row\ \textless{}=\ 0} branch that ranked on \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} is
  gone). \texttt{certified\_row\_measures} hands the plain \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} of the same
  evaluation back through a new optional argument rather than leaving a caller
  to re-derive it, and the iteration line prints the two side by side.
  \texttt{resid\_tol\_of\_solve} is KEPT, with one reader, the
  \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_BRANCH\_REPORT} block, where \texttt{rmax(1)} is deliberately the
  plain mixed-row measure and dividing it by one row\textquotesingle s tolerance would be a
  ratio of two different rows. Tests: \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 290/0 at entry,
  \textbf{300 PASS / 9 FAIL} on a RED build in which both distances read
  \texttt{maxval(rc)/1.0d-8} (the superseded reading, at the \texttt{Resid\ tol} of these
  fixtures), \textbf{309/0} after, the 19 new rows being the block
  \texttt{judged\_distance\_reads\_each\_row\_against\_its\_own\_tolerance}; \texttt{certification}
  57/0, \texttt{steady\_completion\_flag} 3/0, \texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual} 3/0
  unchanged. Impact MEASURED (P10) against a private build of the entry text
  of the one file: \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} reloaded ends \texttt{info\ =\ 0}, \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}}
  1.667E-09, \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30 with \textbf{all eleven output files
  byte-identical}, the ledger handing back the same iterate and the only
  number that moves being the reported best-iterate distance, 1.749E-01
  (which is \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\_best/1e-8}) to 8.469E-02 (the same iterate\textquotesingle s worst row over
  its own tolerance, so 8.5 percent of it). The carrier reload shows the
  re-ranking doing what it is for: pass 2 hands back a mass row of \textbf{4.85E-12
  at \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 2.459E-09} where the control took 6.98E-12 at 1.593E-09, and
  pass 3 \textbf{4.96E-12 at 2.005E-09} against 8.17E-12 at 1.723E-09, with the H2
  carrier row and the worst gated species row identical pass by pass because
  the carrier relaxation sits outside the hydrodynamic solve. The HD 209458 b
  element reload runs its twelve passes in 1064 s against the control\textquotesingle s 1043 s
  (2 percent on a shared machine, not a claim) with every one of the twelve
  worst gated species rows and its cell identical to the control, and ends on
  the same single refusing entry, the hydrodynamic mass row at \textbf{8.893E-10 at
  cell 3} against the control\textquotesingle s 1.054E-09 at cell 16; the solves reporting a
  stalled best iterate fall 5 to 3 and those ending on "no descent direction
  exists" 11 to 10. What stays open, and what this measurement settles: the
  mass row does NOT reach 3e-12 and that state still does not certify, because
  at 1e-9 to 1e-12 the row IS the N33 rounding of the base-layer flux
  difference. MEASURED (P10, finding 3): at pass 1 the ledger\textquotesingle s best distance
  is 4.407E+02, a mass row of 1.32E-09, while the same state re-evaluated at
  its own composition after the best-iterate restore carries \textbf{4.11E-09}, so
  ranking iterates by that row is ranking by the luckiest rounding of a
  quantity that moves when the state is evaluated again, which is why the
  pass-by-pass mass rows scatter rather than descend in the control and in the
  new run alike. The entry is an acceptance question, not a solver item, and
  no further solver item follows from it.
\item
  \textbf{P11: the momentum row\textquotesingle s reference scale is the largest PHYSICAL term of
  the momentum equation} (\texttt{RK\_rhs.f90}, \texttt{steady\_residual.f90},
  \texttt{src/tests/grid\_and\_gates/hydrostatic\_residual.f90} and that suite\textquotesingle s
  \texttt{README.md}; the whole uncommitted diff of the four files stands at +285/-2,
  +151/-33, +736/-12 and +94/-1, MEASURED by \texttt{git\ diff\ -\/-numstat}, with P4\textquotesingle s
  +90/-2, +59/-2, +318/-11 and +44/-1 inside it). The fourth entry of the
  plan\textquotesingle s "what remains", and the "pressure term" of the user\textquotesingle s instruction.
  The scale was built from the pieces of the discretization,
  \texttt{max(\textbar{}dF(2)\textbar{},\ \textbar{}S(2)\textbar{},\ \textbar{}Smom\textbar{})} plus P4\textquotesingle s equilibrium pressure force under
  the well-balanced key; it is now built from the three left-hand terms of
  \texttt{d(rho\ v)/dt\ +\ div(rho\ v\ v)\ +\ dp/dr\ +\ rho\ dphi/dr\ =\ S\_visc}, kept beside
  \texttt{face\_flux}/\texttt{face\_p} as module arrays of \texttt{RK\_integration}, filled on both
  reconstructions, under both key values and on the positivity-repair path,
  and gathered by one routine, \texttt{momentum\_row\_terms\_of\_cell}, with
  \texttt{momentum\_row\_terms\_of\_state} filling a whole state and
  \texttt{reconstruction\_continuation\_rhs} blending the three terms with the same
  \texttt{lambda} as \texttt{dF} and \texttt{S}. The residual \texttt{R(2)\ =\ dF(2)\ -\ S(2)\ -\ S\_visc} is
  untouched on every path, and the split is a rearrangement of the assembled
  row and not a model of it: \texttt{F(2)\ -\ p\_out} is the momentum flux without the
  pressure exactly for every flux function, and the geometric term is
  recovered from the source as \texttt{p\_geom\ =\ S(2)\ +\ weight} so that no second
  definition of the cell pressure exists. MEASURED (P11), on a state with
  structure in density, pressure and velocity,
  \texttt{max\_j\ \textbar{}ram\ +\ dp/dr\ +\ rho\ g\ -\ (dF\_2\ -\ S\_2)\textbar{}} over the scale is \textbf{0 under
  WENO3 and 1.7e-16 (default) / 5.3e-15 (key on) under PLM}, for both Riemann
  solvers, so the three terms add up to the row they scale. The defect is
  closed on the states where the terms are known in closed form: with no
  gravity and a uniform pressure at rest, where every term of the equation
  vanishes, the PLM scale read \textbf{1.000 of the geometric pressure term
  \texttt{(A+\ -\ A-)\ p\_j/dV}}, that is 2 p/r, with both Riemann solvers at every N,
  and now reads the \texttt{tiny} floor 2.22507E-308; on supersonic uniform flow at
  M = 3, where the ram divergence is the only term, it stood \textbf{6.66667E-02 =
  1/(gamma M M) above it} and now agrees with it to 1.9e-14 (PLM) and
  4.2e-13 (WENO3); on the discrete hydrostatic equilibrium the PLM scale was
  \textbf{2.79144E-01, 28 percent BELOW the weight it balances}, because \texttt{S(2)} is
  the weight minus the geometric term and no term of the max was the weight
  itself, and the departure \texttt{(w\ -\ s)/w} is now 0. P4\textquotesingle s own zero-gravity
  diagnostic closes with it: the departure of the well-balanced scale from the
  base scheme\textquotesingle s on the same state falls from \textbf{9.43654E-01 to 3.74E-13} under
  PLM with ROE (2.76E-13 with HLLC), the two having agreed under WENO3 all
  along (1.7e-13 to 2.7e-13). Tests: \texttt{hydrostatic\_residual} \textbf{50 PASS / 10
  FAIL} on the entry-text objects and \textbf{60/0} after, the ten being the four
  scheme/solver pairs of P4\textquotesingle s corrected
  \texttt{zero\_gravity\_momentum\_scale\_is\_the\_rows\_own\_terms} (which had compared the
  option\textquotesingle s scale with \texttt{max(\textbar{}dF\_2\textbar{},\ \textbar{}S\_2\textbar{})}, the SUM of the two terms the row
  holds, 7.50E-01 FAIL to 0) and the PLM pairs of the three new identity
  statements; the whole \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} suite \textbf{188/10 to 198/0} (198 =
  P4\textquotesingle s 178 + 20 new rows); \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 290/0, \texttt{certification} 57/0 and
  \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} 53/0 before and after. Two quantities are REPORTED and
  not asserted, with the reason: the two-sided departure of the scale from the
  weight on the well-balanced scheme\textquotesingle s own equilibrium is not round-off for the base scheme
  (MEASURED 8.5e-4 at N = 250 falling to 2.3e-6 at N = 2000, PLM with ROE),
  because that state is the discrete equilibrium of the WELL-BALANCED reconstruction
  and HLLC\textquotesingle s face pressure carries a dissipative part of size dr/H times the
  weight; and the halving statement subtracts the unperturbed row of the same
  cell, since the undifferenced ratio is not 2 (MEASURED 1.0012 at N = 250,
  1.586 at N = 2000) while the imbalance the perturbation ADDS halves to
  2.000000 within 5e-6. Impact: nothing that was certified stops being
  certified. \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} reloaded with the key OFF is \textbf{CERTIFIED} on
  both binaries, \texttt{info\ =\ 0}, \texttt{log10\ Mdot} 13.30, with the momentum row moving
  \textbf{8.469E-10 to 1.165E-09} of its 1.0E-08 tolerance, the mass row 1.453E-13
  to 2.017E-12 of its 3.0E-12 (so this reload now certifies with less margin
  on the mass row) and \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1.667E-09 to 1.495E-09; the outputs are not
  byte-identical, because the merit and the loop-top gate both divide by the
  momentum scale and the two solves accept different iterates, and the states
  differ by at most \textbf{2.6E-09 relative} over every column (9.3e-10 in
  \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt}, 2.6e-09 in \texttt{Ion\_species.txt}). With \texttt{Well\ balanced:\ True} the same reload is \textbf{CERTIFIED} on both, the momentum row moving
  \textbf{9.719E-10 to 9.621E-11} with all eleven output files byte-identical and
  the \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}Fs\textbar{}\textbar{}2} ladder identical iteration by iteration: at cell 500,
  r = 4.62, the ram divergence exceeds the cell\textquotesingle s weight by about a factor
  ten, which is why the measure of the certified state falls by that factor on
  a supersonic outer cell. The marching path of \texttt{mol\_carrier} at 300 steps and
  one thread is \textbf{byte-identical in all seven output files}, \texttt{log10\ Mdot}
  7.95, the \texttt{run.log} differing in exactly eight lines, every one of them a
  report of the momentum measure (the worst-row entry 1.8164E+00 at cell 438
  to 1.9788E+00 at cell 449) and none of them a state. On the carrier reload
  every pass line is identical but the momentum entry, which falls \textbf{1.32E-12
  to 3.22E-13} after pass 1 (4.47E-13 and 3.54E-13 at passes 2 and 3), the
  state as loaded reading 3.622E-01 to \textbf{3.311E-01} in its scaled momentum
  row, and all four output files byte-identical. What stays open: the
  kind-generic \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_QUAD} assembly still hands back neither the momentum
  terms nor the face departures, so under that option (default off, a rounding
  control experiment nothing is adopted from) both are read from the last
  \texttt{RK\_rhs} evaluation rather than from the state just assembled; the fix
  belongs in \texttt{hydrodynamic\_rows.f90}, which this item does not own.
\item
  \textbf{P12 (with P12b): two test-hygiene defects, and the same shape in the
  sibling scripts} (\texttt{src/tests/test\_columns.f90} new, 125 lines;
  \texttt{attempted\_step/run.sh} +18/-5, \texttt{carrier\_returned\_state\_acceptance/run.sh}
  +18/-5, \texttt{carrier\_retry/run.sh} +18/-5, \texttt{carrier\_reference\_scales/run.sh}
  +22/-6, \texttt{carrier\_constraint\_attribution/run.sh} +22/-6,
  \texttt{constrained\_network\_layout/run.sh} +22/-6, \texttt{species\_masses/run.sh} +22/-8,
  \texttt{element\_operator/run.sh} +5/-0, \texttt{steady\_species\_rows/run.sh} +5/-0, and the
  two drivers, MEASURED by \texttt{git\ diff\ -\/-numstat}; of the drivers\textquotesingle{} totals the
  item\textquotesingle s own share is READ from the P12 report, the \texttt{use} line, the renamed
  calls and 66 lines out of \texttt{element\_operator\_tests.f90} and 43 out of
  \texttt{steady\_species\_rows\_tests.f90}). The fifth entry of the plan\textquotesingle s "what
  remains", both halves of it. The \textbf{\texttt{build\_stamp} fallback}: the trigger is
  narrower than "a tree that has never been built", it needs such a tree AND a
  work directory outside \texttt{\$root/build}, which is the concurrency-safe usage
  the worker rules mandate, because \texttt{source\_closure.py} walks \texttt{\$root/build}
  recursively and a default work directory inside it swept the fallback into
  the closure by accident. Seven \texttt{run.sh} now select
  \texttt{\$\{EXHALE\_OBJDIR:-\$root/build\}/build\_stamp.f90}, write the fallback into
  their own work directory only when that is absent, and compile whichever of
  the two is in force as the FIRST file of the closure with the closure
  filtered of any second copy, which is the shape
  \texttt{src/tests/physics\_probe/run.sh} already had;
  \texttt{grep\ -ln\ \textquotesingle{}if\ {[}\ !\ -f\ "\$root/build/build\_stamp.f90"\ {]}\textquotesingle{}\ src/tests/*/run.sh}
  now returns nothing. Four of them gained a private object directory,
  \texttt{EXHALE\_TEST\_OBJDIR}, without which the no-build verification cannot be run
  on them at all (\texttt{species\_masses}, which had no override of any kind,

  consistent state} (\texttt{src/EXHALE\_main.f90}, \texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion}

  \texttt{src/tests/grid\_and\_gates/output\_state\_consistency.sh} +181/-4; MEASURED by

  real. On a stagnation ending the composition had been updated while the

  count and temperature. MEASURED (P14) on the forced stagnation of the

  temperature by \textbf{1.317089E-08} relative at cell 295, where the same fixture
  ending on its pass budget, which takes no update, moves by \textbf{5.921E-16}; the
  written temperature of the worst cell is 1.768302856793E+03 where the same
  conserved state and composition give 1.768302833502E+03, and the worst

  with the steepest exponential). Four changes. The progress control (the
  \texttt{sp\_worst} test, \texttt{n\_no\_fall}, the omega and trust halving, the setting of the
  stagnation ending) now stands between the certification of the pass and the
  update the next solve would consume, guarded so that a certified or

  the stagnation ending by itself: the pass that ends the iteration takes no
  update. The ending is announced with the other endings, below the summary
  line and the diagnostics, so the pass that ends on it prints its summary, its

  no update says which of the two reasons it was. And the element path\textquotesingle s update
  now refreshes the particle densities and the temperature between
  \texttt{relax\_element\_composition} and the equilibrium sweep

  \textbf{P16: the hydrodynamic mass-row tolerance is anchored on the row\textquotesingle s measured

  \texttt{src/tests/certification/certification\_contexts.f90} +105/-0,

  first decision of the afternoon, and it CLOSES the first entry of the plan\textquotesingle s
  "what remains": the tolerance is now
  \texttt{max(cert\_tol\_mass,\ min(cert\_tol\_mass\_ceiling,\ cert\_mass\_round\_margin\ *\ floor(j)))}
  with \texttt{cert\_mass\_round\_margin\ =\ 10}, the ceiling 1 (a row at 1 is a mass flux
  changing by the whole of itself across the cell, not an operative branch on
  any state measured), \texttt{cert\_tol\_mass\ =\ 3e-12} unchanged and now the FLOOR of
  the tolerance, and the verdict on that row taken cell by cell through the
  pure \texttt{mass\_row\_cell\_verdict}. \textbf{The floor expression the item\textquotesingle s brief
  specified could not serve, and the report of that is part of the result}:
  \texttt{eps\ (\textbar{}A+\ F\_up\textbar{}\ +\ \textbar{}A-\ F\_dn\textbar{})/dV\ /\ residual\_row\_scale(1,j)} is algebraically
  between one and two ulps at every cell, because the row\textquotesingle s scale IS the larger
  of the two addends and the \texttt{dV} cancels, and MEASURED it is \textbf{4.4409E-16 on
  all 500 cells of all four states} looked at, so ten times it never rises
  above 3e-12 and the item as written would have been inert. The cause is the
  one N33 established: the interface flux carries the last bit not of its own
  value \texttt{rho\ v} but of the O(rho c\_s) quantities the Riemann solve differences,
  so the floor is about \texttt{eps/Mach} and not \texttt{eps}. The estimate adopted is
  \texttt{eps\ rho(\textbar{}v\textbar{}\ +\ c\_s)\ A\ /\ dV} over the row\textquotesingle s own scale, and it bounds the row\textquotesingle s
  measured non-smoothness within a factor \textbf{1.5931} (\texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} as
  loaded, base Mach 3.937E-03), \textbf{1.8857} (\texttt{atomic\_elem\_newton} as loaded,
  5.062E-06), \textbf{2.6806} (\texttt{carrier\_elem\_newton} as loaded, 2.400E-04) and
  \textbf{1.7733} (the HD 209458 b state this item certifies, 2.767E-06), where the
  specified expression is short by two to six decades. The margin is measured,
  not chosen: \texttt{c\_round\ =\ 10} is a round number 3.7 times above the largest of
  those four ratios and inside N33\textquotesingle s independent reading of the same floor
  against its own bound, 5.5 to 12 (READ). The measurement itself is in the
  code: \texttt{EXHALE\_MASS\_FLOOR\_SCAN=1} (new, default off) adds one ulp to the
  density of every physical cell, runs the whole flux assembly again and
  reports the STEP each mass row takes beside both estimates, and the state
  given is then assembled once more and reproduces its own mass rows to
  0.00E+00 on every fixture. Tests: \texttt{certification} \textbf{57/0} on the entry
  driver against the entry objects, \textbf{0 PASS / 1 FAIL} with exit 1 on the new
  driver against the entry objects (the compile naming the three new symbols),
  \textbf{70/0} after, the eleven new rows asserting that a floor below the fixed

  transcribed inside the test refuses it, that a row above the anchor still

  \textbf{P21: the carrier relaxation solves the chemistry of the composition it
  advances} (\texttt{src/modules/lower\_atmosphere/diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}
  +115/-27, \texttt{src/EXHALE\_main.f90} +17/-7,
  \texttt{src/tests/carrier\_retry/carrier\_retry.f90} +287/-16; these three counts are
  MEASURED by \texttt{diff\ -u} against the entry texts saved in the scratch directory
  before the first edit and NOT by \texttt{git\ diff}, for the reason in "An external
  commit during the session" below). Verdict: the design does what it was meant
  to do at the level it was meant to act. With the chemistry equilibrated after
  every transport step the pass keeps, the unbounded pass at a fixed wind
  converges to a state that really is stationary for the coupled operator: on
  the hot-Uranus carrier reload the carrier steady residual falls from
  \textbf{7.99E-02 to 1.42E-09} in the worst cell and from \textbf{2.32E-03 to 1.18E-11}
  volume-weighted (MEASURED, P21, M1 below). The frozen fixed point was not a
  fixed point of transport plus chemistry, and this is the measurement of that.
  Two negative results come with it and are in the same subsection: at the
  production bound nothing moves, and the joint fixed point at a fixed wind is a
  fully molecular column.

  The design as implemented. A new private routine
  \texttt{equilibrate\_chemistry\_at\_fixed\_pressure(rho,\ p,\ f\_sp,\ T,\ heat,\ cool,\ eta)}
  takes \texttt{get\_species\_densities} (the particle count and the electron density of
  the composition), then \texttt{comp\_T\_from\_p} (its temperature at the conserved
  state\textquotesingle s own pressure), then \texttt{ioniz\_eq}. No new module dependency was needed in
  either direction and \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90} is not edited. It is called
  inside the trial loop of \texttt{relax\_photochemical\_composition} immediately after a
  trial is ACCEPTED and before the fixed-point test, so a trial the operator
  refuses, or one that leaves the movement bound, is undone and halved before
  any sweep is taken and leaves the chemistry exactly where it found it. \textbf{The
  sweep IS the background reinstall}: \texttt{ioniz\_eq} writes \texttt{bg\_cell(j)\ =\ ieq\_cell} for every cell of a molecular sweep (READ), while
  \texttt{install\_background\_of\_adopted\_state} belongs to the steady solver\textquotesingle s
  best-iterate bookkeeping and is not involved here. The sweep is handed the
  transported partition and fixes x(H2), the oxygen carriers and, where the
  protons are carried, the ionized fraction from the composition it reads, so it
  returns the chemistry OF that partition and does not re-solve it. \textbf{The
  movement bound is read on the transport output, before the sweep}: the bound
  governs what the transport operator moved in one trial, and the chemistry then
  closes on the step that was kept. MEASURED consequence: the reported drift of
  every bounded production pass stays at 9.98E-03 to 1.00E-02 at trust 1e-2, so
  the sweep does not carry the returned state out of its bound. The fixed-point
  test is now taken between two states the chemistry has closed on (\texttt{fnow} is
  recomputed after the sweep and becomes \texttt{fprev}), so \texttt{carrier\_relax\_fixed\_point}
  means the carriers AND their chemistry stopped moving together. Interface:
  \texttt{relax\_photochemical\_composition(rho,\ v,\ p,\ T,\ f\_sp,\ heat,\ cool,\ eta,\ trust,\ drift,\ nstep,\ outcome)}, with \texttt{p} \texttt{intent(in)}, the conserved state\textquotesingle s own
  pressure untouched, and \texttt{T}, \texttt{heat}, \texttt{cool}, \texttt{eta} \texttt{intent(inout)}, written
  only where a refresh happened. The carrier branch of
  \texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion} passes the new arguments and its own
  \texttt{ioniz\_eq} call is DELETED as redundant; the \texttt{get\_species\_densities} and
  \texttt{comp\_T\_from\_p} beside it are KEPT, because they are not a refresh but the
  filling of the density columns the routine keeps beside the composition, and
  they repeat the arithmetic of the pass\textquotesingle s last internal call exactly.

  Tests: \texttt{carrier\_retry} \textbf{122/0 at entry, 124 PASS / 3 FAIL on a RED build}
  carrying the delivered module with the one
  \texttt{call\ equilibrate\_chemistry\_at\_fixed\_pressure} line removed, \textbf{127/0 after}.
  The new section (11) has five rows and states why the joint fixed point cannot
  be asserted on the synthetic 12-cell column at all: the shortest admissible
  trial still moves 6.46e-4 of the largest entry H2 mixing ratio, six decades
  above \texttt{relax\_tol\ =\ 1e-10}. What it asserts instead is the consistency the joint
  fixed point rests on: the background third-body density read from the returned
  composition (GREEN 4.7244E-07 against the entry composition\textquotesingle s 5.5008E-03; RED
  5.4938E-03 against 1.2191E-16), the background temperature (GREEN 2.5675E-07
  against 5.4837E-03; RED 5.5241E-03 against 0), and one further sweep moving the
  composition by 1.3637E-10 against hybrd1\textquotesingle s own \texttt{xtol\ =\ sqrt(eps)\ =\ 1.5E-08}
  (RED 2.0110E-04). Every other suite whose driver links
  \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry} is unchanged before and after:
  \texttt{carrier\_returned\_state\_acceptance} 36/0, \texttt{attempted\_step} 70/0,
  \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 195/0, \texttt{certification} 70/0,
  \texttt{carrier\_constraint\_attribution} 10/0, \texttt{carrier\_reference\_scales} 14/0,
  \texttt{element\_operator} 24/0, \texttt{species\_masses} 9/0.

\subsubsection{The pseudo-time start of the stationary restart route}\label{the-pseudo-time-start-of-the-stationary-restart-route}

Found while running P6 and settled by the advisor. \texttt{Restart\ intent:\ stationary} entered the stationary solve at the CFL interval of the reloaded
state. A state written by a run is already relaxed, and a continuation
started at the CFL step from it does not reach the root: MEASURED, 8 threads,
the hot-Uranus carrier reload\textquotesingle s hydrodynamic solve is handed back at \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}}
2.685E-01 after 40 iterations and the next four passes enter at 1.133, 1.018,
1.026 and 1.025 and hand back 2.976E-01 to 3.579E-01
(\texttt{\textless{}scratchpad\textgreater{}/P6/runs/carrier\_p6/run.log}), while the HD 209458 b element
reload stops on its stagnation detector at \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1.490 and the pass is then
refused because the element relaxation finds no admissible advance
(\texttt{\textless{}scratchpad\textgreater{}/P6/runs/atomic\_partition.new/run.log}). With the start at 1.0,
the value the marching hand-off uses, the same two solves reach \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}}
2.507E-09 at iteration 13 and hand back \textbf{1.498E-09} (carrier) and hand back
\textbf{1.167E-08} (atomic). The advisor therefore made 1.0 the default of the
\texttt{Restart\ intent:\ stationary} route (\texttt{src/EXHALE\_main.f90} line 5234), with
\texttt{EXHALE\_PTC\_DTAU0} as the override for measurement; the direct steady route
keeps its CFL start, which is the pseudo-transient continuation it was
designed as. Verified with no environment variable set: the run prints
\texttt{stationary\ restart:\ dtau0\ =\ 1.00E+00} and reproduces the first pass to every
printed digit (\texttt{\textless{}scratchpad\textgreater{}/P7/carrier\_default\_dtau0/run.log}). No
regression case sets \texttt{Restart\ intent:} at all (MEASURED: the grep over
\texttt{backup/regression/*/input.inp} returns nothing), so this default moves no
golden; it moves any user run that sets the key.

\subsubsection{P7: the measurement of record}\label{p7-the-measurement-of-record}

One binary (\texttt{EXHALE\_P6.x}, built from the merged tree), both fixtures,
\texttt{Restart\ intent:\ stationary}, \texttt{EXHALE\_PTC\_DTAU0=1.0}, \texttt{OMP\_NUM\_THREADS=8},
500 cells. The partitioned route is \texttt{Coupled\ carrier\ solve:\ False} with
\texttt{EXHALE\_OUTER\_PASSES} setting the pass budget and \texttt{EXHALE\_JFNK\_MAXIT} the
hydrodynamic cap; the coupled route is the same fixture with \texttt{Coupled\ carrier\ solve:\ True}. Wall times of the partitioned runs are the sum of the seconds
the \texttt{outer\ pass} lines report, which agrees with the run directory\textquotesingle s file
timestamps to under a second; the coupled carrier control prints no total, so
its time is the timestamp span of its run directory. Every number MEASURED
from the log named.

Sources: \texttt{\textless{}scratchpad\textgreater{}/P7/carrier\_elem\_newton\_part1/run.log},
\texttt{carrier\_part40/run.log}, \texttt{carrier\_coupled250/run.log},
\texttt{atomic\_elem\_newton\_part1/run.log}, \texttt{atomic\_part12/run.log},
\texttt{atomic\_coupled250/run.log}. The atomic coupled control alternates the
element relaxation too, because \texttt{He\_diffusion} is on, and its three completed
passes cost 1380.59 s, 482.04 s and 528.98 s; it was ended in the fourth. The
\texttt{(EXHALE\_main)\ outer\ pass\ N:} line of every pass carries the hydrodynamic
flag, the worst gated species wind row with its cell, the three row maxima,
omega, the carrier trust and the seconds, and the closing \texttt{(certification)}
block names what refuses. Those \texttt{\textless{}scratchpad\textgreater{}/} paths are the session scratch
root and are TEMPORARY: they will be gone, and everything quoted from them is
recorded here, in the two fixture READMEs and in
\texttt{docs/session\_handoff\_20260911.md}. The runs reproduce from the pinned
fixtures by the recipes in those READMEs.

What the ladders show. On the atomic fixture the worst gated species row
falls 2.61e-3, 3.81e-3, 1.77e-3, 9.45e-4, 4.64e-4, 2.36e-4, 1.20e-4,
5.97e-5, 3.07e-5, 1.50e-5, 7.94e-6, 5.47e-6 over the twelve passes (the one
rise, pass 1 to pass 2, is the alternation\textquotesingle s own feedback across a
hydrodynamic solve and is why a single rise does not end the loop), the
refusing entries fall 9 to 1, and in the last pass every elemental wind row
is within 1e-5: He/H 5.468e-6 at cell 288, and the metals 1.582e-6 (Mg) to
3.720e-6 (O). On the carrier fixture the H2 wind row falls 7.38e-2 to
2.23e-2 over the 40 passes with the binding cell moving 290 to 443, which is
the H2 front advancing about one cell in a pass and not a residual settling.

\textbf{The decision.} The plan\textquotesingle s adoption rule is that the route is adopted only
if it certifies where the coupled solve does not, or reaches a strictly lower
joint residual in no more wall time. Neither fixture certifies, so the first
branch is not met on either. The second branch is met on both: on the atomic
fixture the partitioned route stands strictly below the coupled control on
every judged row (mass 1.054e-9 against 1.16e-6, momentum 1.665e-13 against
2.89e-6, energy 1.501e-8 against 2.83e-6, worst gated species row 5.468e-6
against 8.29e-5) in 1043 s against 2392 s for three passes; on the carrier
fixture the partitioned route at 8 passes stands strictly below the coupled
control on every judged row (8.75e-12 against 1.231e-2, 1.66e-12 against
1.009e-1, 2.41e-9 against 5.801e-2, H2 6.217e-2 against 7.815e-2) in 173 s
against 321 s, and the 40-pass continuation goes on to 2.23e-2 for 831 s.
The partitioned route is therefore what the production outer loop runs.
\texttt{Coupled\ carrier\ solve} already defaults to False
(\texttt{carrier\_in\_newton\ =\ .false.}, \texttt{src/modules/init/parameters.f90} line 419),
so no default changes; \texttt{True} remains the coupled measurement option, and what
this session added on the partitioned side is the joint acceptance contract
of P6 and the three defect repairs P1 to P3 that made a pass mean something.

\textbf{What did not certify, and why.} Entry 1 was CLOSED the same afternoon by
the user\textquotesingle s first decision (P16, anchor (6)); entries 2 and 3 are stated as
they stand:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{CLOSED 2026-09-11 by P16.} The atomic element reload refused on the
  hydrodynamic mass row alone,
  1.054E-09 against 3.0E-12 at cell 16 (r = 1.0031), on the three-unknown
  solve of this fixture. Every one of its twelve hydrodynamic solves ends
  with \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} between 8.980E-09 and 1.501E-08 against the run\textquotesingle s \texttt{Resid\ tol}
  of 1e-8, and the last one ends on \texttt{no\ descent\ direction\ exists\ for\ the\ banded\ model\ at\ this\ state} at \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1.501E-08. So the alternation has
  done its work (the element rows are inside their tolerance) and what is
  left is the base-layer mass row of the hydrodynamic solve, which is the
  N33 rounding floor territory and not a species-coupling question.
  \textbf{P10 settled which of the two it is.} With the best-iterate ledger
  reading that row against its own 3e-12 (P10), the same twelve passes end at
  8.893E-10 at cell 3, the same order, and the row is MEASURED to carry no
  signal at that level: the ledger\textquotesingle s best distance of pass 1 is 4.407E+02, a
  mass row of 1.32E-09, while the same state re-evaluated at its own
  composition after the best-iterate restore carries 4.11E-09. At 1e-9 to
  1e-12 the row is the rounding of the base-layer flux difference, so ranking
  iterates by it is ranking by rounding noise and no ledger can bring it
  down. The entry was therefore an ACCEPTANCE question, the 3e-12 tolerance
  against the base-layer rounding floor of this fixture, and not a solver
  item; the user decided it the same afternoon, and with the mass row judged
  at \texttt{max(3e-12,\ 10\ *\ floor(j))} the same twelve-pass recipe \textbf{CERTIFIES at
  pass 12} (MEASURED by P16 and again by P17, which certifies at pass 12 in
  both of its builds). The verdict is taken at a wind cell, not at the base:
  cell 34, r = 1.00665, 6.542E-10 against the 4.6E-09 anchor of that cell,
  distance 0.141 (P16), and cell 50, r = 1.00977, 4.245E-10 against 3.2E-09,
  distance 0.132 (P17). The base-layer floor of this fixture is what the
  anchor is measured on: see the decisions subsection below and
  \texttt{docs/certification\_tolerance\_anchoring\_20260910.md}, "Anchor (6)".
\item
  The carrier reload refuses on the H2 wind row, and the user\textquotesingle s second
  decision of the afternoon ran the continuation that says what that row IS.
  MEASURED over 110 passes (the table and the recipe are in the decisions
  subsection below): the H2 front does NOT stop, \texttt{x2\ =\ 0.5} moving 1.0726 to
  1.1230 R\_p over 108 passes at about 5E-04 R\_p in a pass with no sign of
  slowing, so the continuation is unfinished after 110 passes and 0.05 R\_p.
  And the worst gated row stops being the front after pass 40: it stands at
  \textbf{2.2E-02, nearly uniform in r}, and its cell walks outward through the
  wind, 443 to 500, reaching the outer boundary at pass 108 while the value
  does not fall. That floor is a property of the operator and not of the
  front: the bounded carrier pass (trust 1e-2 on the H2 mixing-ratio maximum,
  met by one transport step) advances the front but leaves the wind cells,
  whose H2 mixing ratio is orders of magnitude below the maximum, at one
  implicit step in a pass, so their own H2 balance never relaxes, and the
  drift measure, absolute in the grid maximum, does not see them. The
  consequence for the method, recorded and not adopted: a bounded front
  advance and a relaxation of the wind cells\textquotesingle{} H2 balance are two different
  operations, and the second needs its own step (a cell-relative measure, or
  a wind-window relaxation after the bounded pass) before the H2 wind row can
  be judged at 1e-5. Whether the front stops at all inside 2 R\_p is a
  physical question and stays open (the P23 comparison against Koskinen et
  al. 2022 Model A).
\item
  The hot-Uranus mass row sits on the same floor throughout the carrier
  run, 4.48E-12 to 1.57E-11 against 3.0E-12 over the 40 passes, with the
  momentum row at 4.14E-13 to 5.13E-12 and the energy row at 1.75E-09 to
  4.56E-09. The mass row of that fixture is the rounding of the base-layer
  flux difference recorded in N33, which is the reading P16 then made
  operative: under anchor (6) the base cells of this fixture are ADMITTED by
  their own anchor at every pass and the refusal moves out to cell 180
  (r = 1.0707), where the floor is below 3e-12 and the row carries signal.
\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection{The Codex adversarial review (2026-09-11)}\label{the-codex-adversarial-review-2026-09-11}

Run on the working tree after P1 to P13, that is on the merged text of the
morning\textquotesingle s items and not on the committed tree. Two findings, both verified in
the source by the advisor before any item was written, and both closed the
same afternoon. The findings are quoted as they were written.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{{[}high{]}} "정체 종료 시 조성과 출력용 상태가 서로 달라집니다
  (src/EXHALE\_main.f90:6503-6514)": on a stagnation exit the composition had
  been updated while the particle densities and the temperature were not
  refreshed on the element path, so the certified state and the written state
  differed; the recommendation was to move the progress control before the
  composition update. Verified in the source, reproduced as a measured
  inconsistency of 1.317089E-08 in the temperature of the written state, and
  \textbf{closed by P14} with exactly that ordering: the progress control now
  stands between the certification of the pass and the update, so a pass that
  ends the iteration takes no update, and the element path\textquotesingle s update refreshes
  the particle densities and the temperature before its equilibrium sweep.
\item
  \textbf{{[}medium{]}} "고정밀 경로가 다른 상태의 압력 정보를 인증에 사용합니다
  (src/modules/time\_step/steady\_residual.f90:256-258)": with
  \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_QUAD} the generic rows stored only \texttt{face\_flux} and \texttt{face\_p},
  so \texttt{momentum\_row\_terms\_of\_state} read the previous \texttt{RK\_rhs} call\textquotesingle s
  \texttt{face\_q\_up}/\texttt{face\_q\_dn} (zeros on the first call); the recommendation was to
  return the terms from the generic computation and to refuse the continuation
  combination. Verified in the source, and \textbf{closed by P15}, both halves: the
  kind-generic rows now hand their own face departures back along the route the fluxes take,
  and the continuation combination is refused, because the kind-generic rows CAN be
  reached on a continuation (\texttt{assemble\_residual} chooses the text from
  \texttt{generic\_precision\_rows\_arm()} alone and the generic text carries its own
  blending branch) and no single pair of departures satisfies the blended row
  identity.
\end{enumerate}

Neither finding moves a certified state of the default path: P14\textquotesingle s
\texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} reload and P15\textquotesingle s, with the key off and with
\texttt{Well\ balanced:\ True} and \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_QUAD=2}, are CERTIFIED with every
output byte-identical apart from the provenance timestamp. What each moves is
stated in its own bullet above.

\subsubsection{User decisions of 2026-09-11 (afternoon)}\label{user-decisions-of-2026-09-11-afternoon}

Two decisions were taken after the open items of the plan were put to the
user.

\textbf{Decision 1: the mass tolerance is re-anchored above the fixture\textquotesingle s measured
rounding floor} (item P16, and the ledger consequence P17). The tolerance of
the continuity row is now

\begin{verbatim}
tol(j) = max( cert_tol_mass, min( cert_tol_mass_ceiling, c_round * floor(j) ) )
\end{verbatim}

with \texttt{cert\_tol\_mass\ =\ 3e-12} unchanged as the FLOOR, the ceiling 1, and
\texttt{c\_round\ =\ cert\_mass\_round\_margin\ =\ 10}. The floor estimate is
\texttt{eps\ rho(\textbar{}v\textbar{}\ +\ c\_s)\ A\ /\ dV} over the row\textquotesingle s own scale, which is one ulp in the
wind and about \texttt{eps/Mach} in a layer whose flux is a tiny residue of the
momentum the cell carries at its own fastest signal speed.

The brief\textquotesingle s first expression for the floor,
\texttt{eps\ (\textbar{}A+\ F\_up\textbar{}\ +\ \textbar{}A-\ F\_dn\textbar{})/dV\ /\ residual\_row\_scale(1,j)}, is inert, and that
is a measured result and not a judgement: the row\textquotesingle s scale IS the larger of the
two addends and the \texttt{dV} cancels, so the expression is algebraically between
one and two ulps at every cell, MEASURED at \textbf{4.4409E-16 on all 500 cells of
all four states} examined, and ten times it never rises above 3e-12. The
adopted estimate bounds the step each mass row actually takes when one ulp is
added to the density of every physical cell (\texttt{EXHALE\_MASS\_FLOOR\_SCAN=1}, new
and default off) within a factor

\textbf{Two regimes.} Passes 1 to 30: the worst gated row is at the front\textquotesingle s outer
edge, its cell moving 290 to 267 (r about 1.28 to 1.25), and it falls 7.4E-02
to 2.6E-02 as the front relaxes. From pass 40 the worst row is no longer at the
front: it sits at 2.2E-02 and its cell walks outward through the wind, 443 to
500, reaching the outer boundary at pass 108 while the value does not fall. The
front itself keeps moving outward at about 5E-04 R\_p in a pass, \texttt{x2\ =\ 0.5} from
1.0726 to 1.1230 over 108 passes, with no sign of stopping.

So three statements, the first two measured and the third a reading of them.
(a) The front has NOT reached a stationary position in 110 passes: 0.05 R\_p
traveled and the continuation unfinished. (b) The remaining wind residual of
2.2E-02 is nearly uniform in r and is NOT the front. (c) The reading: the
bounded carrier pass, trust 1E-02 on the H2 mixing-ratio maximum and met by one
transport step, advances the front but leaves the wind cells, whose H2 mixing
ratio is orders of magnitude below the maximum, at one implicit step in a pass,
so their own H2 balance never relaxes, and the drift measure, absolute in the
grid maximum, does not see them. The progress control ended the run because
after pass 40 the worst gated row measures that wind floor and not the front.

Consequence for the method, recorded and NOT adopted: a bounded front advance
and a relaxation of the wind cells\textquotesingle{} H2 balance are two different operations,
and the second needs its own step, a cell-relative measure or a wind-window
relaxation after the bounded pass, before the H2 wind row can be judged at
1e-5. The physical question whether the front stops at all inside 2 R\_p is
separate and stays open (the P23 comparison against Koskinen et al. 2022
Model A).

\subsubsection{P21: the fixed point at a fixed wind, and the production loop}\label{p21-the-fixed-point-at-a-fixed-wind-and-the-production-loop}

All of this is MEASURED by P21 on \texttt{backup/regression/carrier\_elem\_newton}
(\texttt{Coupled\ carrier\ solve:\ False}, \texttt{Restart\ intent:\ stationary},
\texttt{EXHALE\_JFNK\_MAXIT=40}, 8 threads, scratch copies), against a control binary
built from the entry text of the three files.

\textbf{M1, the fixed point at fixed hydro.} \texttt{EXHALE\_CARRIER\_TRUST=1e6}, that is the
movement bound disabled, and \texttt{EXHALE\_OUTER\_PASSES=2} rather than 1, because the
outer loop takes no composition update on its last pass (P6) and at 1 the
relaxation never runs. Both binaries end the pass on
\texttt{carrier\_relax\_fixed\_point}.

Twelve and sixty passes reproduce the recorded ladder to every printed digit:
the worst gated H2 row, its cell and both front positions are identical at
passes 1, 10, 20, 30, 40 and 50. Every pass ends on the movement bound with 3 to
6 kept steps and a drift of 9.98E-03 to 1.00E-02, and every hydrodynamic solve
returns \texttt{info\ =\ 0}. So \textbf{the 2.2E-02 wind floor does not go away and the frozen
chemistry was not its cause}; the bound\textquotesingle s measure, absolute in the grid
maximum, remains the standing explanation. Cost is not a reason to hesitate: a
production pass costs 3.07 to 3.72 s against the control\textquotesingle s 3.10 to 3.54 s, and
one equilibrium sweep on this 500-cell grid costs below 0.029 s at 8 threads
(MEASURED from 300 marching steps of \texttt{mol\_carrier} in 8.66 s including
initialization), so 3 to 6 of them cannot be resolved against that scatter.

\textbf{M3 and M4, the default paths.} \texttt{mol\_carrier} marching, \texttt{EXHALE\_MAXSTEPS=300},
one thread: \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt}, \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} and both \texttt{\_adv} files identical
except the two \texttt{git=} provenance lines, and \texttt{Cooling\_breakdown.txt},
\texttt{Heating\_breakdown.txt} and \texttt{IC\_dump.txt} byte-identical, which is the expected
result because the marching loop calls \texttt{photochemical\_transport\_step} directly
and never this routine. \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} reloaded, one thread: the two run
logs are byte-identical, both \texttt{info\ =\ 0} and CERTIFIED at \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}\ =\ 1.920E-09},
ten of the twelve written files byte-identical and the other two differing in
the same provenance line alone. No molecules, so the branch is never entered.

\textbf{Does the front stop at fixed hydro, and where?} No, and the way it fails to
stop is new information. At a wind held fixed and with the chemistry solved on
the advancing composition, the pass runs to a genuine fixed point of the coupled
operator (1.42E-09) whose H2 fills the whole domain: x2 = 2n(H2)/n\_H is 0.98 at
1.1 R\_p, 0.96 at 1.2, 0.84 at 1.5, 0.60 at 2.0 and 0.33 at 3.0 R\_p, with no cell
below 1e-2 anywhere. The largest x2 is 0.9848, so this is NOT the element
ceiling and no cell is clamped in either run, and P2\textquotesingle s frozen artifact (x2
driven to the element ceiling in 123 cells) does not appear. A plausible
reading, offered as a reading: the H2 column and its self-shielding grow
together, so re-solving the photodissociating field on the advancing composition
removes the sink that held the front, and nothing but the wind can stop it,
which it cannot while it is held fixed. What this says is that \textbf{the front\textquotesingle s
position is set by the hydrodynamic response and not by the carrier operator
alone}, consistent with the bounded advance being the only usable form of the
update; the physical question, whether the front stops inside 2 R\_p in the
coupled system, stays exactly where P23 has it.

\textbf{Does the wind floor go away?} No, as M2 records. The 2.2E-02 row that appears
at pass 40 and walks outward through the wind is reproduced pass for pass and
cell for cell with the chemistry refreshed. The frozen background was not its
cause; the bound\textquotesingle s measure, absolute in the grid maximum, still is.

\subsubsection{The terminology sweep (A1 to A4, 2026-09-11)}\label{the-terminology-sweep-a1-to-a4-2026-09-11}

The user asked whether "arm" is a word physics uses for what this tree was using
it for, and instructed that terms belonging to software engineering, clinical
trials or management not be used as the names of physical or numerical concepts
at all. The rule is now in \texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/CLAUDE.md} under "금지 표현", in two
bullets. The first: avoid terms that physics, chemistry, mathematics and
astronomy do not use, in documents, comments, log messages and identifiers
alike, and write instead what the thing is, the standard term of those fields or
plain English, naming what is compared ("control build", "measured build"),
which physics option is meant ("the well-balanced option"), or which quantity
("the mass row", "the H2 front"); where such a term already stands, fix it where
it is visible. The second: the NOUN "arm" is forbidden, for one side of a
comparison, for a selectable code path and for one branch of a fixture alike,
and code identifiers (\texttt{ARM\_*}, \texttt{*\_arm}) are held to the same rule; the VERB "arm
/ armed / arming / disarm", meaning to enable a device, is plain English and
stays.

Four workers swept the tree. Every count is MEASURED by the worker that owns the
files, by \texttt{grep\ -w\ -i\ -E\ "arms?"} against the git HEAD text, and no number, file
name or measured statement was changed anywhere in the four diffs.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{A1, the 51 top-level \texttt{docs/*.md} outside the two update logs: 396 to 17.}
  All 17 that remain are allowed: 14 are the verb, 3 are the literal directory
  glob \texttt{arm*}. The replacements are by sense: a side of a comparison became "the
  two runs" or "both builds"; an optional code path became its key or its
  physics ("the well-balanced option", "the kind-generic residual assembly");
  a fixture\textquotesingle s solve became "the element reload" or "the carrier reload", which is
  the word those same documents already use; a rung of a ladder of runs became
  "rung"; one chemical network of a comparison became "network". One code span
  changed, the placeholder \texttt{\textless{}arm\textgreater{}} of a run-directory path in
  \texttt{vulcan\_photochem\_comparison.md}, which named nothing on disk and is now
  \texttt{\textless{}network\textgreater{}}, the names that do exist there being \texttt{vulcan/}, \texttt{ncho/},
  \texttt{zahnle/}, \texttt{zahnle\_s/} and \texttt{eqfit/} (READ from the tree).
\item
  \textbf{A2, \texttt{docs/Update\_EXHALE\_stage2.md}: 176 to 1} (the one is the verb, "when both
  stellar keys arm H(n=2)"), and \texttt{Update\_EXHALE\_appendix.tex} 1 to 0. The
  largest class was "in both arms" of a two-binary comparison, now "in both
  builds"; then the optional paths ("the well-balanced option", "quad
  accumulation", "the kind-generic rows"), the fixtures ("the atomic element
  reload", "the carrier rows"), and the unknown space of B5j/B5k ("the density
  unknown", "the log unknown"). \texttt{Update\_EXHALE\_stage2.tex} was regenerated and the PDF
  rebuilt at \textbf{158 pages}, the same count as before the sweep (MEASURED,
  \texttt{pdfinfo}). Thirteen British spellings were corrected in the same pass, twelve
  in the Markdown and one in the appendix (\texttt{modelling}, \texttt{centre}/\texttt{centred},
  \texttt{labelled}, \texttt{cancelled}, \texttt{behaviour}, \texttt{travelled}, \texttt{NEIGHBOUR}), as the
  American-spelling rule for documents asks.
\item

  1.591E-13), so the renamed selector still selects. That the selection is not

  at a flux spread of 1.588E-13, different in the last digits from mode 2, while

\subsection{9. PLAN\_20260912: the seven findings of the stage-2 review (2026-09-12)}\label{9-plan_20260912-the-seven-findings-of-the-stage-2-review-2026-09-12}

Plan of record: \texttt{docs/PLAN\_20260912\_review\_fixes.md}. User instruction of
2026-09-12: analyze \texttt{docs/Update\_EXHALE\_stage2\_review\_20260912.md} against the
code, and where its analysis is right, write the plan and start the
corrections. All seven findings R1 to R7 were confirmed in the source before
the plan was written (the confirming lines are listed at the head of the plan);
the corrections are items Q1 to Q4, Q1 and Q4 by the advisor, Q2 and Q3 by
workers under \texttt{docs/worker\_rules.md}. Every number below is MEASURED on the
tree of this section unless marked READ.

\subsubsection{The state contract at a fixed hydrodynamic state (R2, R3)}\label{the-state-contract-at-a-fixed-hydrodynamic-state-r2-r3}

The decision behind Q1: at a fixed hydrodynamic state the quantities the solve
holds are the conserved variables \texttt{u\ =\ (rho,\ rho\ v,\ E)}. A composition update
at fixed hydro keeps \texttt{u} and recomputes the pressure and the temperature from
the unchanged thermal energy \texttt{rho\ e\ =\ E\ -\ (rho\ v)\^{}2/(2\ rho)} through the
caloric EOS of the new composition (\texttt{pressure\_from\_energy\_density} after
\texttt{get\_species\_densities}, then \texttt{comp\_T\_from\_p}). A held pressure is not a state
the solve knows: the review\textquotesingle s 500-cell probe showed that keeping \texttt{p} while the
main loop keeps \texttt{E} describes two molecular states 3.3e-3 apart.

\subsubsection{The items}\label{the-items-1}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Q1 (R1, R2, R3, R6, R7 and the outer progress measure), advisor.}
  \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90} (+206/-204), the carrier branch and the
  summary of \texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion} in \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90},
  \texttt{src/tests/carrier\_retry/carrier\_retry.f90} (+195/-77) and its \texttt{run.sh}.

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    R1: \texttt{photochemical\_transport\_step} takes an optional \texttt{trial} argument; a
    trial of the stationary relaxation returns before the history mark, the
    message and the stop, so a rejected trial (an uncovered interval, a
    chemistry that did not close, a displacement beyond the movement bound)
    leaves \texttt{carrier\_history\_certifiable\_flag} as it found it. The marching
    path passes no \texttt{trial} and is unchanged.
  \item
    R2, R3: \texttt{relax\_photochemical\_composition(u,\ v,\ f\_sp,\ p,\ T,\ heat,\ cool,\ eta,\ trust,\ drift,\ nstep,\ outcome)} now takes the conserved state; \texttt{p} and
    \texttt{T} are outputs. \texttt{pressure\_and\_temperature\_at\_fixed\_conserved\_state}
    is the one expression of the contract above, and
    \texttt{equilibrate\_chemistry\_at\_fixed\_conserved\_state} cycles (p, T at fixed u;
    sweep; p, T again) until the largest relative change of T falls below
    1e-6 (at most five cycles), reads the sweep ledger (\texttt{n\_nonfinite}) and a
    finite composition, and reports \texttt{ok}; a refresh that does not close
    refuses the trial (\texttt{carrier\_relax\_chemistry\_refused}, a new named ending
    "the chemistry of the shortest admissible trial did not close") and the
    entry composition, background, p, T, heating, cooling and eta are
    restored. The caller in \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} rebuilds \texttt{W} from the unchanged
    \texttt{u} and takes the returned composition; nothing is reconstructed from a
    held pressure any more.
  \item
    R6: the carrier retry suite\textquotesingle s column is built by
    \texttt{column\_carrying\_its\_own\_density} of \texttt{src/tests/test\_columns.f90} (the
    mass-closed constructor of the element suites; \texttt{run.sh} compiles it
    before the driver) with the ions moved out of the neutrals; a row asserts
    the column reconstructs its own density to 1e-13 before any step. The
    consistency rows are absolute: the returned pressure is that of the
    unchanged thermal energy (0.0), the returned temperature is that of the
    returned composition (0.0), the background third-body density and
    temperature the sweep stored are those of the returned composition
    (6.53e-7 and 2.28e-7 relative, allowance 10 \texttt{ieq\_res\_tol}), and one
    further sweep leaves the returned composition within the sweep tolerance
    (8.83e-7 against \texttt{ieq\_res\_tol}). Nine rows for R1: a rejected trial keeps
    the history certifiable, keeps no step, hands back the entry composition
    bit for bit and names the refused interval; a pass after the refusals
    keeps a step and stays eligible; a refused trial in phys mode reports
    exhausted, leaves the history certifiable and writes no composition.
    RED on the entry text (the trial marking the history, the refresh not
    closed): five rows fail (pressure 1.2e-7, temperature 3.5e-7, further
    sweep 1.2e-6, the two R1 rows 0 of 1); GREEN on the tree: 138 PASS, 0
    FAIL.
  \item
    R7 and the progress measure: the outer summary of
    \texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion} ranks every entry by its binding
    distance (\texttt{dist\_bind}, \texttt{row\_at\_bind}, \texttt{jbind} where the entry carries
    them), so the mass row\textquotesingle s refusal is attributed to the cell that binds it
    and not to the largest row over another cell\textquotesingle s tolerance; and the
    progress control halves omega and the carrier movement bound on the
    JOINT distance of the state (the largest entry over its own tolerance,
    \texttt{prog\_worst}), not on the worst species row alone. An entry that is not
    finite or not available counts as an infinite distance.
  \item
    The review\textquotesingle s 500-cell probe (\texttt{docs/audit\_20260905/stage2\_review\_20260912/\ molecular\_wind\_state\_probe.f90}, adapted to the new signature, hot-Uranus
    carrier reload, one hydro solve and one bounded relaxation at trust
    0.01): returned temperature against the EOS of the returned state 0.0
    (was 5.5997819e-9), pressure of the unchanged thermal energy against the
    returned pressure 0.0 (was 3.2993e-3), thermal-energy change a held
    pressure would need 6.9e-16 (was 3.3250e-3); mass closure 8.33e-15 and
    the five retained steps at drift 9.9955e-3 unchanged.
  \item
    Impact on the routes: the unbounded pass of P21 M1 repeated on the new
    contract (\texttt{EXHALE\_CARRIER\_TRUST=1e6}, cap 40) reaches the fixed wind\textquotesingle s
    fixed point in 61 transport steps at drift 0.995 with the H2 front
    (x2 = 0.5) at 3.079 R\_p (P21: 57 steps, 2.237 R\_p; both fully molecular
    fixed points, the front leaving the domain). The 12-pass production loop
    ends at H2 row 5.49e-2 at cell 279, front 1.0755 R\_p (the 110-pass ladder
    of P16 at pass 12: 5.50e-2 at cell 279, 1.0765 R\_p); no pass triggered
    the halving. \texttt{atomic\_elem\_newton} at three passes: pass lines identical to
    the control build. \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} reload and the \texttt{mol\_carrier} case
    (12000 steps, one thread): data rows identical to the control build and
    to the golden.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Q2 (R4 in the operator), worker (Sonnet).}
  \texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90} (+108/-43),
  \texttt{src/tests/element\_operator/element\_operator\_tests.f90} (+88/-1).
  \texttt{element\_diffusion\_step} judges its candidate on every call: the entry
  composition is kept, the four tests (solved, finite, X inside {[}0,1{]} to
  \texttt{element\_fraction\_bound\_tol}, mass closure no worse than the entry\textquotesingle s by
  \texttt{element\_mass\_closure\_tol}) run whether or not \texttt{status} is present, and the
  entry composition is restored on a refusal. The outcome is always left in
  the new public \texttt{element\_step\_last\_status}, and \texttt{element\_step\_outcome\_text}
  names it. Two new rows repeat the ordinary and the NaN-residual steps with
  no \texttt{status} at the call site: RED on the entry text (the refused candidate
  with departure 0.86 adopted, the status left at accepted), GREEN after
  (\texttt{element\_operator} 28/0). A second, independent defect found by the change:
  the Newton solve of the helium mass fraction (\texttt{solve\_mass\_fraction}) read
  its arithmetic floor only on a DESCENDING pass, so a step whose residual had
  already reached the floor and whose next pass found no improving trial
  reported unsolved (residuals 1e-12 to 1.1e-5 at the refusal, MEASURED on
  the closed-column relaxations of \texttt{diffusion\_tests} T1a, T7c, T7d and T10,
  which went 35/0 to 27/8 under the unconditional judgment alone); the
  no-descent exit now reads the same floor, and \texttt{newton\_drop} moved from
  1e-6 to 2e-5 so that the \textasciitilde1e-5 floor of the K\_zz = 2e12 homopause column
  (T7), which the module header already documented, lies inside it
  (\texttt{diffusion\_tests} 35/0 again). Cost on \texttt{mol\_diffusion} at 300 steps: 54.4 s
  against 54.2 s, inside the noise. \texttt{mol\_diffusion} and \texttt{lower\_profile} at 300
  steps: data rows identical to the control build. \texttt{atomic\_elem\_newton}: pass
  1 identical; at pass 2 the judged rows are identical and omega differs
  (0.500 control, 0.250 Q2), attributable to the floor change turning a
  floor-level residual from unsolved to solved (READ from the Q2 report; the
  advisor\textquotesingle s own three-pass run of the merged tree, above, has omega 0.250 at
  pass 2 on both builds because the control binary of that run already
  carried Q2).
\item
  \textbf{Q3 (R5), worker (Sonnet).} \texttt{certification.f90} (+105/-17),
  \texttt{src/tests/certification/certification\_contexts.f90} (+106),
  \texttt{docs/certification\_tolerance\_anchoring\_20260910.md} (addendum).
  \texttt{mass\_row\_cell\_verdict} returns a third, optional outcome: RESOLVED
  (\texttt{cert\_mass\_round\_margin\ *\ floor\ \textless{}\ cert\_tol\_mass\_ceiling}, judged as P16
  anchored it) or UNRESOLVED (the margin times the floor reaches the ceiling,
  or the floor is not finite): an unresolved cell never reads \texttt{within}, its
  distance is \texttt{max(1,\ q/ceiling)}, so the review\textquotesingle s counterexample (q = 0.5 at
  floor 1) reads \texttt{within\ =\ F}, distance 1.0 (entry text: \texttt{within\ =\ T}, 0.5).
  The column loop is the pure \texttt{mass\_row\_column\_verdict}, which counts the
  unresolved cells and names the first (\texttt{n\_mass\_unresolved},
  \texttt{j\_first\_mass\_unresolved}), and the report names that cell even when a
  different cell binds the verdict. \texttt{certification} 84/0 (70/0 before the
  new rows), \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg} 334/0, \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} 195/0 (READ
  from the Q3 report; the advisor\textquotesingle s run of \texttt{certification} on the merged tree
  84/0). The addendum records that the margin of 10 was measured on three
  converged or converging fixtures only, none a near-zero-flow or
  molecular-EOS column: the UNRESOLVED outcome exists for the regime the
  fixtures do not reach.
\item
  \textbf{Q4 (R4 in the marching caller), advisor.} The marching loop of
  \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} reads \texttt{element\_step\_last\_status} after the element step:
  in phys mode a refused step refuses the whole attempted step
  (\texttt{as\_reject\_element} at the diffusion operation, the verdict text naming
  the test that refused it), which the controller restores and retakes at
  half dt like a carrier interval left uncovered; in init mode the march goes
  on from the composition the step restored and the refusals are counted
  (the final report prints "element steps refused inside the initialization
  march: n"). A test knob, \texttt{element\_refuse\_leading\_steps\_for\_test}
  (\texttt{EXHALE\_ELEMENT\_REFUSE\_STEPS}, zero in every production run), refuses the
  first n element steps so the path can be exercised: on \texttt{mol\_diffusion} in
  phys mode with two refusals injected, attempt 1 at dt 1.47451E-04 and
  attempt 2 at 7.37253E-05 are refused "at the element diffusion step: an
  element invariant broke" and attempt 3 runs at 3.68626E-05; in init mode
  the two refusals are counted and the run exits 0. (The phys-mode run of
  that fixture then fails at step 0 on the coupled source step\textquotesingle s fixed point
  with or without the knob, exit 2: a property of that fixed-step snapshot in
  phys mode, not of Q4.) Found beside it and fixed: the refusal census of
  \texttt{attempted\_step\_note\_step} stopped one reason short
  (\texttt{as\_reject\_source\_fixed\_point}), so a source-energy refusal was never
  counted by reason; the bound is now \texttt{as\_reject\_source\_energy}.
\item
  \textbf{The stagnation row of \texttt{grid\_and\_gates}.} On the joint progress measure
  the configuration \texttt{output\_state\_consistency.sh} used to force the
  stagnation ending (\texttt{EXHALE\_JFNK\_MAXIT=5}, \texttt{EXHALE\_CARRIER\_TRUST=1e-4}) runs
  out its budget instead: the starved hydrodynamic rows keep falling (energy
  4.9E-01 to 3.9E-02 over 8 passes, 1.1E-01 at 20) while the H2 row stands at
  7.3E-02. The row now uses \texttt{EXHALE\_JFNK\_MAXIT=40} with
  \texttt{EXHALE\_CARRIER\_TRUST=1e-6}: the hydro solves reach their roots, the H2
  row stands at 7.38E-02 for four passes and the ending is announced at pass
  4; the second assertion of that script (the ending hands back one state)
  measures 7.1e-16 against 1e-12. The script\textquotesingle s header and the suite README
  say why the configuration moved.
\end{itemize}
